# Spain: national and regional IRPF credits, 2026

**Sourced from the law.** This revision has not had an independent whole-pack review or accountant sign-off. Calculate regional credits after establishing tax residence and the taxpayer's facts. The Madrid, Cataluña, Andalucía, Aragón, Castilla-La Mancha, Asturias, Illes Balears, Cantabria, Canarias, Castilla y León, Extremadura, La Rioja, Murcia, Galicia and Comunitat Valenciana catalogues are drafted below, with specific unresolved interactions listed in Sources. The fifteen common-territory catalogues do not establish completion of the separate territorial or other personal-tax computations.

## Who this applies to

This Guide supplements [es-income-tax](es-income-tax.md) for ordinary resident IRPF. Use that Guide to compute general and savings bases, the separate state and regional scales and the personal/family minima before applying credits. A regional credit reduces tax, rather than the income base. The two general-tax liabilities are not automatically equal ([LIRPF arts. 63, 74 and 77](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a77)).

Choose the community under [LIRPF art. 72](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a72): first the greatest number of days in the period, counting temporary absences; failing that, the statutory principal-centre-of-interests test; failing that, the last declared residence. Apply the additional residence and anti-avoidance provisions where relevant. An address on 31 December alone does not establish the community, and a property in another community does not by itself make its owner's personal return subject to that community's credits.

The common-territory rules here do not calculate the Basque provincial or Navarre systems. Ceuta/Melilla require the separate territorial relief calculation under art. 68.4. An IRNR taxpayer or an incoming worker electing the special art. 93 regime does not inherit ordinary resident regional credits merely by renting a home in Madrid or Barcelona. See the territorial gaps in the [pack README](README.md).

## Rates, bands and thresholds

All amounts are EUR. `BI` means the specified sum of general and savings **taxable income before base reductions** (base imponible); `BL` means base liquidable after the relevant reductions. Use the definition in each row: an income limit based on BI cannot be replaced with gross salary, BL or take-home pay. The table year is 2026 unless a transition is stated.

### National credits: ordinary 2026 calculations

These credits have their own tax position. A state-only credit is not split with the community; a refundable differential credit is not capped at regional gross tax.

| Credit | Amount and core limit | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Qualifying subscription in a new/recent company | 50% of eligible subscription cost; annual base at most 100,000 and article 70 checked-wealth increase condition; state tax only, no automatic unused-credit carry | 2026 | [LIRPF arts. 68.1 and 67.1.a](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68), [art. 70](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a70) |
| Gifts within Ley 49/2002 | 80% of the first 250 of aggregate eligible base; 40% of the remainder, or 45% on the qualifying same-recipient repeated-gift excess | 2026 | [Ley 49/2002 art. 19](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2002-25039#a19) |
| Other qualifying foundation/public-interest association gifts | 10% of eligible amount; not every registered charity qualifies | 2026 | [LIRPF art. 68.3.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68) |
| Political-party qualifying membership/contributions | 20%, annual base at most 600 | 2026 | [Art. 68.3.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68) |
| Combined donation/contribution base limit | Art. 68.3 base at most 10% of total BL; no individual carry-forward imported from the corporate donation regime | 2026 | [Art. 69.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a69), [Ley 49/2002 art. 19.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2002-25039#a19) |
| Legacy ordinary habitual-home investment | Eligible annual base at most 9,040, single limit also on joint return; state 7.5% plus actual legacy regional percentage, default 7.5% | 2026, DT 18 eligibility | [LIRPF DT 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava), [preserved arts. 68.1/78](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68) |
| Legacy disability adaptation | Separate eligible annual base at most 12,080; state 10% plus actual legacy regional percentage, default 10% | 2026, DT 18 eligibility | [DT 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava), [preserved art. 68.1.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68) |
| Maternity | 100 per eligible month/child, ordinarily at most 1,200; qualifying later-registration 30-day month adds 150, raising that year's cap to 1,350 | 2026 | [LIRPF art. 81.1/3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a81), [RIRPF art. 60](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a60) |
| Qualifying childcare increment | `min(1,000 × eligible complete months/12, annual effective unsubsidised eligible cost)` per child; no advance payment | 2026 | [Art. 81.2/3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a81), [RIRPF art. 60.1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a60) |
| Disabled qualifying descendant/ascendant | Up to 1,200 per eligible person/year, prorated by eligible months and shared entitlement | 2026 | [LIRPF art. 81 bis.1.a/b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a81bis) |
| Large family or qualifying separated/unmarried two-child parent | Base up to 1,200/year; special large-family category adds 100%; qualifying excess children add up to 600 each/year | 2026 | [Art. 81 bis.1.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a81bis) |
| Disabled non-separated spouse | Up to 1,200/year; non-exempt spouse income no more than 8,000 and no competing descendant/ascendant disability credit | 2026 | [Art. 81 bis.1.d](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a81bis) |
| Qualifying reinvestment of sole-trade profit | 5% of eligible invested portion of positive general BL attributable to net activity income; 2.5% with art. 32.3 relief or qualifying Ceuta/Melilla art. 68.4 income | 2026 | [LIRPF art. 68.2.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68) |
| Qualifying historic-heritage expenditure | 15%; separate eligible-base ceiling 10% of total BL | 2026 | [LIRPF arts. 68.5/69.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a69) |
| Home heating/cooling demand improvement | 20%, annual base cap 5,000; at least 7% certified demand reduction | Qualifying works/payments through 2026 | [LIRPF DA 50.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-4) |
| Home non-renewable primary-energy improvement | 40%, annual base cap 7,500; at least 30% certified reduction or energy class A/B | Qualifying works/payments through 2026 | [DA 50.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-4) |
| Predominantly residential building energy rehabilitation | 60%; annual base cap 5,000, accumulated base cap 15,000; eligible excess base carried four following years | Qualifying works/payments through 2027 | [DA 50.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-4) |
| Eligible new electric vehicle | 15%; base capped at 20,000 after public aid; one qualifying purchase/elected statutory route | Qualifying 2026 acquisition/advance routes | [LIRPF DA 58.1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-11) |
| Eligible private EV charging installation | 15%; annual base capped at 4,000 after public aid; completion no later than 2026 | Qualifying payments/installation through 2026 | [DA 58.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-11) |
| Private renewable self-consumption installation | 10%; qualifying residential-building route 20%; annual base at most 5,000; same installation cannot use both rates or DA 50 | Installation and payments in 2026 | [LIRPF DA 62](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-16) |

### Madrid: family, care and education

The formal and income conditions following these tables are part of each calculation.

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Birth/adoption | 721.70 per child in the event year and each of the next two years; first-year multiple-birth/adoption addition 721.70 per child | 2026 | [DL 1/2010 art. 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a4) |
| International adoption | Additional 721.70 per adopted child in the event year; increase the amount by 50% when the child lives with both adoptive parents, then split equally for individual returns | 2026 | [Art. 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a5) |
| Foster children | 618.60 / 773.25 / 927.90 for the first / second / third and subsequent qualifying children; each must live with the taxpayer more than 183 days | 2026 | [Art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a6) |
| Unpaid fostering of older/disabled persons | 1,546.50 per qualifying person over 65 or with disability at least 33%; cohabitation more than 183 days, no related Madrid fostering aid | 2026 | [Art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a7) |
| Care of ascendants | 515.50 per ascendant over 65 or with disability qualifying for the art. 59 ascendant minimum; equal division between entitled taxpayers | 2026 | [Art. 7 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a7-2) |
| Schooling/languages/uniform | 15% of eligible schooling and language costs plus 5% of school-only clothing; per-child ceiling 412.40, raised to 927.90 if qualifying schooling costs, or 1,031 for first-cycle infant education | 2026 | [Art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a11) |
| Household-worker contributions for care | 25% of eligible household-worker Social Security paid, cap 463.95; large families 40%, cap 618.60 | 2026 | [Art. 11 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-2) |
| University-study loan interest | Eligible interest actually paid, not principal, for the taxpayer, spouse or qualifying descendant/maintenance-supported child; qualifying credit-institution loan and documented study purpose | 2026 | [Art. 12 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-4) |
| Working students under 30 | 50% of eligible tuition, cap 400; qualifying employment/self-employment registration at least 300 days, a full course enrolment and at least five months of study/work overlap; training contracts excluded | 2026 | [Art. 12 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-8) |
| Newly recognised large family | General category: 50% of regional gross tax, cap 6,186 individual / 12,372 joint. Special category: 100%, cap 12,372 individual / 24,744 joint. Recognition year and next two years | 2026 | [Art. 13 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-3) |
| Two or more qualifying descendants, low income | 10% of regional tax left after the other regional credits and the state credits allocated to that half; combined BI of all entitled parents and the qualifying descendants no more than 24,744 | 2026 | [Art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a14) |

Birth/adoption requires living with the child; split art. 4 equally if the child lives with both parents and they file individually. The international-adoption addition is compatible with art. 4. For foster-child ordering, count only children satisfying the more-than-183-day rule; a child adopted during the year is excluded from that fostering credit. Spouses/registered couples split qualifying fostering amounts on individual returns. Art. 7's exclusion for relatives through the fourth degree concerns the over-65 fostering case; do not substitute the unrelated-person credit for the ordinary ascendant credit ([arts. 4–7 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a6)).

For education, use children/descendants qualifying for the descendant minimum and living with the parent/ascendant. Cover only the education stages and expense categories in art. 11. In first-cycle infant education, only qualifying schooling not paid at public or administration-authorised private prices enters the base. Subtract grants covering those costs. Split the deduction equally between cohabiting entitled parents on individual returns. Books, meals and transport are not added to this base merely because they are school-related ([art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a11)).

For household-worker care, take contributions only for months with an eligible under-three child, qualifying dependent older relative, qualifying disabled relative or the taxpayer's own qualifying dependency/disability. The employer must register as a household employer; the worker(s) must provide at least 40 hours a month. The under-three route requires the employer and relevant other parent to have qualifying work/social coverage at least 183 days. In the older/disabled-relative routes, the employer needs the specified work registration or qualifying unemployment/pension benefits; this condition does not apply to the taxpayer's own dependency/disability route. Ascendants/descendants must qualify for their minimum; spouses and second-degree blood relatives must live with the taxpayer more than 183 days and have non-exempt annual income no more than 8,000. Retain the disability/dependency evidence ([art. 11 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-2)).

### Madrid: housing, work, donations and investment

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Tenant's habitual-home rent | 30% of rent paid, cap 1,237.20, taxpayer under 40; rent must exceed 20% of the taxpayer's BI | 2026 | [Art. 8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a8) |
| Landlord's eligible letting costs | 10% of conservation/repair, contract formalisation, damage/non-payment insurance and energy-certificate costs linked to housing rentals; annual cap 154.65 | 2026 | [Art. 8 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a8-2) |
| Bringing an empty home into rental | 1,000 per property in the contract year, proportionate to ownership; vacant at least one year; qualifying tenancy lasting at least three years | 2026 | [Art. 8 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a8-3) |
| Qualifying foundation/sports-club gifts | 15% of qualifying donations; donation base capped at 10% of total BL | 2026 | [Arts. 9 and 18.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a9) |
| Variable mortgage cost increase | 25% of positive excess of actual interest over interest under the same loan conditions using December 2022 Euribor; cap 300; home acquired before the tax year for a price excluding taxes/costs no more than 390,000 | 2026 | [Art. 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a10) |
| Move to a small municipality | 1,000 in the move year and next year; taxpayer under 35; population below 2,500 at 1 January of the preceding year; residence maintained through the move year and next three years | 2026 | [Art. 10 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-6) |
| Buy a home in such a municipality | 10% of qualifying acquisition cost divided into ten annual tenths, each capped at 1,546.50; under 35 and residence maintained through acquisition year and next three years | 2026 | [Art. 10 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-7) |
| Young homebuyer's mortgage interest | 25% of eligible interest, cap 1,031; include interest only up to the month before turning 30 | 2026 | [Art. 12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a12) |
| Family home bought following birth/adoption | 10% of qualifying acquisition price divided into ten annual tenths, each capped at 1,546.50; acquisition within three years of birth/adoption and actual occupation within 12 months | 2026 | [Art. 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a13) |
| New/recent company subscription | 40% of eligible investment, cap 9,279; qualifying university/research-linked companies and the art. 15.5 labour-company/cooperative route: 50%, cap 12,372 | 2026 | [Art. 15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a15) |
| First self-employment census registration | 1,031, under 35, activity mainly in Madrid; remain registered at least one year | 2026 | [Art. 16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a16) |
| Qualifying MAB growth-market subscription/offer | 20%, cap 10,310; hold two years, ownership no more than 10%, qualifying Madrid company | 2026 | [Art. 17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a17) |
| Qualifying investment by a new resident from abroad | 20% of eligible security acquisition cost, including acquisition taxes/costs but excluding interest; six-year holding requirement; unused deduction available in the investment year and next five years, except an eligible preceding-year Spanish-security investment starts in the Madrid residence-acquisition year and next five years | 2026 | [Art. 17 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-5) |

The vacant-home credit requires no occupation, rental, use or business allocation in the preceding vacancy year; the owner/usufructuary can hold at most five residential properties, excluding garages/storage rooms. The tenant cannot be a spouse or relative through the third degree. An early-ended contract preserves the required letting duration only under the statutory re-letting-within-six-months and total-three-year conditions. Arts. 8 bis and 8 ter are compatible ([art. 8 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a8-3)).

For gifts, obtain the foundation's Madrid registration, qualifying purposes and required deposited accounts, or the elementary/basic sports club's registration. Do not substitute a generic environmental donation for this statutory recipient test. For variable mortgages, obtain both actual and counterfactual interest schedules; art. 10 is incompatible with art. 12. For rural relief, use the actual INE population at the specified date. A rented move home cannot be let by the spouse or a relative through the third degree. Arts. 10 bis/10 ter are compatible; art. 10 ter also permits the stated art. 12 combination. Its acquisition cost includes taxes/expenses and excludes interest ([arts. 9–12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a10)).

For art. 13, the child must qualify for the descendant minimum and the home must become the family habitual residence. Selling during the ten-year deduction schedule ends the remaining annual instalments; failing the occupation or minimum continuous three-year use condition triggers recapture, subject to the statutory habitual-home exceptions ([art. 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a13)).

For art. 15, verify subscription on formation/capital increase; combined ownership with spouse and relatives through the third degree cannot exceed 40% on any day, and the holding period is three years. The company needs Madrid registered/tax offices and a genuine economic activity. Formation ordinarily requires a full-time general-regime employee from the first fiscal year. A capital increase requires incorporation within the preceding three years and at least one additional average full-time-equivalent employee in the two subsequent fiscal years, maintained another 24 months. The art. 15.5 labour-company/cooperative route waives the formation employee requirement specifically identified there. Art. 17 instead uses its own two-year, 10% holding and non-property-management-company conditions, maintained throughout. Arts. 15 and 17 cannot apply to the same investment ([arts. 15–17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a15)).

For the inbound-investor credit, verify no Spanish residence in the five years before becoming an ordinary Madrid IRPF taxpayer. Eligible assets are debt or equity securities, not a direct home purchase. Invest in the residence-acquisition year or next year; the preceding year is also available for the specified Spanish-entity securities. For unlisted equity, apply the no-tax-haven-company test, combined family ownership no more than 40% through the second degree and no executive, management or employment role in the investee. Hold for six years; disposal requires full reinvestment within one month under the statutory rules. A permitted preceding-year investment must remain held until residence is acquired before that reinvestment route can operate. Maintain Madrid IRPF residence through the last holding year. Apply this credit after the other regional credits. Ordinarily the claim window is the investment year and its five immediately following years, subject to insufficient quota. For the expressly permitted Spanish-entity securities acquired in the year before Madrid residence, article17bis.3 instead starts the window in the **Madrid residence-acquisition year**, plus its five immediately following years. For example, otherwise qualifying securities acquired in2025 before ordinary Madrid residence in2026 have a2026–2031 utilisation window, not2025–2030. Record that claim window separately from the six-year investment-holding and Madrid-residence conditions. It is incompatible with arts. 15/17 for the same investment. The statute includes taxpayers becoming Madrid IRPF residents from 1 January 2024 under its conditions ([art. 17 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-5)).

### Madrid: the income and evidence gates

| Affected credits | Required income test | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Foster children, older/disabled fostering and tenant rent (arts. 6, 7, 8) | Taxpayer BI no more than 26,414.22 individual / 37,322.20 joint | 2026 | [Art. 18.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a18) |
| Birth/adoption (art. 4) | Taxpayer BI no more than 30,930 individual / 37,322.20 joint | 2026 | [Art. 18.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a18) |
| Birth/adoption and tenant rent (arts. 4, 8) | Additional combined BI of all members of the potential family unit no more than 61,860, even if filing individually | 2026 | [Art. 18.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a18) |
| Arts. 10, 10 bis, 10 ter, 11, 11 bis, 13, 13 bis | Combined BI of the family unit no more than 30,930 multiplied by its member count | 2026 | [Art. 18.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a18) |

Do not apply these income gates to credits not listed in the relevant paragraph. Art. 14 has its own combined-parent/descendant test. Joint filing does not automatically double a credit or limit.

All Madrid credits require adequate evidence. Obtain the foster certificate for art. 6 and no-Madrid-aid certificate for art. 7. For arts. 8, 8 bis and 8 ter, retain the landlord's deposit receipt/copy from Agencia de Vivienda Social; for the tenant's art. 8 credit, the statutory alternative is a copy of the complaint about the landlord's failure to provide the receipt. Education requires proof of payment; study loans require their contractual purpose and evidence of use; working-student relief requires tuition, full-course and course-period documents ([art. 18.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a18)).

### Cataluña: regional credits

| Credit | Amount or calculation and key gates | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Birth/adoption/fostering | 150 per parent on individual returns; 300 on parents' joint return or the single-parent-family parent's return; adoption and fostering credits incompatible | 2026 | [DL 1/2024 art. 612-1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-3) |
| Widowhood | 150 in widowhood year and next two years; 300 while a qualifying dependent descendant entitles the taxpayer to the minimum | 2026 | [Art. 612-2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-4) |
| Ordinary habitual-home rent | 10%, cap 500 individual; large/single-parent families cap 1,000; qualifying joint returns cap 1,000. BI less personal/family minimum no more than 30,000 individual / 45,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 612-3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-5) |
| Habitual-home rehabilitation | 1.5% of eligible annual expenditure; base cap 9,040 | 2026 | [Art. 612-4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-6) |
| Master's/doctoral loan interest | Interest actually paid on qualifying loans through AGAUR | 2026 | [Art. 612-5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-7) |
| Catalan/Occitan language gifts | 15% of qualifying gifts, capped at 10% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 612-6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-8) |
| Scientific/research/innovation gifts | 30%, capped at 10% of regional gross tax; combined state and this regional gift relief cannot exceed 100% of the donation | 2026 | [Art. 612-7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-9) |
| Environment/natural heritage gifts | 15%, capped at 5% of regional gross tax; recipient must be in the statutory environmental-entity census | 2026 | [Art. 612-8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-10) |
| Angel investment | 40%, cap 12,000 per taxpayer, including on joint returns; qualifying university/research-linked company 50%, same cap | 2026 | [Art. 612-9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-11) |
| Filing required because of multiple employment payers | Positive excess of regional gross tax over state gross tax; unavailable to taxpayers using or eligible for RIRPF art. 89.A's special withholding procedure | 2026 | [Art. 612-10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-12) |
| Rent for a documented victim of gender violence | 20%, cap 1,000; 25%, cap 1,200 if disability at least 65% or a minor child dependent; BI less personal/family minimum no more than 30,000; at most three consecutive years | 2026 | [Art. 612-11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-134) |
| Agrarian/housing cooperative capital | 20% of eligible paid capital contributions, cap 3,000 per year; statute permits later use for insufficient tax, with a five-year holding/eligibility condition | 2026 | [Art. 612-12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-135) |
| Thalidomide public aid,1950–1985 cohort | Multiply qualifying aid actually included in general BL by the sum of the separate state/Catalan general average rates; older2023–2025 returns require rectification |2023 onward, including2026; July enactment |[Ley11/2026 art.37.1, new612-13](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=42) |
| Qualifying nonproductive/fire-prevention forest investment |15% eligible investment, cap5% regional gross quota; current approved forest-management instrument and investment certificate required |2026; general commencement14July |[Art.37.2, new612-14/DF8.3](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=42) |
| Diagnosed coeliac disease, enacted conditional route |250 per qualifying family person, shared equally between entitled taxpayers for that person; commencement depends on the31December2026 statutory condition |Conditional; not an unconditional operative2026 credit at27October |[Art.37.3, new612-15/DF8.2](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=42) |

For ordinary rent, qualify through age 35 or below at tax accrual, at least 183 days unemployed, disability at least 65%, or widowhood with age at least 65; the large/single-parent-family route instead uses its own condition. Do not require every tenant to satisfy all categories. Apply the art. 612-3.5 **per-home** cap and allocation where multiple taxpayers qualify: the statute divides the 10%-of-total-rent amount, capped at 1,000, between entitled declarants. Obtain the landlord NIF and supporting documents. Do not impose the old income limits or invent a percentage-of-income rent condition absent from the current article. The separate violence-victim credit requires the statutory proof of status; the labelled separate-grant worksheet immediately below supplies a same-rent calculation within its stated interpretation and claimant limits ([arts. 612-3 and 612-11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-5)).

**Same rent, two independently established grants: labelled statutory interpretation.** For a sole entitled individual tenant, calculate the ordinary612-3 credit under its own category, income and cap conditions, then calculate612-11 under its own proof, income,20%/25% and1,000/1,200cap conditions. Under this method add the separately capped amounts for the same eligible rent, limited by available regional tax. The basis is the two distinct grants:612-3.4’s once-only provision expressly prevents multiplying that credit because several of its own eligibility categories coincide; it does not name612-11. The original2025enactment introduced612-11 alongside the amended612-3 without a mutual-incompatibility clause. This is a labelled interpretation of that structure, not an express same-spend ruling or a general right to stack every housing relief. The current ATC victim section11 and both AEAT2025 pages describe the grants separately; ATC’s later express incompatibility with612-3 concerns **rural rent**, not the victim paragraph ([current612-3/612-11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-5); [DL5/2025art3.3/3.4](https://www.boe.es/diario_boe/txt.php?id=BOE-A-2025-10270); [ATC sections11/2026rural rent](https://atc.gencat.cat/es/tributs/irpf/deduccions-tram-irpf/)).

**Binding same-payment control.** A30-year-old woman, sole named tenant and sole entitled claimant, pays6,000habitual-home rent in2026, has general BI=BL28,000, savings0 and personal/family minimum5,550, with no other deductions. She is neither a large/single-parent-family member nor disabled and has no minor dependent. She holds a current protection order establishing the statutory victim status and is in the first of at most three consecutive claim years; retain that proof, the lease, payment evidence and landlord NIF. The income amount is22,450, within each30,000ceiling. Ordinary relief is `min(6,000×10%,500)=500`; victim relief is `min(6,000×20%,1,000)=1,000`. Under the labelled separate-grant reading use **1,500**, not just the larger1,000. Her regional general quota is `2,375+(28,000−22,000)×16%−5,550×9.5%=2,807.75`, leaving1,307.75. The victim cap does not erase the independently qualifying ordinary500cap. If ordinary eligibility fails, retain only the independently qualifying victim amount; if documented victim status fails, retain only an independently qualifying ordinary amount. A minor descendant must independently satisfy the published descendant-minimum condition for the victim25%route; it does not itself establish official single-parent-family status for the ordinary1,000cap ([own scale611-1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-2); [AEAT ordinary rent](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cataluna/alquiler-vivienda-habitual.html); [AEAT victim rent](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cataluna/alquiler-vivienda-habitual-victimas-violencia-machista.html)).

The statutory grants and operative conditions remain current for2026; the cited AEATmanual is explicitly for2025. No2026software outcome, binding same-rent compatibility ruling or court adjudication is claimed. An authoritative contrary interpretation or challenge to the separate-grant reading, multiple-claimant/joint sharing across these grants and ordering against other credits where regional tax is insufficient remain precise ordinary limits. Do not transfer this worksheet to rural-rent incompatibility or remove the existing documentary, per-home and temporal conditions.

For language gifts, verify the named institute or recipient's inclusion in the language-policy census. For research gifts, use the specified Catalan universities, affiliated institutes or Generalitat-promoted/participated research centres. Retain receipts and reconcile the recipient's reporting; arts. 612-6 to 612-8 require the recipient's donor list within the first 20 days of the following year ([arts. 612-6 to 612-8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-8)).

Angel investment requires eligible SA/SL or labour-company form, registered and tax offices in Cataluña, genuine economic activity, at least one full-time general-regime employee and annual turnover no more than 1 million. Combined ownership with spouse and relatives through the third degree cannot exceed 35%. Capital-increase companies must be formed within the preceding three years and not listed on the national or alternative market. The investor can be a board member but cannot exercise executive/management functions or be an employee. Formalise the subscription in a public deed identifying investors/amounts. Hold the investment, location, activity, employee and ownership conditions for the statutory three years; breach brings recapture plus interest ([art. 612-9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-11)).

The cooperative credit requires non-temporary membership, no more than 25% of total votes, a duly registered Catalan agrarian/housing cooperative, excluding tourist/short-stay housing, and the cooperative's certificate. Maintain the relevant conditions and contributions five years. The statute preserves relief for the specified reinvestment in another qualifying cooperative or statutory transfer. It does not state a numeric carry-forward expiry in art. 612-12; do not invent one ([art. 612-12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-135)).

First establish [LIRPF DT18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava) entitlement using the existing national legacy-home procedure below. For the ordinary acquisition/construction/rehabilitation/extension base, add eligible amounts actually borne and paid in the year: qualifying acquisition/construction costs and taxes, principal repaid on traced acquisition finance, interest less hedge receipts, and qualifying financing costs. Exclude unrelated extra borrowing and returned mortgage-floor amounts excluded by LIRPF DA45. Apply the prior-home/reinvestment restrictions and net-wealth test already stated in that procedure. The annual base `B` is capped at **9,040 in total, including on a joint return**, with no carry-forward of excess payments. A qualifying couple paying 6,000 each has a joint base of 9,040; on separate returns each claimant's documented 6,000 stays below their own ceiling. These are deduction bases, not tax credits ([preserved arts.68.1/70/78/84](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68); [current AEAT expenditure/base method](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c16-deducciones-generales-cuota/deduccion-inversion-vivienda-habitual-regimen-transitorio/modalidades-deduccion/adquisicion-construc-rehabilitacion-ampliacion-vivienda-habitual/cantidades-satisfechas-derecho-deduccion-base-deducible.html)).

[Cataluña art.613-1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-13) applies **7.5% regional**, separately from the preserved **7.5% state** amount. For acquisition, or construction payments, **before 30 July 2011**, use **9% regional** if the claimant is at most 32 at accrual, unemployed at least 183 days, disabled at least 65%, or a member of a family unit containing a child at accrual, and their **general BI + savings BI − personal/family minimum is at most 30,000**. Re-test the condition and income in the claim year. The 30,000 income gate is individual for each investing contributor even on a joint return; it is not a 60,000 household allowance. Rehabilitation/extension alone does not qualify for 9%. At `B=9,040`, ordinary regional relief is **678** and enhanced regional relief **813.60**; adding state relief gives **1,356** or **1,491.60**. A contributor failing the enhanced income test falls back to 7.5% if the underlying transition remains valid. Preserve the contributors' own expenditure/rate allocation within the single joint base, rather than granting 9% to the entire joint base because one contributor qualifies ([current ATC legacy-home explanation](https://atc.gencat.cat/es/tributs/irpf/deduccions-tram-irpf/), section13).

Keep disability adaptation in its **separate annual base capped at 12,080**, also one ceiling on a joint return. It carries **10% state + 15% Catalan regional**, giving maxima **1,208 + 1,812 = 3,020**, independently of the ordinary 9,040 base. Require the preserved certified necessity, qualifying disabled occupant/family relationship and ownership/tenancy/usufruct or qualifying common-element status. Pre-2013 payments and completion before 2017 are indispensable; a new adaptation in 2026 does not qualify merely because the house is old. The preserved regulation defines eligible accessibility, sensory-communication and common-element works ([preserved art.68.1.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68); [preserved RIRPF57](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820&p=20111220&tn=1#a57); DT18 and art.613-1 above).

**Mixed-rate joint cap: observed administrative worksheet.** The formula remains `regional = 0.075×B + 0.015×B9`. For the bounded case of two contributors to the same ordinary grandfathered acquisition/construction, both acquired or paid qualifying construction amounts before30July2011, first establish each contributor’s own eligible investment and current personal/income rate. Let `E9` be the independently evidenced eligible amount of the contributor passing the9%conditions and `E7.5` the other’s eligible amount. The observed official public2025OPEN calculation applies `B=min(E9+E7.5,9,040)`, `B9=min(E9,B)` and `B7.5=B−B9`; calculate regional `9%×B9+7.5%×B7.5` and state `7.5%×B`. Thus the observed allocation uses the enhanced contributor’s own eligible expenditure first within the one cap. It neither upgrades the other contributor’s rate nor permits allocating to9%more than that contributor’s eligible payment. This is a labelled administrative implementation corroborated by the controls below under unchanged current613-1 and DT18 rules, not an express statutory ordering/election or proof that the2026return software is identical ([current613-1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2024-6951#a6-13); [AEAT personal-rate conditions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cataluna/tramo-autonomico-deduccion-inversion-vivienda-habitual.html); [official public2025OPEN](https://www2.agenciatributaria.gob.es/wlpl/PARE-RW25/OPEN/index.zul?EJER=2025&TACCESO=COLAB)).

**Separating joint controls, observed28October2026.** For independently eligible payments6,000at9%and6,000at7.5%, the observed joint base is9,040, state678and regional `6,000×9%+3,040×7.5%=768`. A proportional split would give745.80 and incorrectly assigning9%to the whole base would give813.60. Reversing which spouse qualifies for9%still returned768, separating this result from first-declarant priority. In the unequal control the **spouse** has eligible3,000at9%and the first declarant9,000at7.5%; the observed joint amounts are base9,040, state678and regional `3,000×9%+6,040×7.5%=723`, rather than proportional711.90 or first-declarant-first678.60. The separate-return checks returned regional270on the spouse’s3,000and675on the first declarant’s9,000. For that reversed fixture both are45, have no children, the spouse alone has disability at least65%, her individual general BI47,000 less minimum17,550 is29,450, and the ordinary first declarant has BI28,000/minimum5,550. The declared acquisition is1January2010. Available regional tax exceeds each amount; observed joint gross regional tax10,545.25 less723leaves9,822.25. These are fictional2025public calculations, not taxpayer records or2026filing results.

Use this worksheet only after independently verifying DT18, previous claims, habitual use, ownership/payment attribution, finance tracing, prior-home/reinvestment restrictions and the net-wealth test. The calculator’s already-eligible expenditure fields do not establish those facts. The personal-category and income tests for9%remain individual even joint. The observations do not establish an allocation for rehabilitation/extension-only expenditure, the separate disability-adaptation base, different homes or changes of ownership, mixed historical entitlements, disputed payment attribution, other-credit quota competition or decisive operational rounding. Those cases and an authoritative contrary ordering/challenge to this bounded administrative worksheet remain precise ordinary limits; no free election or universal favourable priority is inferred.

### Cataluña: rural move, home investment and rent from2026

[Ley8/2025 arts.57–61 and DF10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-16833#a5-9) bring these IRPF provisions into effect on **1 January 2026**. Their scope includes legally classified rural municipalities, rural municipalities of special attention and the municipalities included in the regime by DA1.1. A PRE/415 entry labelled **DA1 ap1** is included under that provision and must not be rejected merely because its annex label is not MR or MRAE. Apply the actual legal designation rather than assigning the enhanced special-attention rate to every included municipality ([Ley8/2025 DA1.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-16833#da); [PRE/415 official annex](https://dogc.gencat.cat/es/document-del-dogc/index.html?documentId=1037694)). Record the municipality code, class and any partial-area designation using [published PRE/415/2026](https://dogc.gencat.cat/es/document-del-dogc/index.html?documentId=1037694), subsequent qualifying decisions and the actual location. DA2 grants partial coverage to the qualifying aggregated nuclei of **Artesa de Segre and Tremp**, not their municipal capitals. DA1(2), as amended by [Ley11/2026 art.126](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf), permits qualifying 2,000–3,000-population rural-county municipalities and qualifying county capitals to **apply** for inclusion; meeting demographic conditions alone does not substitute for the decision. Use the municipal sites linked by ATC to establish an aggregated nucleus ([Artesa](https://www.artesadesegre.cat/la-ciutat/nuclis-agregats); [Tremp](https://www.ajuntamentdetremp.cat/el-municipi/nuclis-agregats-1)).

**Published municipality-code controls.** The actual [PRE/415/2026 gazette annex](https://portaldogc.gencat.cat/utilsEADOP/PDF/9611/2136736.pdf) separates MR, MRAE, DA1 ap1 and DA2. Use the published code and designation with the credit conditions above; demographic estimates are not a substitute. The resolution corrects the preliminary-list classifications for Fontanals de Cerdanya, Fontcoberta and Ivars d’Urgell. These controls identify the published designation, not a taxpayer’s satisfaction of every credit condition:

| Municipality code | Published municipality | Designation and location control |
|---|---|---|
| 170694 | Fontanals de Cerdanya | MR; do not assign the special-attention rate merely from its mountain location |
| 170715 | Fontcoberta | MRAE |
| 251137 | Ivars d’Urgell | MRAE; distinguish Ivars de Noguera, code251121 |
| 170255 | Bordils | DA1 ap1: included regime; this label does not designate MRAE |
| 250349 /252347 | Artesa de Segre /Tremp | DA2: qualifying aggregated nuclei only, with the actual site evidence required above |

The actual [PRE/3375/2026 gazette, published29September2026](https://portaldogc.gencat.cat/utilsEADOP/PDF/9760/2172440.pdf), separately publishes DA1(2) inclusions under the Directorate’s28/31July2026 decisions. Its full annex contains **12** municipalities: Alguaire, Arbeca, Avià, Bagà, Bellvís, Falset, el Perelló, el Pont de Suert, Prats de Lluçanès, Santa Coloma de Queralt, Sort and **Torregrossa (252304)**. The annex publishes inclusion in the regime, without designating these entries MRAE. Retain the actual applicable inclusion decision and period for a timing-sensitive claim; the publication alone does not establish each municipality’s individual effective date. The [ATC property-transfer page](https://atc.gencat.cat/ca/tributs/itpajd/tpo/tarifes-tipus/) is a useful locator for both gazettes, but its property-transfer application dates are not an IRPF commencement rule. The IRPF commencement and each credit’s own conditions remain those set out above.

| Rural credit | Amount and principal conditions | Income/base and annual limit | 2026 primary provision |
|---|---|---|---|
| First move of habitual residence | In the move year: **750 rural / 1,000 special-attention**; **1,500 / 2,000** for a single-parent-family member. Age18–35 inclusive, or dependent child up to16 satisfying the prescribed local-school condition; no school condition for children up to3 | General+savings BI less personal/family minimum **≤30,000 individual / ≤45,000 joint**. First qualifying move only | [Art.57](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-16833#a5-9) |
| Acquisition of habitual home | **15% rural / 20% special-attention** of qualifying current-year payments. Requires a dependent child up to16 satisfying the local-school condition, waived up to3 | Income after the stated minimum **≤36,000 per investing contributor**, including on a joint return; annual base **6,000**, so nominal credit900/1,200 | [Art.58](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-16833#a5-10) |
| Qualifying rehabilitation of habitual home | **15% / 20%** of qualifying payments, including eligible acquisition payments from the licence/start year. House built **before1January1970**; prescribed structural reconstruction test; this article does not impose acquisition's child condition | Same individually tested36,000 income gate; annual base **6,000**; reduce the computed deduction by same-year public rehabilitation aid as art.59.5 directs | [Art.59](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-16833#a5-11) |
| Habitual-home rent | **15% / 20%**, cap **600 individual / 1,200 qualifying joint**. Age≤35 or family unit with child up to16 satisfying local schooling, waived up to3 | Income after the stated minimum **≤30,000 individual / ≤45,000 joint**. Shared-home amount is divided among entitled declarants under art.61.5 | [Art.61](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-16833#a6-3) |

**Home-investment computation:** use each claimant's actual eligible payments and [art.60](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-16833#a6-2): attributable acquisition/rehabilitation expenses and taxes borne by the purchaser, eligible finance principal/interest/other financing costs, with hedge receipts reducing interest. Apply at most6,000 annually to the respective deduction. No provision doubles that base on a joint return: apply LIRPF84.2's unchanged quantitative ceiling, while testing the36,000 income condition individually as the rural articles expressly require. The acquisition/licence-start year plus **14 following years** is an expenditure-entitlement window, not a credit carry-forward or permission to deduct the same payment in successive years. DT1's separate new-event window ends four years after the law's approval; preserve its wording rather than substituting an unsupported31December2029 cutoff. A qualifying2026 home-investment vintage can have its15 claim years in2026–2040, subject to annual qualifying payments/conditions.

Rehabilitation means principally reconstruction through structural/facade/roof or analogous consolidation, with total qualifying rehabilitation cost **strictly exceeding25%** of the acquisition price if acquired within the preceding two years, otherwise market value at commencement; exclude the land value from that comparator. The licence year establishes start. Art.59.5 and ATC both say reduce the **deduction** by qualifying public aid received in the period; do not silently change that into a reduction of the expense base. For example,6,000 qualifying rural cost gives900 before aid;500 same-year qualifying aid leaves400 under that literal instruction, subject to available regional tax. Record the gross cost, aid and resulting credit separately.

Habitual occupation is within **12months of acquisition**, or **24months from the rehabilitation licence**, followed by **three continuous years**. Preserve art.60.4–5's death, necessary-move/disability and employment-provided-home exceptions, rather than treating every voluntary early move as harmless. One storage room and up to two qualifying parking spaces follow art.60.6. Prior deducted home investments and exempt reinvestment gains postpone/reduce new-home eligible investment under60.7–8. Apply60.10's net-wealth increase test, excluding financing interest/cost and mere asset revaluation. Under the specified separation/divorce/partner-termination conditions, payments for the former family home can continue when it remains the common children's and custodial parent's habitual home. If later failure causes loss of an already claimed regional deduction, restore that regional amount with the prescribed interest under [current RIRPF59.1/2(c)](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a59).

**Compatibility and rent:** art.58.3 excludes rural acquisition where the claimant was applying the DT18 state home-investment transition at the rural provision's entry; do not give both routes automatically. Rural rehabilitation is incompatible with ordinary Catalan rehabilitation under612-4; rural rent is incompatible with612-3 ordinary rent. Identify the landlord NIF and allocate only supported tenant payments. For a qualifying single rural tenant paying3,000, the credit is450, or600 in special-attention status. For qualifying joint rent of8,000 it is1,200 at either rural rate because of the joint cap. With several entitled declarants for the same home, use the art.61.5 division of the applicable percentage amount subject to the1,200 home ceiling, then each applicable individual ceiling. This is not an extra1,200 for every occupant.

**Primary discrepancy retained:** enacted art.57.2(b) gives the ordinary special-attention move **1,000**, but the current ATC page gives **1,500**. Ley11/2026 art.126 changes only rural DA1(2), not57. The retrieved October2/27 corrections do not change37/126/DF8 or rural57. The draft applies the enacted1,000 and identifies the conflict. The move article has no express per-return multiplier for multiple individually eligible movers; this draft does not invent a duplicate household award. Simultaneous acquisition/rehabilitation claims for exactly the same cost are not modelled as two independent bases without express authority.

### Cataluña: July2026 additions and conditional commencement

Ley11/2026 art.37 adds three further routes; these are separate from Ley8/2025's rural-municipality credits. General commencement is14July2026 under DF8.3, subject to each provision's specific temporal rule. The October2/27 corrigenda do not change art.37 or DF8. The pre-July consolidated list of612-1–12 does not prove that the2026 catalogue ends there. [Official enactment](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf), [October2 correction](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGC-f-2026-90237), [October27 correction](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGC-f-2026-90249).

Under 612-13, identify the qualifying public aid for the 1950–1985 thalidomide cohort received and included in general BI. The aid under RD 574/2023 is **non-exempt employment income**, with no 30% irregular-income reduction; apply the ordinary employment-income computation before determining the amount actually included in general BL. Let `A` be that included aid amount and `B` the positive general BL. Calculate the **state and Catalan general average rates separately** under LIRPF arts. 63.2 and 74.2: each is `100 × its general quota after the applicable personal/family-minimum calculation / B`, expressed to the statutory two decimals. If those rates are `rE` and `rC`, the regional credit is `A × (rE + rC) / 100`, subject to the Guide's regional-quota settlement limits. Use the applicable year's rates and minima for an older return. For example, with already established general average rates of 12.50% state and 15.00% Catalan, and 10,000 of aid actually included in BL, the computed credit is 2,750. The formula uses the two general average rates; it does not use a marginal rate, savings rate or average calculated from combined general and savings quota ([612-13](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=42); [LIRPF art. 63.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a63), [art. 74.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a74); [AEAT INFORMA 147637 summary](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/irpf/novedades-impuesto/novedades-publicadas-informa-2024/novedades-publicadas-informa-mes-septiembre.html)). Its express temporal scope covers aid received and integrated from2023 onward; to use it for2023,2024 or2025 request rectification under the applicable procedure. Do not substitute gross aid for the included liquidable-base amount where base reductions alter it. The attributable-tax worksheet under The base supplies the separate general/savings, minimum-spillover and global-base-reduction calculation. Allocation of common employment expenses/reductions between wages and aid remains a precise numerator question; do not assume that gross aid survives unchanged into liquidable base. [Art.37.1,612-13](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=42).

For612-14, verify ownership of qualifying forest land and an approved, current forest-management instrument, eligible nonproductive/fire-prevention investment and sufficient documentary evidence. Compute `min(15% × eligible investment,5% × regional gross quota)`. A qualifying10,000 investment with regional gross quota20,000 gives1,000, not1,500. Request the investment certificate from Centro de la Propiedad Forestal within the first20days of the year for transmission to the Catalan tax authority. The short statutory clause does not establish a further carry period or an unrestricted credit for every forestry cost. Retain the certificate's actual expenditure period and the applicable issue/submission procedure. [Art.37.2,612-14](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=42).

For612-15, the special family definition includes the taxpayer, spouse and ascendants/descendants giving State-minimum entitlement. Each person must have a definitive coeliac diagnosis certificate from a practising doctor, with full identification, registration number/speciality and issue/diagnosis dates. The enacted nominal amount is250 for each qualifying person, divided equally where several taxpayers qualify for the same person; two entitled taxpayers would receive125 each for that person. **DF8.2 makes commencement conditional on the government not having approved the specified alternative food-cost aid system by31December2026.** At the27October read date, do not grant an unconditional250credit or assume the future condition is already satisfied. Establish that condition and its actual operative period before applying the sharing calculation. [Art.37.3/DF8.2](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=145).

### Andalucía: regional credits

`BI` in the income column is general plus savings base imponible; limits are individual / joint. A dash means the cited credit article does not impose a BI ceiling, not that evidence or other conditions disappear.

| Credit | Calculation and conditions | BI limit, EUR | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Protected home or young homebuyer | 6% of qualifying acquisition/rehabilitation expenditure, annual base cap 9,040; protected housing or buyer under 35 | 25,000 / 30,000 | 2026 | [Ley 5/2021 art. 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a9) |
| Habitual-home rent | 15% of eligible rent, cap 1,200 for under-35, over-65 or qualifying domestic-violence/terrorism-victim routes; disability route cap 1,500 | 25,000 / 30,000 | 2026 | [Art. 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-2) |
| Birth/adoption/fostering | 200 per child in event year, or 400 where resident in a municipality with depopulation problems; multiple events add 200 per child; divide equally between entitled taxpayers | — | 2026 | [Art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-3) |
| International adoption | 600 per child in the year of Civil Registry inscription; split equally between entitled taxpayers | 80,000 / 100,000 | 2026 | [Art. 12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-4) |
| Single-parent family | 100, plus 100 per cohabiting ascendant qualifying for the over-75 ascendant minimum | 80,000 / 100,000 | 2026 | [Art. 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-5) |
| Large family | 200 general / 400 special category; split among entitled taxpayers, including the specified orphan-sibling cases | 25,000 / 30,000 | 2026 | [Art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-6) |
| Language/computing education | 15% of eligible expenditure, cap 150 per qualifying descendant; each payer uses their own actual costs and ceiling | 80,000 / 100,000 | 2026 | [Art. 15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-7) |
| Taxpayer disability | 150 for disability meeting art. 3's at-least-33% or deemed-proof rules | 25,000 / 30,000 | 2026 | [Arts. 3 and 16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-8) |
| Disabled spouse/registered partner | 100; partner disability at least 65%, no individual return by that spouse/partner, and no art. 16 disability credit applied by them | 25,000 / 30,000 | 2026 | [Art. 17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-9) |
| Disabled ascendant/descendant assistance | 100 per person qualifying for the disability minimum, with its sharing/cohabitation rules | 80,000 / 100,000 for this art. 18.1 amount | 2026 | [Art. 18.1–18.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-10) |
| Additional household-worker disability assistance | 20% of the employer's qualifying household-worker contribution, cap 500 per taxpayer; person must need third-party help and qualify for the assistance-expense minimum | separate art.18.3 route;do not import art.18.1 BI ceiling | 2026 | [Art. 18.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-10) |
| Domestic help | 20% of qualifying employer contributions for one household employee, cap 500; qualifying working-parent household or householder/spouse/registered partner aged at least 75 | — | 2026 | [Art. 19](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-11) |
| New/recent company subscription | 20%, cap 4,000; qualifying university/research-linked company 50%, cap 12,000 | — | 2026 | [Art. 20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-2) |
| Employment judicial defence | Eligible actual costs of dismissal, contract-termination or wage-claim court proceedings, cap 200 | — | 2026 | [Art. 21](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-3) |
| Ecological gifts | 10% of eligible gifts, cap 150, with statutory recipient and donation-certificate tests | — | 2026 | [Art. 22](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-4) |
| Sport/exercise memberships | 15% of qualifying membership/affiliation fees, cap 100 per taxpayer, on amounts actually paid for taxpayer, spouse/registered partner or minimum-entitled ascendants/descendants | — | 2026 | [Art. 22 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-14) |
| Qualifying veterinary costs | 30%, cap 100 per taxpayer, for the statutory compulsory treatments/sterilisation, within the eligible acquisition/adoption/assistance-dog period | 80,000 / 100,000 | 2026 | [Art. 22 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-15) |
| Diagnosed coeliac disease | 100 per qualifying family member, divided equally where multiple taxpayers qualify for that person | — | 2026 | [Art. 22 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-16) |

Apply the regional definitions, not another community's thresholds. Art. 2 habitual residence normally requires continuous use for three years and occupation within 12 months, with its death/necessary-move exceptions and specified jointly acquired annexes/up to two garages. Art. 3 disability starts at 33%, with its recognised pension cases. Art. 4 single-parent status concerns the defined parent/cohabiting-child family, including the specified adult child assisted by a court-appointed curator. Art. 5 requires the large-family title/category at accrual. Arts. 6–7 require the prescribed victim status/evidence; art. 6 includes an effective registered protection order or final judgment within the previous ten years. Art. 8's depopulation threshold is **below 3,000 residents**, using its cross-referenced statutory population definition ([arts. 2–8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2)).

For the home credit, the age alternative on a joint return must be met by at least one spouse or the single parent. The qualifying base includes the article's acquisition costs, financing repayment/interest and eligible hedge costs net of hedge receipts. Apply the prior-home-deduction and reinvested-exempt-gain offsets before using the new-home base. Rehabilitation means the statutory qualifying subsidised work or structural reconstruction meeting the **more-than-25%** cost test, with land excluded and the specified two-year acquisition/market-value comparison; ordinary repairs do not become rehabilitation by relabelling them. The article preserves the specified separated/divorced parent's payments for the former family home occupied by the common children/other parent. For rent, identify the landlord NIF, use what each claimant actually paid and the applicable claimant's cap; on a joint return one spouse/single parent must satisfy the qualifying category ([arts. 9–10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a9)).

The birth/adoption/fostering credit's old income ceiling was removed with effect from 1 January 2025; do not reinstate it for 2026. Fostering requires at least **90 days** of cohabitation and no related Andalusian administration aid; pre-adoptive fostering followed by adoption in the same year does not also receive the fostering credit. Art. 11 is incompatible with international-adoption art. 12 and large-family art. 14, so compare the legally eligible alternatives before claiming. Language/computing tuition requires a minimum-entitled descendant; allocate school charges by the qualifying subject's proportion of total teaching hours. Qualifying extra-school centres or appropriately IAE-registered private tutors are included; general tuition/books are not automatically in this base ([arts. 11–15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-3)).

For domestic help, the household must be the habitual home and have the employer registered with TGSS in Andalucía. The working-parent route requires qualifying descendants and employment/business income; both spouses/registered partners must have such income when applicable. The alternative age route is at least 75. The statute permits the householder or spouse/registered partner to apply the credit. Do not combine art. 19 and art. 18.3 for the same employee. Art. 18.3 instead requires the registered household employer and documented third-party-help/assistance-minimum entitlement ([arts. 18–19](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-10)).

Investment art. 20 requires eligible SA, SL, labour-company or cooperative subscriptions on formation/capital increase; public deed naming investor/amount; no executive, management or employment role; combined ownership with spouse/relatives through the third degree no more than **40%** on any day; and a **three-year** holding period. The company needs registered/tax offices in Andalucía and genuine economic activity. Formation requires at least one full-time employee from the first fiscal year, with contract conditions maintained at least **24 months**. A capital increase must be within **three years** of incorporation and add at least one average full-time-equivalent employee in the next two fiscal years against the preceding 12-month baseline, maintained a further **24 months**. The enhanced university/research route keeps those conditions ([art. 20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-2)).

Ecological donations must go to the specified Andalusian/local public environmental bodies with earmarked programmes, or qualifying registered nonprofit/patronage entities with exclusively environmental purposes. Sport relief covers qualifying membership fees rather than every sports purchase. Veterinary relief covers only the article's compulsory vaccinations/deworming/treatments and compulsory sterilisation, with a legally authorised vet's invoice and lawful acquisition. Use the acquisition-linked one-year,adoption three-year or assistance-dog holding-period method below. The precise acquisition/anniversary-day endpoint remains reserved; the whole next calendar year is not automatically eligible. Coeliac relief requires the statutory official definitive medical report from an identified public-service professional or an insurer-affiliated private professional; the family group is taxpayer, spouse/registered partner and minimum-entitled ascendants/descendants. Retain all entitlement evidence under art. 60 ([arts. 22–22 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-4); [art. 60](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a6-2)).

### Andalucía: independent assistance route and dated veterinary payments

The80,000 individual/100,000 joint BI ceiling applies to the fixed100-per-person **art18.1** amount. Do not import it into **art18.3**. For18.3,require third-party-help and assistance-expense-minimum entitlement,plus the taxpayer's own household-employer registration with TGSS in Andalucía,then calculate `min(20%×eligible employer Social Security paid,500)` annually. This contribution amount belongs to the registered employer and is not automatically shared under18.1's minimum-sharing rule; do not use art19 for the same employee. If only18.1's income test fails,the independently qualifying18.3 amount remains available. At2,000contributions,it is400. For a full fixed-credit entitlement and individual BI80,000,the two amounts give100+400=500;at80,000.01,the fixed100fails but the400contribution amount survives. This follows the separately worded statute and the Junta's separately listed subroutes,not an assumed general income waiver ([art18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a1-10);[Junta2025benefits guide pp10–11](https://www.juntadeandalucia.es/sites/default/files/inline-files/2026/03/Medidas%20auton%C3%B3micas%20renta2025.pdf);[AEAT assistance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-andalucia/asistencia-personas-discapacidad.html)).

For veterinary relief,keep acquisition/adoption and actual payment dates separately. Ordinary acquisitions from1January2025use the **one-year acquisition-linked window**,not the whole following calendar year. Adoption extends it to **three years from adoption**;an assistance dog uses the actual holding period without the ordinary post2024acquisition restriction. Only payments from1January2025qualify. Apply the80,000/100,000BIgate and `min(30%×annual eligible paid cost,100)` per taxpayer,without multiplying the cap by animal count. Acquisition1July2025with a30June2026compulsory-treatment payment is inside;2July2026is outside. Adoption1July2025still covers otherwise eligible31December2026costs. The exact acquisition/anniversary-day endpoint remains unresolved where it changes the result; do not replace the statutory year with an invented365-day rule. Joint-account payments divide equally unless contrary payment evidence is proved;ganancial payments divide equally even if one spouse alone appears on the invoice/account. Thus600eligible ganancial cost gives90each where both otherwise qualify,not a single100household cap ([art22ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-17915#a2-15);[AEAT calculation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_1-andalucia/10_1_16-gastos-veterinarios-derivados-adquisicion-asistencia.html)).

[BindingConsulta14-2026](https://www.juntadeandalucia.es/sites/default/files/inline-files/2026/06/Consulta%2014-26%20web.pdf) accepts the leishmaniosis preventive vaccine and an inherent necessary preoperative blood analysis for compulsory sterilisation in its stated mandatory-treatment framework. It excludes the merely recommended kennel-cough vaccine. Where a municipality paid for the operation,only the taxpayer's own eligible preoperative payment enters that taxpayer's base. This is not permission to deduct every recommended vaccine or general veterinary expense.

### Aragón: ordinary credits and rural enhancements

Apply the ordinary credit first, then any qualifying rural enhancement. `BI` is general plus savings base imponible; `BL` is the corresponding sum of bases liquidables. Where specified, `adjusted BI` means **BI less only the taxpayer minimum and descendant minimum**, not every personal/family minimum. Income comparisons, ages and holding periods in these rows are statutory conditions, not general defaults.

| Credit | Ordinary calculation and entitlement | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Third/subsequent child birth or adoption | 500 in the event year; 600 if adjusted BI is at most 21,000 individual / 35,000 joint; cohabitation and equal sharing between entitled cohabiting taxpayers | 2026 | [DL 1/2005 art. 110-2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-2) |
| Birth/adoption of a disabled child | 200, disability at least 33% at accrual with competent-body recognition; equal sharing; compatible with art. 110-2 | 2026 | [Art. 110-3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-3) |
| International adoption | 600 per child in the judicial-constitution year; equal sharing; compatible with arts. 110-2, 110-3 and 110-16 | 2026 | [Art. 110-4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1104) |
| Dependent-person care | 150; cohabitation at least half the period; dependent is an ascendant over 75 or ascendant/descendant with disability at least 65%, income excluding exempt amounts at most 8,000; adjusted BI at most 21,000 individual / 35,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 110-5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1105) |
| Environmental/scientific gifts | 20% of qualifying unconditional monetary gifts, capped at 10% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 110-6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1106) |
| First protected new home, terrorism-victim route | 3% of qualifying acquisition expenditure, using the incorporated historical home/base rules below; priority is victim, then spouse/partner, then previously cohabiting children | 2026 | [Art. 110-7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1107) |
| MAB growth-market capital subscription | 20%, cap 10,000; ownership no more than 10%, holding at least two years, registered/tax offices in Aragón and no principal property-portfolio activity | 2026 | [Art. 110-8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1108) |
| New/recent company subscription | 20%, cap 4,000, **only on investment exceeding the national art. 68.1 maximum deduction base**; national eligibility and additional Aragón conditions apply | 2026 | [Art. 110-9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1109) |
| First habitual home in a rural locality | 5% of eligible acquisition/rehabilitation spending; under 36 at accrual, Aragón resident, qualifying first home; adjusted BI at most 21,000 individual / 35,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 110-10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11010) |
| Primary/compulsory-secondary books and school material | Eligible spending, subject to per-descendant income-band cap below, then subtract the relevant public grants | 2026 | [Art. 110-11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11011) |
| Habitual-home rent following qualifying dación en pago | 10% of rent, annual base cap 4,800; BI at most 15,000 individual / 25,000 joint and timely regional deposit of the tenancy bond | 2026 | [Arts. 110-12 and 121-10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11012) |
| Landlord's social-housing programme | 30% of the regional gross tax attributable to general BL from the qualifying reduced net rental income, rather than 30% of rent receipts | 2026 | [Art. 110-13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11013) |
| Taxpayer aged at least 70 | 75; BI at most 23,000 individual / 35,000 joint, with general-base income that is not exclusively capital income | 2026 | [Art. 110-14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11014) |
| First/second child in a small municipality | Event-year 100 / 150; raised to 200 / 300 if BI at most 23,000 individual / 35,000 joint; residence in municipalities below 10,000 inhabitants in the event year and preceding year | 2026 | [Art. 110-16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11016) |
| Under-three childcare | 15% of qualifying care spending, cap 250 per child, or 125 in the year the child turns three; BL strictly below 35,000 individual / 50,000 joint and savings BI at most 4,000 | 2026 | [Art. 110-17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11017) |
| Social-economy entity investment | 20%, cap 4,000; family ownership at most 40%, public deed, qualifying Aragón entity/full-time employee, five-year holding and entity-condition maintenance | 2026 | [Art. 110-19](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11019) |
| Extra-school support/refresher tuition | 25% of qualifying spending, subject to per-descendant income-band caps below, then subtract the relevant public aid | 2026 | [Art. 110-21](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-35) |
| Independent-living training for a disabled minor | 25% of qualifying spending for a minimum-entitled minor descendant with disability at least 65%, using the same income-band caps as support tuition, then subtract relevant public aid | 2026 | [Art. 110-22](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-38) |

For dependent care, share equally where several taxpayers qualify, with the closer-degree relative taking priority unless that relative's non-exempt annual income is at most 8,000, when the next degree can qualify. For first/second-child relief, share between entitled cohabiting taxpayers and do not also claim art. 110-3 for that same child. Childcare requires a minimum-entitled descendant and an authorised centre; share the credit equally when the child lives with several entitled taxpayers. The childcare and extreme-depopulation-residence tests use **BL** for their main threshold and separately restrict **savings BI**; do not apply a generic total-BI test ([arts. 110-5, 110-16 and 110-17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11017)).

The gift recipient must be Aragón or its dependent public body with the specified environmental/research purpose, or a qualifying registered nonprofit whose exclusive/principal aim is environmental protection or scientific/technical research and development. Investment arts. 110-8, 110-9 and 110-19 are incompatible for the **same investment**. The social-economy route requires the entity to qualify under Ley 5/2011, registered/tax offices in Aragón, at least one full-time general-regime employee and a public deed identifying investor/amount. Count spouse and relatives through the third degree for its 40% ownership test; maintain the contribution and prescribed entity conditions five years. Breach brings recapture plus interest ([arts. 110-6, 110-8, 110-9 and 110-19](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11019)).

The art. 110-9 new-company route incorporates current **LIRPF art. 68.1**: national maximum base **100,000**; eligible unlisted SA/SL/labour-company subscription, genuine economic means/activity, company/group own funds at acquisition-year start no more than **400,000**, formation or capital increase within **five years**, or **seven** for a qualifying emerging company; holding **more than three and less than twelve years**. The ordinary 40% combined spouse/second-degree-family ownership ceiling has the statutory qualifying startup-founder exception. Exclude continuation of the same activity under a different ownership vehicle and obtain the investee's certificate. Aragón additionally requires registered/tax offices there and prohibits the investor's executive, management or employment role, while allowing board membership. Account for the statutory reinvestment-exemption restriction and prevent the same subscription amount entering both state and regional bases. For one otherwise qualifying subscription amount `I`, with exempt-reinvestment disposal proceeds `P` (zero if that restriction does not apply), the two minimum-investment floors give regional base `max(0, I − max(100,000, P))`, then credit `min(4,000, 20% × regional base)`. Both floors constrain the same investment; they are not blindly added together. A small subscription does not receive the Aragón credit merely because it is a new company ([LIRPF art. 68.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68); [Aragón art. 110-9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1109)).

For arts. 110-7/110-10, use the incorporated pre-2013 home rules, including the **9,040 annual acquisition/rehabilitation base cap**, eligible acquisition/financing costs, prior-home/reinvestment adjustments and the end-versus-start net-wealth check, excluding interest/other financing costs from the investment amount tested and excluding mere valuation movements. Ordinary habitual residence requires three continuous years and actual occupation within twelve months, with the historical necessary-move/death exceptions. Qualifying rehabilitation is protected work or the prescribed structural reconstruction exceeding 25% of the relevant acquisition/market value excluding land; it is not every repair.
