Rural art. 110-10 applies to acquisitions/rehabilitations from 1 January 2012, with a home in a municipality below 3,000 inhabitants or a legally recognised local/population entity separate from the municipal capital. These incorporated definitions do not impose the national DT 18 pre-2013 acquisition gate on every new Aragón regional purchase ([Aragón arts. 110-7/110-10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11010); [historical LIRPF arts. 68.1/70](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68); [historical RIRPF arts. 54–55](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820&p=20111220&tn=1#a54)).

For post-dación rent, establish settlement of the **whole outstanding mortgage debt** by the habitual home and a lease with a purchase option over that same home under art. 121-10, alongside the bond and income conditions. Ordinary young-tenant rent does not automatically qualify for this special credit. The social-housing landlord route requires placing the property at the disposal of the regional government or authorised programme manager, then the actual reduced rental-income and attributable-tax calculation. Use the attributable-tax worksheet under The base for mixed general/savings income, minima and ordinary base reductions; preserve its precise signed-income allocation boundary ([arts. 110-12/110-13 and 121-10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a12110)).

The following caps apply **per descendant**, not to a pooled household education budget. Books/material use the primary/compulsory-secondary curriculum and school-approved equipment/material definition. Support tuition covers the prescribed infant, compulsory basic or basic-vocational subjects outside school hours, including properly IAE-registered private tutors. Both tuition/training and books require descendant-minimum entitlement, actual payment, evidence and public-aid reconciliation. Compute the capped deduction first, then subtract the public aid specified by the article, with no negative credit. The paying ascendant claims, with the statutory equal expenditure attribution for spouses under the Aragón marital-property consortium or an analogous regime ([arts. 110-11/110-21/110-22](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-35)).

| Filing/family category and BI, EUR | Ordinary books/material cap | Ordinary support tuition / independent-living training cap | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Joint, not large family, at most 12,000 | 100 | 200 | 2026 | [Arts. 110-11, 110-21, 110-22](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11011) |
| Joint, not large family, 12,000.01–20,000 | 50 | 100 | 2026 | [Same articles](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-35) |
| Joint, not large family, 20,000.01–25,000 | 37.50 | 80 | 2026 | [Same articles](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-38) |
| Individual, not large family, at most 6,500 | 50 | 100 | 2026 | [Same articles](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11011) |
| Individual, not large family, 6,500.01–10,000 | 37.50 | 80 | 2026 | [Same articles](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-35) |
| Individual, not large family, 10,000.01–12,500 | 25 | 50 | 2026 | [Same articles](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1-38) |
| Large family, joint BI at most 40,000 / individual at most 30,000 | 150 joint / 75 individual | 300 in either filing category | 2026 | [Arts. 110-11.5 and 110-21/110-22.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11011) |

The regional **DA 2 payment condition** requires credit/debit card, bank transfer, named cheque or payment into a financial-institution account for the expense-based credits to which it applies. An invoice alone does not establish the required payment method ([DA 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#da-4)).

#### Aragón differentiated rural regime

Arts. 160-1/160-2 apply the following enhancements, without replacing the ordinary credit's other conditions. Establish the actual settlement rank and **ISDT below 100**: eligible high-depopulation-risk settlements are ranks **VIII/IX**, and extreme-risk settlements rank **X**. Residence ordinarily must continue through the tax-accrual calendar year and the next **four years**; on a married joint return the general residence condition can be satisfied by either spouse. Property-based benefits also require the property in a qualifying settlement. The statutory acquisition/starting-business relocation alternative has its own four-year timing rule. Obtain the settlement classification evidence and maintain a future-condition/recapture schedule ([arts. 160-1/160-2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1601)).

| Affected credit | Differentiated regime adjustment | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Third/subsequent child, disabled-child event, international adoption | Increase the respective ordinary deduction by 20% | 2026 | [Art. 160-2.1–160-2.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1602) |
| Dependent-person care | Replace 150 with 300 | 2026 | [Art. 160-2.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1602) |
| Rural habitual-home acquisition/rehabilitation | Replace 5% with 7.5% | 2026 | [Art. 160-2.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1602) |
| Books/material cap | Increase each ordinary cap by 20%: joint 120 / 60 / 45; individual 60 / 45 / 30; large-family joint 180 / individual 90 | 2026 | [Art. 160-2.6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1602); [AEAT cap interpretation](https://www3.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-aragon/adquisicion-libros-texto-material-escolar.html) |
| Childcare caps | Increase 250 / 125 by 20%, giving 300 / 150; the 15% expense rate remains | 2026 | [Art. 160-2.7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1602) |
| Qualifying extreme-risk residence | 600 per qualifying taxpayer, including each entitled taxpayer on a joint return; rank X, BL strictly below 35,000 individual / 50,000 joint and savings BI at most 4,000 | 2026 | [Art. 160-2.8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1602) |

Do not use the below-3,000 home-location test or below-10,000 child-event test as a substitute for the rank/ISDT test. Do not add a general 20% uplift to support tuition or independent-living training: those articles are not among the enumerated enhancements. The art. 160-2.8 residence credit is specifically for rank X, not every rank VIII/IX claimant. Its joint-return per-person entitlement still requires that person's conditions ([arts. 160-1/160-2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a1602)).

Art. 110-15 health-insurance relief has no content from 2016; art. 110-18 flood aid is repealed. Art. 110-20 describes personal public-transport/bicycle-system passes up to **50**, but expressly conditions use on implementing rules entering into force. Their operative application for 2026 is not confirmed here, so no transport claim is calculated from the headline amount ([arts. 110-15, 110-18 and 110-20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11020)).

### Castilla-La Mancha: family, education, housing and rural credits

Separate the income measure and the order of calculation for each credit. Here `BI` means total general plus savings base imponible, `BL` their bases liquidables, and `education/rent income` means **BI less the descendant minimum only**. The common art. 13 income gate and incompatibilities below are part of these tables. Apply these tables together with [Ley1/2026 art.18/DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm): the official March2026 amendment changes the ordinary rental caps, adds12octies/12nonies and adds13.3(g). DF6 expressly applies the IRPF amendments to tax periods beginning1January2025. The linked older BOE consolidation has not incorporated those changes; it remains evidence for the unchanged clauses, not authority for the superseded450 caps.

| Credit | Amount and principal conditions | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Birth/adoption | 100 for one child, 500 for a two-child event, 900 for three or more in the event; descendant-minimum entitlement required; not an amount per child multiplied by the event count | 2026 | [Ley 8/2013 art. 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1) |
| Large family | General/special category 200 / 400; 300 / 900 if the specified spouse/minimum-entitled descendant has disability at least 65% and disability-minimum entitlement | 2026 | [Art. 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a2) |
| Single-parent family | 200; statutory sole economic dependency and family definition, not merely separate filing | 2026 | [Art. 2 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a2-2) |
| Education | Eligible textbooks plus 15% of the specified other educational costs, within the combined per-child income-band cap below, then subtract the relevant public aid | 2026 | [Art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |
| Under-three childcare | 30% of qualifying actual care costs **after subtracting public aid per child**, cap 500 per child; cap 250 in the year the child turns three | 2026 | [Art. 3 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3-5) |
| Taxpayer disability | 300, disability at least 65% and taxpayer disability-minimum entitlement | 2026 | [Art. 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a4) |
| Ascendant/descendant disability | 300 per qualifying person with disability at least 65% and corresponding disability-minimum entitlement | 2026 | [Art. 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a5) |
| Older taxpayer / ascendant care | 150 for taxpayer **over 75**; 150 per qualifying ascendant over 75; apply the specified residential-centre exclusion and disability incompatibilities | 2026 | [Art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a6) |
| Unpaid foster children | 500 first / 600 second and subsequent qualifying minors; cohabitation more than 183 days, BI at most 12,500 individual / 25,000 joint and regional certification/no associated regional aid | 2026 | [Art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a7) |
| Unpaid fostering of older/disabled persons | 600 per person over 65 or with disability at least 33%; cohabitation more than 183 days; BI at most 12,500 individual / 25,000 joint; required no-aid certification | 2026 | [Art. 8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a8) |
| Tenant under 36 | 15% of eligible rent, cap 500; qualifying small/remote municipality route 20%, cap 612; education/rent income at most 12,500 individual / 25,000 joint and landlord NIF | 2026 | [Art. 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a9) |
| Rent following qualifying dación en pago | 15%, cap 500; same education/rent income ceiling and landlord NIF; the habitual home must have discharged the entire outstanding secured debt | 2026 | [Art. 9 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a9-2) |
| Large-family / single-parent / disabled tenant | Separate qualifying routes each give 15%, cap 500; same education/rent income ceiling and landlord NIF; disability route requires at least 65% and disability-minimum entitlement | 2026 | [Arts. 9 ter/9 quater/9 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a9-3) |
| Development/poverty/disability/social-exclusion gifts | 15% of qualifying cash gifts; donation base no more than 10% of BL | 2026 | [Art. 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a10) |
| Scientific/development/innovation gifts | 15% of qualifying cash gifts, credit capped at 10% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a11) |
| Cultural property/conservation/patronage | 15% of the three qualifying bases; combined base capped at 10% of BL; incompatible with the cultural-patronage tax credit while that separate credit operates | 2026 | [Art. 11 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-2) |
| Under-40 first-home variable-loan interest | Actual eligible interest, cap 150 when BI at most 12,500 individual / 25,000 joint; otherwise cap 100 when BI at most 27,000 / 36,000; no credit above the latter ceilings | 2026 | [Art. 12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a12) |
| Rural home acquisition/rehabilitation | 15% of eligible expenditure; annual base cap 12,000 and overall base cap described below | 2026 | [Art. 12 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-5) |
| Employment-related rural relocation | 500 in move year and following year, limited to attributable regional tax on employment/business income; initial-year BL strictly below 22,946 individual / 31,485 joint; stay through move year and next three years | 2026 | [Art. 12 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-6) |
| Qualifying new-company subscription | 20%, cap 4,000; specified formation/capital-increase, ownership, holding, employee and public-deed conditions | 2026 | [Art. 12 sexies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-8) |
| Social-economy membership investment | 20%, cap 4,000; five-year holding/entity-condition requirement and public deed | 2026 | [Art. 12 septies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-9) |
| First-main-home savings account | 15% of eligible deposits; annual credit cap750 and total cap3,000; qualifying age/account/destination and BI conditions below | 2026 | [Ley1/2026 art.18/DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm) (new12octies) |
| Qualifying assistance-dog veterinary/vaccination costs | 30% of eligible actual spending, annual cap100 per taxpayer; recognised assistance unit, authorised invoice, no subsidy and BI gate below | 2026 | [Ley1/2026 art.18/DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm) (new12nonies) |

**Common family-income gate:** total BI must be at most **27,000 individual / 36,000 joint** for arts. **1, 2, 2 bis, 3 bis, 4, 5 and 6**. Do not extend this gate to every other credit or replace the narrower education/rent tests with it. Art. 13 prescribes application in the statutory article order. Use the minimum-entitlement/sharing rules it incorporates; for the listed shared birth, large-family, disabled-relative and older-ascendant credits, an ineligible co-claimant does not transfer their share to the eligible claimant ([art. 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a13)).

Compare eligible alternatives before claiming. Large-family art. 2 cannot be combined with large-family rent art. 9 ter; single-parent art. 2 bis with art. 9 quater; taxpayer disability art. 4 with disabled rent art. 9 quinquies. All five regional rent routes are mutually incompatible. Arts. 4/5 cannot both apply for the same disabled person; on a joint return, use art. 5 for the qualifying disabled descendant. For the same over-75 person, the age credit cannot also be taken with disability arts. 4/5; the statute directs the qualifying disability route instead. Under the new13.3(g), art.12 first-home variable-loan interest and art.12ter rural-home relief are also **mutually incompatible**; compare the eligible alternatives instead of adding them. If the same claimant otherwise qualifies for150 interest credit and1,500 rural-home credit, the larger eligible choice is1,500, not1,650. This is the incompatibility enacted by [Ley1/2026 art.18/DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm), and it applies even though interest is part of the otherwise eligible rural-home payment base. The national rent credit still has its separate state-half entitlement test ([art. 13.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a13)).

Single-parent status requires the defined separated/unmarried parent and cohabiting minor or adult child with the specified civil-law support measures, exclusive economic dependency evidenced by full descendant-minimum entitlement, and no child-maintenance annuities received. Apply the statutory exclusion for a final intentional-homicide conviction involving the former spouse/analogous partner. Older-person relief is excluded where the person stays more than **30 calendar days** in a regional/publicly contracted/subsidised older-person residential place, except properly certified temporary convalescence. For foster-child ordering, count only minors satisfying the more-than-183-day condition and exclude children adopted during the year; prescribed individual-return fostering amounts are split equally between spouses/partners. The older-person fostering exclusion for relatives through the fourth degree concerns the over-65 route; the competent department must certify the absence of related regional aid ([arts. 2 bis and 6–8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a7)).

#### Castilla-La Mancha education and rent calculations

The education base includes textbooks for the statutory education-system stages and 15% of: extra-school language tuition; qualifying external support/refresher teaching; initial internet signup and the **first month's line fee only**, excluding a provider change or a business line; and qualifying study/residence away from the family municipality where the required educational offer is unavailable locally, or regionally for the specified out-of-region study. It does not grant 15% of every computer or every monthly internet bill. Obtain payment evidence and descendant-minimum entitlement. Add the eligible textbook amount to the rate-based educational amounts, cap that combined credit per child, then subtract the article's public aid, never below zero ([art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3)).

| Education/rent income, EUR; education filing category | Combined education cap per child, EUR | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Joint, not large family, at most 12,000 | 200 | 2026 | [Art. 3.2.a](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |
| Joint, not large family, 12,000.01–20,000 | 100 | 2026 | [Same article](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |
| Joint, not large family, 20,000.01–25,000 | 75 | 2026 | [Same article](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |
| Individual, not large family, at most 6,500 | 100 | 2026 | [Art. 3.2.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |
| Individual, not large family, 6,500.01–10,000 | 75 | 2026 | [Same article](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |
| Individual, not large family, 10,000.01–12,500 | 50 | 2026 | [Same article](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |
| Large family, joint income at most 40,000 / individual at most 30,000 | 300 joint / 150 individual | 2026 | [Art. 3.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3) |

Childcare instead starts with eligible costs **net of the child's public aid**, then applies 30% and the applicable 500/250 ceiling. Include qualifying registration/enrolment, full-month attendance and meals in an authorised nursery/first-cycle infant centre; exclude costs treated as the specified exempt employment benefit. Split consistently with shared descendant-minimum entitlement ([art. 3 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a3-5)).

For the young-tenant enhanced rate, the municipality must have at most **2,500** inhabitants, or more than 2,500 and at most **10,000** while more than **30 km** from a municipality above **50,000** inhabitants, using the prescribed 1 January population. The four other regional rent routes in arts. 9 bis–9 quinquies have a **500** ceiling prorated by days the lease is in force and divided equally between entitled declarants for the same home. Under [Ley1/2026 art.18/DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm), the changed15% ordinary cap is500; the separately enacted20% young-tenant rural/remote cap remains612. Eligible full-year ordinary rent4,000 gives `min(4,000 ×15%, 500) =500`. Do not automatically double those caps on a joint return or stack two qualifying categories ([arts. 9–9 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a9)).

#### Castilla-La Mancha first-home savings and assistance dogs

For new **art.12octies**, the taxpayer must be under36, expressly including contributions made in the **year they turn36**. Eligible deposits must fund legal ownership/full title (which can be shared) of their **first main home in Castilla-La Mancha**, by acquisition or construction, **before six years have elapsed from account opening**. Apply the main-home definition in12ter.6(a), including actual occupation/continuity and its justified exceptions. This is a savings-stage deduction; an unrestricted bank deposit or investment account is not enough.

Keep one separately identified qualifying account per taxpayer, separate from other deposits. The balance at tax accrual must exceed the previous tax-accrual balance by at least the amount generating the deduction. Identify the account, opening date and year's balance increase on the return. Full restoration/transfer before tax accrual to a qualifying account with the same purpose and conditions preserves the specified withdrawal rule. Death before the destination deadline does not itself breach the destination requirement. Do not deduct the same eligible deposits again when buying the home. Total general-plus-savings **BI must not exceed27,000 individual/36,000 joint**; use BI, not BL or BI less descendant minima. The **joint annual credit cap is750, without automatic multiplication by the number of contributors**; allocate the allowed joint deduction in proportion to each qualifying taxpayer's contributions under12octies.3(b) and [LIRPF84.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a84). Keep the deductions previously attributed to each qualifying account, including a qualifying replacement, instead of resetting the total cap each year.

For an **individual return**, with all entitlement and balance-growth conditions satisfied, calculate `credit = min(15% ×eligible_deposits, 750, max(3000 −prior_deductions, 0))`, before available regional-tax limits. Eligible deposits6,000 with no previous deduction give750, not900. A previous750 deduction in2025 leaves2,250 of total capacity, so the same qualifying2026 deposits still give750. A qualifying36th-birthday year is included; the year they turn37 is not. On a joint return with two qualifying contributors, no prior use and otherwise sufficient credit/tax capacity, eligible deposits4,000 and2,000 produce one750 deduction, allocated500/250; two6,000 deposits produce one750, allocated375/375. The deduction starts in2025, so an ordinary two-contributor2026 joint return has at most1,500 of combined valid earlier2025 deductions, leaving enough total capacity for the750 annual cap even on the more restrictive aggregated-history reading. A claimed earlier2,800 deduction history would not be a feasible2026 fact pattern under this new relief. Breach causing loss of earlier deductions triggers regularisation under the incorporated State IRPF rules; retain actual destination and replacement-account evidence. Source: [Ley1/2026 art.18/DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm), new12octies.1–6.

For **art.12nonies**, use a legally qualifying assistance dog under the incorporated national/regional definitions, with the taxpayer-and-dog assistance unit formally recognised by the competent social-welfare authority in Castilla-La Mancha or another autonomous community. Eligible actual costs are the specified vaccinations, deworming, sterilisation and other treatments **mandatory under the applicable assistance-dog rules**. Do not grant30% of every pet or optional veterinary bill. Require an invoice from a legally authorised veterinary professional/centre, **no public or private subsidy of the claimed costs**, and the art.13.2 BI limits27,000 individual/36,000 joint. Apply `min(eligible_unsubsidised_costs ×30%, 100)` per taxpayer per year throughout qualifying ownership. An otherwise eligible400 invoice yields100, not120. A merely registered pet or an unsupported assertion of assistance status does not establish entitlement; the no-subsidy condition is not an automatic net-of-aid formula. Source: [Ley1/2026 art.18/DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm), new12nonies.1–5.

#### Castilla-La Mancha rural residence, home and relocation

| Approved rural zone | Municipality below 2,000 inhabitants | Municipality at least 2,000 and below 5,000 | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Intense depopulation | 20% of regional gross tax | 15% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 12 bis.1.a](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-4) |
| Extreme depopulation | 25% of regional gross tax | 20% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 12 bis.1.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-4) |
| At risk of depopulation | 15% of regional gross tax | 10% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 12 bis.1.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-4) |

For residence relief, use the approved zone classification and **effective stay**, rather than a mailing address alone. Ley 2/2021 art. 5 requires municipal-register certification matching the specified local health-area registration/certification and, where applicable, compulsory-school-age children's reference-school enrolment. It includes the specified mutual-insurance healthcare certification route. DA 6 extends the risk-zone treatment to the specified agriculture-dominant intermediate-zone municipalities below 2,000 inhabitants that lost population over the five years before 1 January 2021. Do not classify a town from population alone or assume that every rural municipality qualifies ([Ley 2/2021 arts. 5, 12–13 and DA 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-11513#a5)).

For arts. 12 bis–12 quater, regional **DA 2** uses each year's 1 January municipal population but preserves the conditions/amounts from the 2021 register where later population change would reduce or remove the deduction. Check both records; crossing a modern population threshold does not automatically remove the protected entitlement. The residence deduction uses the selected percentage of **regional gross tax**, with remaining available tax controlled under the ordinary liquid-tax floor. It is not a reduction of income ([DA 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#da-2)).

Rural home relief requires a home in a qualifying **Ley 2/2021 art. 12** zone, population below **5,000**, and acquisition/rehabilitation from **1 January 2021**. This is narrower than simply every municipality eligible for risk-zone residence relief. The eligible base includes acquisition costs, financing repayment/interest and permitted hedge costs net of hedge proceeds. For a single home before the separate prior-home/reinvestment adjustments, the overall ceiling is the lesser of **180,000** and the qualifying acquisition/rehabilitation amount **after** the relevant regional grants. The 180,000 ceiling belongs to the home and is allocated by ownership share; annual base cannot exceed **12,000**, including across the members of a joint return. Keep a cumulative-used-base schedule, apply prior-home deducted-investment and exempt-reinvestment restrictions, then calculate `15% × eligible annual base`. For that single-home case, compute remaining overall base after previously used amounts, then annual base `min(eligible current payments, 12,000, remaining overall base)`. The annual cap alone gives a derived maximum **1,800** before available-tax limits; it is not an unconditional annual credit ([art. 12 ter.1–12 ter.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-5); [AEAT clarification of the unchanged ceiling](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-mancha/adquisicion-rehabilitacion-vivienda-habitual-zonas-rurales.html)).

The regional home definition ordinarily requires **three years** of continuous use and actual occupation within **twelve months**, with the listed death/necessary-change exceptions; ownership can be shared. The article expressly includes qualifying construction/amplification, with a final construction certificate within **four years** from investment start. Structural rehabilitation requires costs exceeding **25%** of the relevant acquisition/market value excluding land, with the specified two-year acquisition comparison. Apply the end-versus-start wealth-increase test excluding financing costs and mere valuation movements. The specified separated/divorced parent's former-family-home payments have their own rule ([art. 12 ter.4–12 ter.6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-5)).

Relocation requires an actual work-related move to the prescribed art. 12 zone, initial-year BL below the table's threshold and continued residence through the move year and next three years. On a joint return, each qualifying mover has the statutory 500 amount but only within the regional tax attributable to that mover's employment/business income. The attributable-tax worksheet under The base supplies the mixed-income/minimum and base-reduction cap calculation, with its precise signed-income boundary. Breach of the applicable rural conditions triggers statutory recapture plus interest ([arts. 12 bis/12 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-6)).

The new-company route requires eligible SA/SL/labour-company/cooperative subscriptions, ownership with spouse/relatives through the third degree no more than **40%** on any day, at least **three years** holding and no executive/management role. The entity needs regional registered/tax offices and genuine economic activity. Formation requires, from the first fiscal year, one full-time employee or two part-time employees whose total hours reach one full-time job, with social registration and employment conditions maintained **24 months**. Capital increases must be within **three years** of incorporation, increase average staffing by the prescribed full-time equivalent over the preceding 12-month baseline in the next two fiscal years and maintain that increase a further **24 months**. Record the public deed identifying investor/amount and the recapture conditions. Do not import another region's different employee or investor-employment restriction into this article ([art. 12 sexies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-8)).

The social-economy route instead needs a qualifying Ley 5/2011 entity, regional registered/tax offices, at least one full-time general-regime employee, a public deed, family ownership through the third degree at most 40% and a **five-year** holding/entity-condition period. It is incompatible with the new-company route for the same investment. Its statute specifies the recapture treatment for breached conditions ([art. 12 septies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-9)).

For gifts, verify the precise recipient and certificate: art. 10 requires qualifying regionally registered nonprofit purposes and, for foundations, the deposited-account condition; art. 11 covers the stated regional public/research or registered nonprofit research/innovation entities. Art. 11 bis concerns inventoried regional cultural property, qualifying conservation/restoration and the specified cultural-patronage plan/recipients, with an aggregate base ceiling rather than a ceiling on tax. Detailed in-kind cultural valuations/patronage-credit interactions remain to confirm. Art. 12 quinquies' inflation relief is expressly limited to **2022** and is not claimed for 2026 ([arts. 10–12 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-7)).

### Asturias: family, care and education

Select the credit using the taxpayer's family, housing and residence facts. The income limits below use general plus savings BI, unless a different test is stated. `I/J` means individual/joint filing. A 35,000/45,000 limit is not a universal gate for every Asturias credit. The current wording includes Ley 5/2025: its art. 39.1–17 amendments took effect on publication on **31 December 2025**, while the electric-vehicle extension in art. 39.21 took effect on **1 January 2026** ([Ley 5/2025 art. 39 and final provision 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-2888)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Unpaid fostering of a person over 65 | 500 per person; cohabitation more than 183 days; BI at most 26,000 I / 37,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a3) |
| International adoption | 1,500 per adopted child in the event year | 2026 | [Art. 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a9) |
| Multiple birth or at least two adoptions on the same date | 1,000 per child in the event year | 2026 | [Art. 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a10) |
| Large family | 1,000 general category / 2,000 special category; the assimilated one/two-ascendant, two-child route has BI limits 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a11) |
| Single-parent family | 500; BI at most 45,000 and a separate 45,000 test on period income plus exempt maintenance annuities | 2026 | [Art. 12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a12) |
| Unpaid non-preadoptive fostering of minors | 500 per minor for at least 183 days; 250 for more than 90 but fewer than 183 days | 2026 | [Art. 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a13) |
| Nursery, zero to three | 15% of eligible expenditure, cap 500 per child; BI at most 26,000 I / 37,000 J. Demographic-risk/crisis route: 30%, cap 1,000, BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 14 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14bis); [AEAT interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/gastos-descendientes-centros-cero-tres-anos.html) |
| Second/subsequent child born or adopted in a demographic-risk/crisis municipality | 300 per qualifying child in the event year; BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 14 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-8) |
| Care of descendants | 600 per qualifying descendant; BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J; nursery-credit incompatibility for the same claimant/child | 2026 | [Art. 14 duodecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-16) |
| Descendant whose other parent died in a workplace accident | 1,000 per descendant entitled to the statutory descendant minimum, while that entitlement continues | 2026 | [Art. 14 septendecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-21) |
| Diagnosed coeliac disease | 100 per eligible diagnosed family member, requiring associated expenditure, the prescribed medical report and BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 14 novodecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-23) |

Art. 3 excludes relatives through the third degree and requires neither party to receive regional fostering aid; retain the competent certificate and split equally between entitled cohabiting taxpayers. Adoption credits require cohabitation and the statutory registration/resolution event date; split between entitled individual claimants. Large-family status is determined at accrual, with the competent title and cohabitation; divide between entitled individual filers. Do not apply the assimilated two-child route's income limit automatically to titled large families ([arts. 3 and 9–11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a11)).

For the single-parent credit, the child must satisfy the article's minority/disability, cohabitation or specialised-institution economic-dependence conditions and non-exempt annual income at most **8,000**. Civil-law guardianship/fostering is assimilated. Other cohabitants are excluded except minimum-entitled ascendants; a cohabiting descendant not generating the credit does not disqualify the parent if that descendant's non-exempt income is at most 8,000. Shared custody is excluded. A changed family situation must have existed for at least **183 days**. Apply both income tests in the table, rather than ignoring exempt child-maintenance annuities ([art. 12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a12)).

For nursery relief, require cohabitation and documented eligible costs. Compute the percentage and child cap first, then subtract the Asturias care aid specified in art. 14 bis.5; do not merely subtract aid from the expense base. Split the credit between entitled cohabiting claimants, including where only one meets the income test. In the year the child turns three, prorate by qualifying months, including the birthday month under AEAT's guidance. That guidance expressly applies the higher income limits to **both** demographic-risk and crisis municipalities despite the shorter wording in art. 14 bis.3. The nursery/care incompatibility is claimant-and-child specific, so it does not prevent different credits for different children ([art. 14 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14bis); [AEAT](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/gastos-descendientes-centros-cero-tres-anos.html)).

The 600 care credit covers descendants generating the art. 58 minimum; it also extends to the period before age **26** where only the age condition prevents that minimum and the remaining art. 58 conditions hold. Do not exclude an older disabled descendant who still generates the minimum merely because of the article's heading. Require cohabitation and equal sharing; prorate by qualifying months in the year of the 26th birthday. For coeliac relief, the defined family comprises the taxpayer, a spouse **on a joint return**, and minimum-entitled ascendants/descendants. Divide the amount for shared ascendants/descendants equally. The definitive medical report must identify the public-service clinician by CNP/equivalent, or the private-insurer clinician by professional registration number ([arts. 14 duodecies and 14 novodecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-23)).

For primary/compulsory-secondary textbooks and school materials, cap actual eligible spending per descendant as follows. The large-family alternative still requires BI at most **26,000 I / 37,000 J**. The paying ascendant claims; divide equally where more than one is entitled. Subtract the relevant public grants from the capped deduction per child, rather than applying the percentage to a net expense base ([art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter)).

| Filing | General plus savings BI | Maximum credit per descendant | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Joint | At most 12,000 | 100 | 2026 | [DL 2/2014 art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter) |
| Joint | 12,000.01–20,000 | 75 | 2026 | [DL 2/2014 art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter) |
| Joint | 20,000.01–37,000 | 50 | 2026 | [DL 2/2014 art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter) |
| Individual | At most 6,500 | 50 | 2026 | [DL 2/2014 art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter) |
| Individual | 6,500.01–10,000 | 37.50 | 2026 | [DL 2/2014 art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter) |
| Individual | 10,000.01–26,000 | 25 | 2026 | [DL 2/2014 art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter) |
| Titled large family | Within the applicable 26,000 I / 37,000 J limit | 75 I / 150 J | 2026 | [DL 2/2014 art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14ter) |

### Asturias: housing, living expenses and sustainability

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Disability-related habitual home | 3% of eligible acquisition/adaptation payments; maximum base 15,000; disability at least 65% | 2026 | [Art. 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a4) |
| Protected habitual home | Eligible acquisition/rehabilitation payments, credit cap 5,000 in the acquisition/rehabilitation year and 1,000 in subsequent years; insufficient-tax carry for three following periods | 2026 | [Art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a6); [AEAT interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/inversion-vivienda-habitual-que-tenga-protegida.html) |
| Habitual-home rent | 10%, cap 500; 30%, cap 1,500 for a demographic-risk/crisis municipality or a claimant aged at most 35, in a large/single-parent family, or a woman victim of gender-based violence; BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a7) |
| Specified habitual-home acquisition/rehabilitation | In demographic-risk/crisis municipalities: 5%, or 10% for age at most 35 / large or single-parent family. Elsewhere: 5% for age at most 35 if home value at most 250,000. Annual base cap 10,000; BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 14 decies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-14) |
| First emancipation, age at most 35 | Eligible evidenced costs up to 1,000, once in the first emancipation year; BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 14 terdecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-17) |
| Habitual-home landlord costs | Eligible repair/conservation, lease formalisation, damage/non-payment insurance and energy-certificate costs, cap 500 per taxpayer; socially sustainable rent condition | 2026 | [Art. 14 quindecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-19); [AEAT interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/gastos-derivados-arrendamiento-viviendas.html) |
| Living expenses, age at most 35 | Actual eligible expenses, cap 2,000 up to age 25; 1,500 ages 26–30; 1,000 ages 31–35; BI at most 28,000 | 2026 | [Art. 14 sexdecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-20); [AEAT age interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/gastos-vitales-que-incurran-contribuyentes-anos.html) |
| Sustainable forest-management certification | 30% of eligible certification expenditure net of grants, cap 1,000 per taxpayer | 2026 | [Art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a14) |
| Qualifying electric-vehicle purchase | 15% of payments net of public aid; cumulative maximum base 50,000 **per vehicle** across purchasers, one vehicle per taxpayer, no economic-activity use | 2026; statutory 2022–2026 window | [Art. 14 undecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-15) |

For disability housing, the beneficiary can be the taxpayer or a spouse/ascendant/descendant living with the taxpayer more than **183 days** whose annual income **including exempt income** is at most **35,000**. A competent certificate must establish the strict accessibility/sensory-communication need. Include eligible payments and mortgage principal repayments; divide the base cap between claimants for the same ascendant/descendant, with the nearest degree taking priority. Art. 5's former separate relative-housing credit is suppressed ([arts. 4–5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a5)).

Protected-home relief requires the statutory protected classification. It is limited by actual eligible payments, including qualifying financed repayments. AEAT identifies the acquisition contract/deed year and, for multi-year rehabilitation, the years in which rehabilitation occurs; the 1,000 subsequent-year limit concerns the later qualifying-payment years. Divide the credit cap equally between entitled claimants for the same property. Apply carried amounts **after current-year credits**, within the three following tax periods and the applicable **1,000 subsequent-year ceiling**. Do not refund an unused credit or restart its expiry merely because tax becomes available. A year combining new rehabilitation expenditure and old carried credit requires the interaction confirmation recorded in Sources ([art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a6); [AEAT](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/inversion-vivienda-habitual-que-tenga-protegida.html)).

For rent, divide the statutory cap equally between entitled claimants for the same home; do not duplicate a household cap. For art. 14 decies, include qualifying principal repayments, divide the annual **base** cap equally where the same property generates more than one claim, and maintain the fiscal address in the qualifying demographic municipality for at least **three years**. Its large-family route expressly requires the competent title; its single-parent route uses art. 12. Emancipation means leaving the ascendants' home for a new owned/rented habitual home in Asturias; returning to the family home before **three years** loses that credit ([arts. 7, 14 decies and 14 terdecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-17)).

The landlord cap is per taxpayer across properties, not 500 for every lease. Costs must relate to qualifying habitual-home letting, including evidenced preparatory costs. AEAT's guidance uses the state SERPAVI upper reference where no regional reference is established, comparing the monthly rent at 1 January for continuing contracts or at commencement for new contracts. Confirm the reference applicable to the claim year. The guidance gives conflicting instructions for owner charges passed to the tenant, including IBI/community charges; the expressly labelled included-charge worksheet below supplies a bounded administrative route while preserving the contrary exclusion and its exact effect ([art. 14 quindecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-19); [AEAT](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/gastos-derivados-arrendamiento-viviendas.html)).

**Landlord reference gate: included-owner-charge administrative worksheet.** Where the claim-year reference is established under the state fallback and the dwelling is within SERPAVI, let `R` be the documented monthly dwelling rent at the relevant measurement date and `C` the itemised monthly owner charges contractually passed to the tenant. Use `R+C <= U`, where `U` is the applicable verified upper monthly SERPAVI reference. The [official completion help10.3.22](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_3-principado-asturias/10_3_22-gastos-derivados-arrendamiento-viviendas.html) expressly includes passed-on owner IBI/community charges, agreeing with the first reference-test paragraph of the [practical manual](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/gastos-derivados-arrendamiento-viviendas.html). This selects that published administrative route; it is not a ruling that resolves the same manual's later contrary instruction to exclude charges/tributes contractually borne by the tenant, which names IBI and community charges too. In the crossing band `R <= U < R+C`, the two instructions give different eligibility outcomes. Preserve both calculations and the source conflict for any challenge or request for clarification; do not represent the selected worksheet as universally settled law.

Use actual charge obligations and the contract's recovery schedule, not every amount received from a tenant. A tenant's own separately contracted electricity or water is not automatically an owner charge passed on. Do not uplift rent by an estimated percentage or divide an annual bill arbitrarily: retain the lease and annexes, owner IBI assessment/community demand, actual agreed instalments and rent/charge receipts. Keep the claim-year reference report with the dwelling's address/cadastral identity, characteristics, upper value and applicable date, plus evidence that the state fallback rather than an applicable regional reference governs. Also retain habitual-home use, capital-income classification, eligible-cost invoices/payments and available regional quota. IBI/community reimbursements used in the reference comparison do not thereby become eligible repair, formalisation, insurance or energy-certificate costs.

**Separating fixed full-year example.** A sole ordinary landlord has an evidenced continuing habitual-home lease throughout2026, unchanged monthly sums, and a verified applicable upper reference `U=700`. The rent is `R=610`; the contract recovers owner IBI in12actual monthly instalments of30 (assessment360) and community charges in12monthly instalments of60 (demand720), so `C=90`. Tenant-contracted utilities80are separate. The selected comparison is **610+30+60=700**, meeting the reference at equality; it is neither610alone nor780including tenant-own utilities. Assume all other conditions pass, this is the claimant's only eligible letting-cost credit, eligible paid repairs400+insurance160+energy certificate80=**640**, and sufficient regional quota. The credit is **min(640,500)=500**. The landlord's actual cost ledger, rather than rent or passed-on charges, supplies that credit base.

Change only contractual dwelling rent to620: **620+90=710>700**, so this lease fails the selected included-charge gate and no credit is entered under that worksheet. The contrary exclusion instruction would compare620to700and admit the500cost credit on the same remaining facts. That difference is retained as the precise unresolved administrative conflict, not silently converted into a categorical zero legal entitlement. These derived examples assume an actually established reference and constant sums for all occupied months; they do not invent a reference for an uncovered dwelling, alter the existing measurement dates or settle mid-year change/partial-period attribution.

Living-expense categories are housing and associated utilities, education, transport/mobility, technology, sport and culture. Use evidenced actual costs. The statute overlaps age **30** between its final two brackets; AEAT expressly assigns age 30 to the **1,500** bracket and starts the 1,000 bracket at **31**. Age is measured when the event giving entitlement to the expense credit occurs; the guidance applies the higher ceiling if the claimant crosses brackets during the year. On a joint return test each qualifying member, rather than imposing one joint-return age cap ([art. 14 sexdecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-20); [AEAT](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/gastos-vitales-que-incurran-contribuyentes-anos.html)).

Forest relief requires an owned forest in Asturias and a competent sustainable-management certificate; claim in the certification year and share equally between entitled individual co-owners. For electric vehicles, retain the sales contract, buyer allocation, qualifying technical category and payment/grant schedule. Art. 14 undecies incorporates the eligible categories in [RD 266/2021 annex I, Programme 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2021-5869); it does not make every MOVES subsidy condition a separate tax-credit condition. The 50,000 base is cumulative for the vehicle, including shared or multi-year purchases. AEAT's 2025 manual predates the statutory extension through **2026**; the current statute controls that window ([arts. 14 and 14 undecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-15); [AEAT vehicle guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/adquisicion-vehiculos-electricos.html)).

### Asturias: demographic zones, employment, investment and aid

Demographic-risk/crisis relief requires the property's or taxpayer's actual qualifying municipality under **Decreto 83/2025** and the applicable published updates. Art. 4 distinguishes risk (ISDA 0–8), crisis (8.01–20), unstable (20.01–30) and dynamic (30.01–100); only the first two satisfy the credits above that expressly name them. Annex II supplies the classifications, and final provision 1 requires review/update at least annually. Do not treat the original 2025 town list as a permanent classification for every claim year ([Decreto 83/2025 arts. 4–5, annex II and final provision 1](https://miprincipado.asturias.es/ast/bopa/disposiciones?_pa_sede_bopa_web_portlet_SedeBopaDispositionWeb_mvcRenderCommandName=%2Fdisposition%2Fdetail&p_p_id=pa_sede_bopa_web_portlet_SedeBopaDispositionWeb&p_p_lifecycle=0&p_r_p_dispositionDate=27%2F06%2F2025&p_r_p_dispositionReference=2025-05426&p_r_p_dispositionText=2025-05426)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| New self-employment in a demographic-risk/crisis municipality | 1,000 per eligible claimant; BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 14 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-9) |
| Public transport in a demographic-risk/crisis municipality | Actual personal named-pass costs, cap 100; additional 10% of qualifying study-descendant pass costs, cap 300 per descendant; BI at most 35,000 I / 45,000 J | 2026 | [Art. 14 sexies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-10) |
| Training for especially qualified work | Actual eligible training expenditure, cap 2,000, once on labour-market entry | 2026 | [Art. 14 septies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-11) |
| Work-related move to Asturias | 15% of eligible relocation costs, cap 1,000, or 2,000 for the statutory especially qualified work; move year and next three years | 2026 | [Art. 14 octies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-12) |
| New/recent-company equity investment | 30% of qualifying formation/capital-increase investment, credit cap 6,000; three-year holding period | 2026 | [Art. 14 octodecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-22) |
| Regional ALS grant | Apply the separate general rates to the liquidable-base grant amount using the attributable-tax worksheet; retain its precise numerator boundaries | 2026 | [Art. 14 quaterdecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-18) |

New self-employment requires activity in Asturias and residence in the qualifying municipality. Exclude collaborating family self-employed persons and partners in capital companies. Claim on initial Social Security/mutuality registration, retain registration at least **one year** except death, and exclude a restart of the same activity ceased within the previous **six months**. A joint return can claim 1,000 per eligible person. Transport relief requires named personal public-transport passes; the additional descendant must generate the statutory minimum and study bachillerato, vocational training or university outside the relevant municipality. Apply nearest-degree priority/equal sharing for the descendant; the joint personal-pass ceiling increases by qualifying persons but never exceeds actual costs. Claim in the payment year, regardless of the pass's validity period ([arts. 14 quinquies–14 sexies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-10)).

Training relief requires entry to qualifying work no later than **three years** after academic completion, habitual residence maintained in Asturias at least **three years**, and an employment contract or self-employed registration. The latter registration must continue at least **one year**, except death. Especially qualified work requires contribution Group 1 and direct/main connection to the statutory activities: basic/applied research, experimental development, advanced innovative software with a favourable regional report, technological innovation/diagnostics, the listed environmental/resource projects, EU-defined innovative companies, justified productive business-promotion projects, or qualifying early-stage/high-growth micro/SME projects. A technical job title alone is insufficient. Claim once in the labour-market-entry year for eligible training costs and do not combine with the work-move credit ([art. 14 septies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-11)).

The move credit requires no habitual Asturias residence in the preceding **four years**, residence in the move year and at least **three additional years**, and an employment contract or self-employed registration. Costs comprise necessary family travel/moving, descendants' Asturias schooling and the habitual-home purchase/rent and connected service/supply contracting costs. The enhanced qualified-work cap uses art. 14 septies.3's definition and is incompatible with that training credit ([art. 14 octies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-12)).

Investment relief covers SA, SL and cooperatives, including labour companies, with registered/tax offices in Asturias, micro/SME status and genuine economic activity rather than principal asset management. Combined ownership/votes with spouse and relatives through the third degree must never exceed **40%** on any day of the year. Formation requires at least one full-time general-regime employee from the first fiscal year. A capital increase must occur within **three years** of incorporation, increase average employment by at least one full-time equivalent over the prior 12 months during the two following fiscal years, and maintain that increase for another **24 months**. Keep the three-year holding and employment schedules separately ([art. 14 octodecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-22)).

Do not apply the displayed COVID-aid credit in art. 14 nonies as an operative 2026 credit: the [operative ruling in STC 21/2022](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2022-3808) declares it unconstitutional and void. The judgment's FJ 4 protection concerns obligations accrued from 31 December 2020 to 9 November 2021, not a new 2026 claim. Art. 8's former rural-land gift credit is suppressed. The ALS route requires a qualifying regional grant included in general BI, but the article does not supply a single flat credit rate; its separate general-rate and liquidable-base calculation is supplied in the attributable-tax worksheet under The base, with the stated signed-income boundary ([arts. 8, 14 nonies and 14 quaterdecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-13)).


### Illes Balears: income gates, housing and island conditions

Test each credit's own income limit, family status and island conditions. `BI` is general plus savings base imponible; `I/J` means individual/joint filing. The table uses the current wording for an ordinary **31 December 2026** accrual. [DL 1/2026 final provision 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-9525) expressly renews mortgage-interest relief for 2026 and substitutes the Middle East-war aid route. [Ley 4/2026 final provision 67](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-15579) amended rental/landlord rules and increased birth/adoption amounts; its general entry date is **14 June 2026**, confirmed by [ATIB's notice](https://atib.es/General/Novedad.aspx?idTexto=16987&idTipoTexto=1&lang=es). Do not apply this current wording automatically to a short period ending before that date; the transition remains to confirm.

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Habitual-home sustainability investment | 50% of actual eligible investment; annual base cap 10,000; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3) |
| Habitual-home rent | 15%, cap 530 for age under 36, or over 65 without work/professional activity; enhanced statutory groups 20%, cap 650; BI gate normally 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026, current wording | [Art. 3 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3bis) |
| Variable-rate home mortgage interest increase | Positive difference between 2026 and 2025 interest paid, credit cap 400 per taxpayer; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 expressly | [Art. 3 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-14) |
| Landlord non-payment insurance | 75% of eligible premiums, annual credit cap 440 | 2026, current conditions | [Art. 4 quater.1/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-13) |
| Landlord bringing a previously unlet home into permanent letting | 50% of specified current-year costs, once; credit cap 1,500, raised to 1,800 for the qualifying public-sector subletting programme | 2026, current conditions | [Art. 4 quater.2/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-13) |
| Landlord renewal or replacement letting without an excessive rent increase | Once, 50% of net rental yield; annual cap 800 per home, time-apportioned where applicable, and 2,000 per taxpayer | Current art. 4 quater.3 introduced June 2026 | [Art. 4 quater.3/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-13) |
| Temporary work-related move between islands | 15% of actual rent, annual credit cap 440 per qualifying taxpayer; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 4 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-14) |
| Illegally occupied home or qualifying suspended eviction | 40% of eligible owner-paid expenses, credit cap 500; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 4 sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-16) |

Sustainability relief covers the stated renewable-energy and water-saving/reuse/sanitation investments in a Balearic habitual home of the taxpayer or an LAU tenant. **At least one energy-rating level of improvement** and registered before/after certificates are required; do not grant it for an invoice alone or waive that condition for water works. A landlord must have at least a one-year contract with the same tenant, the IBAVI deposit, declared property-rental income and no passing of the investment cost to the tenant ([art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3)).

The enhanced rent groups are age **under 30**, disability at least **33%**, entitlement to an ascendant/descendant disability minimum, the qualifying cohabiting large/single-parent family, or qualifying self-employed registration at least **183 days** in the year. AEAT treats these as their own eligibility alternatives; do not impose the ordinary under-36/over-65 condition again on a disabled or self-employed claimant. Require actual occupation and a contract of at least **one year**. For at least half the period, neither the claimant nor a family-unit member can hold any full-ownership/use/enjoyment share in another home less than **70 km** away, except another island, outside the Balearics or a home yielding property-rental income that year. There must be no entitlement to a habitual-home investment deduction for the same period. Only the named tenant's eligible payments count; joint filing does not make a non-tenant spouse a tenant ([art. 3 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3bis); [AEAT rent interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-illes-balears/arrendamiento-vivienda-habitual.html)).

For rent and textbooks, qualifying general large/single-parent families raise the ordinary **33,000 I / 52,800 J** income gate by **20%**, to **39,600 I / 63,360 J**; special-category large families raise it by **30%**, to **42,900 I / 68,640 J**. The particular family references matter: [Ley 8/2018 art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-13193#ar-6) includes the stated one-disabled-child single-parent family, while [art. 7.7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-13193#ar-7) concerns single-parent families with at least two children. Obtain the recognised category and the article's cohabitation/parental-authority facts rather than treating every unmarried parent as eligible ([arts. 3 bis and 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4)).

Mortgage-interest relief concerns a variable-rate loan/credit financing the habitual home, requires the bank's comparison certificate and is incompatible with the national DT 18 home-investment credit. A fall in interest does not create a negative credit. The landlord routes require a whole-home letting in **one contract**, at least a one-year initial term, the IBAVI deposit, declared property income and BI at most **52,800 I / 84,480 J** under current art. 4 quater.4. Insurance relief is compatible with either other landlord route, but those two routes are mutually incompatible. For the unused-home route, the home must not have been let in either of the **two preceding fiscal years** and rent per tenant must not exceed **15 per useful square metre per month**. Its eligible costs are damage insurance, conservation/repair, applicable community/tax costs, lease formalisation and energy certificates, paid that year. For renewal/replacement relief, the new lease must begin before **six months** from the prior lease ending; rent must stay unchanged or rise no more than the statutory annual-update index. Use the bounded net-yield/time method below where its facts apply; early-accrual and mixed-contract attribution remain specifically unconfirmed ([arts. 3 quater and 4 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-13)).

**Renewal/replacement net-yield method, labelled statutory inference.** For a sole owner with a full 2026 calendar-year tax period, a qualifying new lease beginning after 14 June 2026 and no 2026 rent from an earlier contract, compute that home's rental income under [LIRPF 22](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a22), less its supported deductible expenses and depreciation under [23.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a23). This is net yield **before** the separate housing-rental percentage reduction in 23.2, rather than gross rent or reduced net yield. With positive eligible net yield `N_h`, calculate `min(50%×N_h,800)` for each qualifying home, then cap their sum at 2,000 per taxpayer, within available regional gross tax. Retain actual income, expense and ownership records; the example does not create an expense-allocation shortcut.

The 800 wording refers to the **tax period**. [LIRPF 12–13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a12) make that period the calendar year, shortened on death. On this labelled reading, a midyear lease does not itself shorten a surviving taxpayer's tax period: use the 800 ceiling in this full-year branch, rather than silently multiplying it by rental days. [Ley 4/2026 DF 67.3/DF 72](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-15579) supplies the amended wording and 14 June 2026 commencement. Neither provision expressly rules on this numerical time-allocation example; a contrary administrative lease-day interpretation has not been confirmed. Short tax periods and transactions predating the amendment need their own date analysis.

**Separating example.** The sole owner's previous lease ended 31 December 2025. A replacement whole-home lease runs from 15 June 2026 for an initial year, before six months have elapsed; the monthly rent remains 800, and the deposit, income, documentary and other 4 quaterconditions pass. It charges 400 for 15–30 June and 4,800 forJuly–December:5,200 total 2026 rent. Verified expenses/depreciation attributable to that qualifying rental income are 4,000, giving 1,200 net yield and **600 credit**, subject to available regional gross tax. The 50%rate applies to 1,200; it is not applied again after a national housing-rental percentage reduction. This full-tax-period reading does not replace 800 with `800×200/365`, which would instead reduce this particular 600 result to 438.36 under the competing lease-day approach. With three independently qualifying homes and net yields 1,200/2,000/3,000, the home amounts 600/800/800 sum to 2,200; the taxpayer ceiling gives **2,000**, not 2,200. These are derived controls, not published administrative worked examples.

This supplies the stated positive-net-yield, sole-owner, post-commencement full-year branch. Allocation between earlier and replacement contracts in the same 2026 income stream, co-owner/joint-return cap attribution, short-period proportional mechanics and early-accrual transition remain unconfirmed. Do not infer zero relief or a general lease-day rule from those limits ([art 4 quater.3–4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-13)).

Temporary island relocation requires the **same employment relationship**, actual rented-home occupation, IBAVI deposit, landlord identification and evidence, and a move lasting no more than **three years**; each eligible joint-return member can claim their own credit. The occupation/eviction route covers IBI, waste tax, home utilities, ordinary community costs, conservation/maintenance and recovery-of-possession court costs. In a vulnerability-based suspended eviction, the landlord must also qualify for the rental bad-debt expense in that same fiscal year ([arts. 4 quinquies–4 sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-16)).

### Illes Balears: education, family and care

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Textbooks | 100% of eligible spending, cap 220 per child; enhanced groups cap 350 per child | 2026 | [Art. 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4) |
| Extra-curricular foreign languages | 15%, cap 110 per child; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 4 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4bis) |
| Higher education outside the home island | 1,800 per eligible descendant, limited to 50% of regional gross tax; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 4 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-12) |
| Taxpayer/family-unit disability | Physical/sensory disability 33%–under 65%: 88; physical/sensory at least 65%, or mental disability at least 33%: 165; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6) |
| Work/family reconciliation, descendants/foster children under six | 40% of eligible costs, annual credit cap 660; enhanced groups 50%, cap 900; ordinary BI gate 33,000 I / 52,800 J with the stated family uplifts | 2026 | [Art. 6 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-12) |
| Birth | First / second / third / fourth and subsequent child: 900 / 1,200 / 1,500 / 1,800; BI gate 52,800 I / 84,480 J, qualifying-family uplift 20%; above that gate retain 50% of the corresponding credit | Current June 2026 wording; early-accrual transition unresolved | [Art. 6 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-13) |
| Adoption | Same current child-order amounts as birth; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J, qualifying-family uplift 20%; **no statutory above-limit half-credit** | Current June 2026 wording; early-accrual transition unresolved | [Art. 6 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-14) |
| Care of over-65/disabled persons | 40% of eligible costs, credit cap 660; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 6 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-15) |
| ALS-related expenses | 100% of eligible costs, credit cap 3,500; BI at most 52,800 I / 84,480 J | 2026 | [Art. 6 sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-16) |

Textbooks must support the second infant-education cycle, primary, compulsory secondary, bachillerato or specific vocational education, and the child must generate the descendant minimum. The 350 cap uses the enhanced rent groups above, except that the textbook age route is **under 30**. Foreign-language relief concerns extra-curricular language learning by children in those stages; do not import the textbook family income uplift into art. 4 bis. Split these child credits equally between cohabiting parents filing individually and retain invoices. Higher-education relief requires economic dependence, the descendant minimum, study outside the taxpayer's Balearic home island, at least a complete academic course or **30 credits**, and descendant income no more than **8,000**; claim in the year the academic course starts and share between cohabiting entitled individual ascendants ([arts. 4–4 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a4-12)).

The disability credit expressly allows each individually filing spouse entitled to the descendant minimum to claim the **full** art. 6 amount. This is not the equal-sharing rule used for the education credits. Under-six care requires income-generating employment/self-employment, qualifying zero-to-three nursery costs, three-to-six school custody/meals/activities, or an employed carer registered in household Social Security. Pay by card, transfer, named cheque or credit-institution deposit. Enhanced care groups match rent except the age route is **under 36**; family income gates rise by 20% or 30% as stated above. The statutory day-proration and sharing rules are applied below ([arts.6–6bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-12)).

**Under-six child-day calculation.** Keep a separate record for each eligible child: supported expense, paid service period, birth/adoption/fostering date, sixth birthday, qualifying days and number of entitled individual filers. The exact [AEAT return instructions10.4.18](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_4-illes-balears/10_4_18-gastos-relativos-descendientes-acogidos-anos.html) administer the660/900 limit **per child** and require child-by-child inputs. For2026 use the child's actual qualifying days `d/365` when birth/adoption/fostering or age-out requires proration. Count days from birth/placement and only days before the sixth birthday, and exclude the post-birthday part of that month's expense. Include only expense for the months in which the claimant undertakes the required income-generating work/activity. Calculate the eligible percentage against each child's supported cost, subject to that child's prorated cap, then add the child amounts. Do not replace two overlapping children's periods with a single union of days: that would discard a separately entered child's cap. This follows AEAT's expressly stated per-child implementation of [art6bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-12), whose practical-manual summary uses plural/sum-of-days wording.

For two entitled parents filing separately, divide the child's applicable maximum equally as the practical manual instructs; on a joint return do not divide it between the two spouses. For the ordinary same-rate/equal-payment case, each parent uses their own half of qualifying cost and half the child's prorated maximum. Thus a full-year child with2,000 eligible shared cost gives660 on the joint return or330 to each equally paying entitled individual parent. Two full-year children each with2,000 eligible cost give1,320 on the joint return, or660 to each such individual parent. With twins born1July2026, each has184 qualifying days: each ordinary child cap is `660×184/365=332.7123…`; sufficient qualifying expense gives665.42 across both children on the joint return. At the enhanced rate the corresponding combined cap is `2×900×184/365=907.3972…`, or907.40. Keep full precision until the return's monetary display; these amounts do not prescribe a special statutory rounding convention.

**Unequal payments and different personal rates on individual returns.** Apply the published AEAT administrative worksheet separately to each entitled claimant and each child. Establish that both taxpayers meet the existing work, income, child/service and payment conditions and file individual returns. Use the claimant's **own evidenced eligible payments** `Eij`, their own rate `ri` (40% or50%) and their own annual maximum `Li` (660 or900). With that child's qualifying-day fraction `fj` established above, calculate `creditij = min(ri × Eij, Li × fj /2)`; add the child results for that claimant. If only one taxpayer is entitled, the two-entitled-person sharing condition does not apply. Do not divide the claimant's own payment by two again, transfer the other payer's expense, or reallocate an unused half-cap. A shared bank account does not itself prove an unequal personal-payment allocation. The [practical manual](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-illes-balears/gastos-descendientes-acogidos-menores-seis-conciliacion.html) expressly divides the **maximum** for two entitled individual filers; the existing return instructions expressly require payments by the titular, entitled-person count and child-day inputs. This supplies the ordinary published administrative method under current6bis, continued from the2025 instructions where the relevant statutory rate/sharing provisions are unchanged; it does not assume a2027 form box or procedure.

For one full-year child and two ordinary40%claimants, actual own payments of1,500and500give `min(600,330)=330` and `min(200,330)=200`, **530total**. Pooling2,000then dividing a660credit into330each would give the smaller payer130more than this own-payment method. If the500payer independently qualifies for the50%/900enhancement through at least183days in the relevant self-employed Social Security regime and the1,500payer remains ordinary, their results are `min(250,450)=250` and `min(600,330)=330`, **580total**. Reversing which payer has the enhancement gives `min(750,450)=450` and `min(200,330)=200`, **650total**.
