These are individual-return household totals before available regional quota, with all separate eligibility gates assumed established. The statutory enhancement is tested for the actual claimant; one person's age/registration condition does not automatically upgrade the other person's rate. Actual public Renta2025OPEN1.19 inputs corroborate each of the330/200/250/450claimant amounts; this is2025 software evidence, not a binding ruling or a2026-income simulator claim.

**Precise sharing interpretation boundary.** Current [6bis(4)](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-12) refers to the deduction being shared equally, while the practical manual expressly shares the maximum and the form asks for own payments. The positive worksheet above states that published administrative application; it does not claim that the statutory wording was amended or that a contrary whole-credit-sharing challenge has been authoritatively decided. The existing same-rate/equal-payment and joint examples remain as stated above. A mixed-personal-rate joint return needs its own supported joint attribution and cannot be computed merely by pooling individual rates or transferring half-caps; that narrower joint interaction remains unconfirmed here.


The original equal-payment sharing controls above assume equally paying, equally entitled parents at the same rate. For unequal payments or different personal rates on individual returns, use the supplied individual-return worksheet above. Neither set of controls transfers enhanced status between parents or establishes entitlement for a non-paying parent. The mixed-rate joint-return and statutory-interpretation boundaries remain as stated above.

Birth requires Balearic fiscal residence in the **preceding year**; adoption does not copy that condition. Child order counts cohabiting natural/adopted children generating the minimum at accrual. Birth's current sharing rule halves the amount for each entitled parent when two parents are involved and do not file jointly together, **even if only one qualifies**. Each parent's pre-existing children can give a different half-amount; a joint return in that case adds those two halves. Adoption's sharing provision instead refers to two taxpayers with entitlement, and excludes adoption of a spouse/partner's child. Claim adoption in the Civil Registry inscription year. Both credits take priority over other regional credits and are incompatible with the regional birth aid specified in their articles. Apply the current birth/adoption amounts above only after establishing the relevant period and sharing facts ([arts. 6 ter–6 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-14)).

Insufficient regional tax does not make these two family credits an automatic negative regional liability. Their articles authorise a **separate regional payment request**. For birth, [Order 37/2024 arts. 2–7](https://www.caib.es/seucaib/ca/arxiuServlet?id=6261230) supplies **IB50** advance payment, normally within **15 working days** of birth, and **IB52** insufficient-tax payment, from the day after the annual-return filing window ends through **30 September of that filing year**. Missing the advance deadline does not remove the ordinary return claim. Test prior-year income for the advance and reconcile actual birth-year entitlement and any advance on the return. IB51/IB53 handle the corresponding repayment, with art. 10's one-month deadlines and triggers; current statutory entitlement controls where an older form/order predates an amendment. The current adoption payment procedure has not been confirmed here; do not present the birth-only forms as adoption forms ([arts. 6 ter–6 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-14); [Order 37/2024](https://www.caib.es/seucaib/ca/arxiuServlet?id=6261230)).

Older-person care covers the taxpayer or minimum-entitled ascendants over **65**; disability relief extends to the taxpayer or dependants with at least **33%** disability and entitlement to an art. 60 disability-minimum component. Eligible services are residential/day-centre stays, day-centre custody/meals/activities and an employed carer registered in Social Security. Require the specified bank payment methods. For a shared ascendant each claimant can claim their own full qualifying deduction; for shared descendants on individual returns divide equally. Art. 6 quinquies is incompatible with the under-six care credit. ALS relief covers the taxpayer and minimum-entitled ascendants/descendants, requires invoices and an ALS causal connection, excludes health-insurance premiums and unrelated costs, and is expressly compatible with the older/disabled-care credit ([arts. 6 quinquies–6 sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a6-16)).

### Illes Balears: gifts, investment, self-employment and public aid

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Research/science/technology/innovation cash gifts | 25%, capped at 15% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a5) |
| Cultural/scientific/technology patronage | 15% of statutory value, credit cap 660; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J. Qualifying own regional-government/instrumental projects: 25%, cap 1,200, without that BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 5 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a5bis) |
| Sports patronage | 15% of statutory value, credit cap 660; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 5 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a5ter) |
| Catalan-language cash gifts | 15%, capped at 10% of regional gross tax; incompatible with art. 5 bis | 2026 | [Art. 5 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a5quater) |
| Registered qualifying third-sector cash gifts | 25%, **base** cap 165, so maximum credit 41.25 | 2026 | [Art. 5 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a5-12) |
| New/recent-company equity | 30%, annual credit cap 6,000 per investing taxpayer; research-centre/university-participated company route 50%, cap 12,000 | 2026 | [Art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a7) |
| First self-employment census registration | 1,000 per eligible taxpayer; BI at most 33,000 I / 52,800 J | 2026 | [Art. 7 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a7-13) |
| Difficult-to-fill public posts | 40% of eligible home/inter-island travel expenditure; cap 2,000 at BI up to 33,000 I / 52,800 J, or 1,000 at BI up to 52,800 I / 84,480 J | 2026 | [Art. 7 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a7-14) |
| Declared civil-protection emergency aid | Average-rate credit on the qualifying grant amount included in BL; regional grant, declared qualifying emergency and general-BI inclusion required | 2026 | [Art. 3 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-12) |
| Regional Middle East-war aid | Regional average rate times the included regional-BL grant amount; activity-income attribution uses the grant's share of gross activity receipts times reduced net activity income | 2026 expressly | [Art. 3 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-15) |
| Qualifying thalidomide public aid | Average-rate credit on the aid included in BL, subject to the general-BI inclusion condition | Article expressly starts with fiscal 2023; added June 2026 | [Art. 3 sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-17) |

Research gifts require the listed regional administration/instrumental entity, UIB, qualifying regionally registered research nonprofit or LIS art. 9.3 partially exempt entity, a donor certificate and, for the statutory nonprofit category, the competent research resolution. Catalan-language gifts have their own recipient/certificate and, for the relevant foundation category, language-policy resolution requirements. Third-sector recipients need the relevant regional registration and the stated Ley 49/2002 or partial-CIT-exemption status. Art. 5 sexties' Ukraine donation credit is expressly **2022 only** and is not renewed merely because a donation is made in 2026 ([arts. 5–5 sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a5-13)).

For cultural/scientific or sports patronage, verify the recipient, project/activity and instrument under [Ley 3/2015 arts. 2–4 and 7–14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-4331#a7-2) or [Ley 6/2015 arts. 2–4 and 7–14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-4791#a7). Cash donations, qualifying free-use loans and written collaboration agreements have different valuation/evidence rules. Cultural recipient rules exclude the specified related-person arrangements and noncompliant entities. Retain donor/recipient names and tax IDs, date, amount or statutory valuation, authentic transfer/agreement evidence, purpose and the required irrevocability statement. Free-use annual bases use **4%** of the statutory valuation, apportioned by days; cultural protected/art assets use the heritage commission's valuation, scientific/technology premises use cadastral value, and sports premises/land/real estate use cadastral value. Apply the article's short-loan cap proration and maximum **three claim years** for longer loans. Detailed non-cash gift valuations and combined-instrument cap/proration remain to confirm; do not substitute a commercial rental estimate for the statutory base ([Ley 3/2015 arts. 8–14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-4331#a11-2); [Ley 6/2015 arts. 8–14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-4791#a1-3); [DL 1/2014 arts. 5 bis–5 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a5ter)).

Equity investment must be formation/capital-increase subscriptions in SA, SL, labour SA/SL or cooperatives with Balearic registered/tax offices, genuine economic activity excluding principal asset management and property letting, at least one full-time general-regime **non-shareholder** employee fiscally domiciled in the Balearics, maintained average employment and statutory turnover no more than **2 million**. A capital-increase company must be no more than **two years** old unless it holds the current registered innovative-SME seal. Family-through-third-degree ownership/votes must stay at most **40%**; the investor cannot perform executive/management functions or work for the company. Retain the public deed identifying investors and amounts. Shares, the listed entity conditions, ownership limit and role exclusion must continue for **four years**; breach recaptures the tax saved plus interest in the breach-year return ([art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a7)).

The ordinary investment credit can be used in the investment year and **two following years**, oldest first, within one aggregate **6,000 annual cap per taxpayer** across the investment vintages. The enhanced research/university route's 12,000 cap is not an extra independent allowance on top of 6,000; AEAT confirms a combined maximum of **12,000** where both types arise. Do not regenerate the percentage credit in each year. The detailed allocation of mixed enhanced/ordinary unused credits remains a specific gap ([art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a7); [AEAT investment interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-illes-balears/inversion-adquisicion-acciones-nuevas-entidades-creacion.html)).

The self-employment credit uses the **first** entry in the Empresarios, Profesionales y Retenedores census, activity mainly in the Balearics and at least **one year** continuously on that census; do not replace it with any later RETA re-entry. Each eligible joint-return member can claim. Difficult-post relief requires the qualifying regional public-sector supplement for all or **more than half** the period, or an effective Policía Nacional/Guardia Civil Balearic posting for that duration. Home acquisition/construction costs mean **mortgage interest only**. For those police/guard postings, reduce the cap **30% in Mallorca** or increase it **30% in Formentera**, and apportion caps by posting days where islands change ([arts. 7 bis–7 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a7-14)).

For the three public-aid routes, first exclude exempt aid from the taxable-grant computation and establish the included amount under the proper income category. Art. 3 quinquies supplies a specific regional-rate and activity-income attribution; it does not exempt all war-related receipts or convert the gross grant into the base where only a share of reduced net income is attributable. The attributable-tax worksheet under The base supplies the civil-emergency general-rate/base method and the distinct current war-aid formula. The newly enacted thalidomide article still needs direct clarification of combined versus regional-only general rate and, where applicable, its share of common employment expenses; old-year correction administration also remains to confirm ([arts. 3 ter, 3 quinquies and 3 sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-17)).


### Cantabria: income definitions, housing, health and family

Use each credit's own income, family and municipality tests. The current statutory wording includes [Ley 5/2026 art. 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-10431), effective **1 May 2026** under its final provision 3. The table concerns ordinary year-end 2026 claims; short-period treatment before that date remains to confirm. Where the article requires liquidable income after the personal/family minimum, calculate **general BL + savings BL − the personal/family minimum**, as confirmed by [AEAT's interpretation of that test](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/gastos-enfermedad.html). The test is **strictly below 22,946 I / 31,485 J**, not an inclusive BI or salary limit. Apply DA 1's state-law habitual-home/family/disability concepts and DA 2's registered-partner equivalence to the relevant regional spouse conditions; state-law family-unit rules still determine the filing mode ([DA 1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#da-4)). Apply it only to the credits that prescribe it ([art. 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ordinary habitual-home rent | 10%, credit cap 300 I / 600 J; eligible age/disability and rent exceeding 10% of the taxpayer's statutory income | 2026, amended age route | [Art. 2.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Family care | 100 per qualifying under-three descendant, over-70 ascendant or listed relative with disability at least 65%; relative gross-income gate 6,000, or 1.5 annual IPREM for disability | 2026 | [Art. 2.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Home improvement works | 15%; annual credit cap 1,000 I / 1,500 J, with the specified 500 disability increases; excess over the annual ceiling has a two-following-year route | 2026 | [Art. 2.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Unpaid qualifying fostering | 240 times the maximum concurrent qualifying foster-child count, credit cap 1,200; statutory continued adult fostering included | 2026 | [Art. 2.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Ordinary uninsured health professional fees | 10%, credit cap 500 I / 700 J; increase by 100 for the claimant's at-least-65% disability, or by 100 for each qualifying joint-return contributor; liquidable-income/minimum test above | 2026, current wording | [Art. 2.7.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Registered rare-disease diagnosis/treatment | 20%, credit cap 1,000 I / 1,200 J; same liquidable-income/minimum test | 2026, new route | [Art. 2.7.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| ALS diagnosis/treatment | 50%, credit cap 2,000; do not import the other two health routes' income ceiling into this paragraph | 2026, new route | [Art. 2.7.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Nursery for biological/adopted children under three | 15%, credit cap 300 per child, allocated by each claimant's justified costs without duplicating the child ceiling | 2026 | [Art. 2.8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Single-parent family | 200; general plus savings BL less the personal/family minimum strictly below 31,485 | 2026 | [Art. 2.9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Birth/adoption | 1,400, raised **to** 1,600 for disability at least 33%; event year and next two years, for births/adoptions from 1 January 2024 | 2026, current wording | [Art. 2.10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Compulsory-stage education | 100% of qualifying textbooks plus 15% of eligible extra-curricular language teaching, combined credit cap **200 per family unit**, split in half per parent filing individually | 2026 | [Art. 2, second paragraph numbered 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Household help | 20% of employer-paid household-worker Social Security, credit cap 300; specific family/age/disability route and registration required | 2026, current wording | [Art. 2.14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Exercise/sport | 30%, credit cap 150; liquidable-income/minimum test above; qualifying services in Cantabria | 2026, new route | [Art. 2.18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Under-36 habitual-home purchase/rehabilitation | 15% of eligible current-year payments, annual **base** cap 9,000; acquisition/rehabilitation year and next two; liquidable-income/minimum test above | 2026, new route | [Art. 2.19.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Under-36 qualifying home-savings account | 15% of qualifying deposits, annual **base** cap 6,000; bank agreement and ten-year destination rules; same liquidable-income/minimum test | 2026, new route | [Art. 2.19.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Environmental commitment | Municipal home-waste fee actually paid, credit cap 100, divided between entitled owners/tenants; certified qualifying participation required | 2026, new route | [Art. 2.20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |

Ordinary rent requires age **under 36**, or **under 40 with at least two children included in the family minimum**, or **at least 65**; disability at least **65%** removes the age condition. At least one declarant must qualify for the joint 600 cap. Require rent strictly exceeding **10%** of the taxpayer's income, a named tenancy, invoice/receipt and a permitted bank payment. Do not reuse another community's BI ceiling. The ordinary individual positive-employment/bank-interest method and the bounded individual progressivity-exempt/current-share-loss extension are supplied below; other income populations remain specifically reserved. The same home's ordinary and depopulation-zone rent routes cannot both be claimed ([art. 2.1/11.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [AEAT rent interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/arrendamiento-vivienda-habitual-jovenes-mayores-discapacidad.html)).

Family care requires cohabitation **more than 183 days**, except for under-three children. The disabled-relative list is ascendant, descendant, spouse or sibling, including the statutory affinity cases. For the disability income gate, AEAT uses annual IPREM including extra payments (**8,400** under the continued budget), giving **1.5 × 8,400 = 12,600**, rather than silently using the 12-payment annual figure of 7,200. The 100 age-based and 100 disability-based amounts can both arise for the same qualifying person. The birth-credit incompatibility concerns the same child, with the express disability exception preserved ([art. 2.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [AEAT care interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/cuidado-familiares.html); [Ley 31/2022 DA 90](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2022-22128#da-90); [2026 budget extension](https://www.sepg.pap.hacienda.gob.es/sitios/sepg/es-ES/Presupuestos/PGE/PGE2025Prorroga/Paginas/PGE2025Prorroga.aspx)).

**Ordinary individual rent-burden computation.** For an individual ordinary claim with positive taxable employment income and/or bank-interest savings income, no exempt income or loss/offset interactions, use `R=general BI+savings BI` for the rent-burden test, before pension/base-liquidable reductions and without subtracting the personal/family minimum. This follows the actual AEAT public return implementation, read with the unchanged statutory10% condition. Eligibility requires `eligible rent>0.10×R`; equality fails. Then compute `min(10%×eligible rent,300)` and apply the ordinary regional-quota limit. For gross employment30,000, Social Security3,000 and ordinary deductible employment expense2,000, R is25,000; rent2,500 fails and2,500.01 gives250.00. A1,500 pension reduction does not change that R. Adding10,000 taxable bank interest raises R to35,000; rent3,500 fails and3,500.01 gives the300 cap. Keep the existing age/disability, contract-holder, non-cash payment and incompatibility requirements. [Art2.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [exact return inputs](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_6-cantabria/10_6_2-arrendamiento-vivienda-habitual.html); [official public simulator](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/tramitacion/ZZ08.shtml), checked27October2026.

This is controlled inference from the official RentaWEBOpen2025 version1.19 OPEN implementation, checked against the unchanged income-comparison wording of current2026 art2.1. It is not an express statutory definition or a claim that the2026 return campaign was tested. Six fictional-input observations distinguished gross employment, net taxable income, pension-reduced BL and savings inclusion. Those original controls did not test exempt income or loss/offset interactions; the additional bounded individual population tested below extends that evidence, without a universal denominator rule. The ordinary married joint-return implementation in the same positive-income population is supplied next.

**Ordinary married joint rent-burden calculation.** In the same positive taxable-income population, test each claimant using their own attributed eligible rent and individual `R=general BI+savings BI`, before base-liquidable reductions/minima. At least one declarant must satisfy the applicable age/disability and strict `own rent>10%×own R` requirements. Once that test is met, compute `min(10%×combined eligible rent paid by the joint-return contract holders,600)`, subject to available regional tax. Do not replace that personal qualification with a test against aggregate joint BI or pension/joint-filing-reduced BL. Nor is the joint calculation the sum of two separately300-capped individual credits. Keep the documented contract-holder/payment attribution: a non-tenant spouse's payment is not made eligible by filing jointly. Where community-property funds pay a lease naming only one spouse, AEAT attributes only50% of that rent to the named tenant ([art2.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [exact AEAT joint-return and payment instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_6-cantabria/10_6_2-arrendamiento-vivienda-habitual.html)).

This is a controlled inference from the official2025 simulator's unchanged income-comparison implementation, rather than an observed2026 campaign. In four fictional configurations, both spouses were35 and each had30,000 taxable bank interest; joint BI was60,000 and joint BL56,600. With eligible rent paid only by one spouse,4,000 gave300 individually and400 jointly;3,000 gave no credit;3,000.01 gave300 individually and300 jointly. With that spouse paying3,000.01 and the other named tenant paying3,000, the latter failed the personal test individually, but the joint calculation used6,000.01 and reached600 because the first spouse qualified. These are eligible attributed payment inputs, not authority to claim amounts paid by an unnamed spouse. For joint claims, exempt income, losses/offsets and other family/entitlement combinations remain to verify.

**Individual current-share-loss and progressivity-exempt extension, observed administrative method.** Keep all existing eligibility, documented contract-holder payment, strict10%test and regional-tax conditions. This additional bounded population has a positive verified employment-income amount `W` entering general BI, positive taxable bank-interest income `B`, and a current-year fully computable listed-share disposal loss `L`, with no other taxable income/gains/losses or prior-year loss carry used. A separately established general-base **exempt income with progressivity** amount `E` can also be present. The loss must actually qualify for current integration, including the [33.5(f) homogeneous-share restriction](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33); an economic loss with deferred tax recognition is not an eligible `L`.

First apply [LIRPF49.1(b)](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a49): `C=min(L,25%×B)`, leaving savings BI `B−C`; the unused current loss `L−C` is separately tracked under article49, not subtracted again this year. For this observed population, the rent-burden denominator is `R=W+B−C`. Do not add `E` to this bounded denominator or subtract pension/base-liquidable reductions or personal/family minima. Exempt-progressivity income remains relevant to the tax-rate calculation under [DA20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-20); excluding it from this observed rent test does not eliminate that separate tax effect. Require `eligible rent>10%×R`, then individual credit=`min(10%×eligible rent,300)`, within available regional tax. This combines the statutory integration rules with actual regional software observations; neither the regional statute nor its manual expressly defines a universal `R=BI` formula or a clamp for every signed category.

**Separating fictional controls, actually observed28October2026 in RentaWEBOpen2025 version1.19OPEN.** A single eligible claimant born1January1995 has verified employment income25,000 entering general BI, bank interest10,000 and pension reduction1,500. Adding10,000of general-base exempt income with progressivity leaves rent3,500 failing and3,500.01 giving300, while the simulator separately changes the tax-rate/quota calculation. Adding a currently computable listed-share loss8,000, from a1,000sale/9,000acquisition with no homogeneous-share reacquisition, permits a2,500current offset against the10,000interest; savings BI becomes7,500 and `R=32,500`. Rent3,250 fails;3,250.01 gives300. The remaining5,500loss does not reduce this year's denominator. The raw signed total27,000, unoffset35,000, pension-reduced combined BL31,000 and exempt-inclusive42,500 would give different separating outcomes; none replaces the observed32,500. Actual tax bases, active exempt input, loss recognition, retained rent inputs and recalculated credit were read; no filing or login occurred.

The software is2025 implementation evidence for the unchanged income-comparison wording of [current2026 Cantabria2.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2), not an observed2026campaign or a statutory definition of every income population. The existing positive individual and married-joint methods remain as supplied. Other exempt categories, savings-side exempt-progressivity treatment, negative general income, other current-category combinations, prior-year offsets and joint/family combinations beyond their existing supplied methods remain unconfirmed; no universal BI clamp, full-economic-loss subtraction or zero credit is inferred ([official public simulator](https://www2.agenciatributaria.gob.es/wlpl/PARE-RW25/OPEN/index.zul?EJER=2025&TACCESO=COLAB)).

Home works can concern an owned Cantabrian home or its building and must fit qualified rehabilitation, efficiency/environment/accessibility, the stated renewable-energy/safety/utility works, or qualifying telecommunications infrastructure. Exclude economic-activity homes, garages, gardens, pools/sports facilities and analogous items, expenditure eligible for a state deduction, and expenditure funded by the specified untaxed/exempt grants. The disability increase is **500** for an eligible individual claimant or **500 per qualifying joint contributor**. The two-year route is expressly for sums not deducted because they exceed the annual ceiling; it does not authorise carrying every amount lost for insufficient tax ([art. 2.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

Fostering requires public selection/supervision, no kinship and no adoption of that foster child during the period. Use the **maximum simultaneous** count, not every child sequentially fostered. Split between spouses, registered or unregistered permanent partners filing individually. Continued cohabitation after adulthood qualifies only under the public authority's approval/supervision and the adult-descendant-minimum conditions. The single-parent definition uses separation/no marriage, cohabiting minor children except independently living minors, or the statutory adult-incapacity/extended-parental-authority route. Do not substitute another region's single-parent definition ([art. 2.5/9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

Health credits concern the taxpayer or persons included in the family minimum and eligible costs not covered by Social Security, mutuality or insurance. Rare-disease relief requires the condition's inclusion in the state register under RD 1091/2015. Split shared rare-disease and ALS claims equally. All three require invoices and permitted bank payment; exclude health-insurance premiums, reimbursable treatment and the specified payments to public-benefit associations enjoying the national donation credit. The source-derived same-cost enhanced-rate method and its precise competing-text boundary are set out below. Nursery also requires invoiced bank payment. Birth/adoption amounts are halved per parent/adopter on individual filing and are compatible with the stated national credit; the 2026 uplift does not itself establish a correction entitlement for every earlier return ([art. 2.7–10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

**One same health cost: source-derived enhanced-rate reading.** Establish each route separately before applying a percentage: ordinary2.7.1 covers eligible professional health fees, rare-disease2.7.2 needs documented inclusion of the condition in the statutory state register and diagnosis/treatment expense, and ALS2.7.3 needs an evidenced ALS diagnosis/treatment connection. A diagnosis does not by itself prove professional-fee status or registry inclusion. Apply the first two routes’ own strict income test and their own annual caps; the ALS paragraph has no such income ceiling. For a single identifiable expense independently satisfying more than one of these cases, this method treats the specific20%rare or50%ALS rate as the enhanced rate **instead of adding**10%/20%again to that same cost. Use the ALS case for an evidenced ALS cost, including where rare-disease status is separately established; otherwise use the qualifying rare-disease case or ordinary case. Do not recycle the same expense above the selected branch’s cap into another branch in this one-cost worksheet. This is a labelled interpretation of the original2026amendment’s express explanation that the health deduction is raised to20%for rare diseases and50%for ALS, read with current2.7’s nested cases/common paragraph4. It is not a universal rule against claiming different tax credits, an express enacted election/incompatibility clause or a2026agency calculation ([current2.7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [Ley5/2026 preamble and art2.Dos](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-10431)).

**Binding-cap same-invoice ledger.** A full-year2026Cantabrian45-year-old individual taxpayer, with no disability-cap increase or competing family claimant, has general BL28,000, savings BL0 and personal/family minimum5,550: income-test amount `28,000+0−5,550=22,450`, strictly below22,946. On15July2026 the taxpayer pays one5,000neurologist professional invoice by bank transfer for their own documented ALS diagnosis/treatment; the condition’s state-register inclusion is separately evidenced. None is covered/reimbursable, an insurance premium, a cash payment or an excluded public-benefit-association payment. Thus all three route definitions independently hold. The capped branch amounts are ordinary `min(10%×5,000,500)=500`, rare `min(20%×5,000,1,000)=1,000` and ALS `min(50%×5,000,2,000)=2,000`. Under the labelled enhanced-rate reading use **2,000once** for this invoice. With no other credits, the regional general quota is `1,985+(28,000−21,000)×14.5%−5,550×8.5%=2,528.25`: use2,000and leave528.25. Adding all three capped amounts would give3,500nominal and consume2,528.25, a different result ([own regional scale](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a1); [LIRPF74/77](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a74)). For the same facts with income-test amount exactly22,946, ordinary/rare fail their strict income gate while ALS still gives2,000: do not import their ceiling into ALS. The figures are a hypothetical source-derived2026ledger, not a software result or a real patient’s records.

**Exact remaining interpretation boundary.** The operative replacement separately says the taxpayer may deduct10%,20%and50%, gives separate ceilings and refers to three cases/deductions; it does not expressly state same-cost exclusivity or cumulation. That wording supports a competing cumulative-grants reading. The selected enhanced-rate method relies on the same enactment’s specific explanatory increase-to wording; a definitive ruling or operative2026agency worksheet resolving that tension has not been established here. The2025AEATmanual explains only the unchanged ordinary10%method and its income/payment gates, so neither it nor2025public software proves the new2026overlap. Keep a route-by-route expense ledger and the exact source reasoning; the competing statutory reading/challenge, allocations involving distinct mixed annual expense pools and shared-claimant/quota ordering beyond this one-cost individual example remain precise ordinary limits. The existing rates, separate non-overlap claims and payment/exclusion rules above remain applicable ([original enactment](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-10431); [dated ordinary explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/gastos-enfermedad.html)).

Education covers compulsory-stage books and extra-curricular language teaching by the specified educational institutions or appropriately registered individual tutors, with invoices/bank payment. It has its own **200 family-unit** ceiling, not 200 per child. Domestic help uses **employer contributions**, one worker on individual returns with permitted allocation, the habitual workplace at home and the Cantabria household-system registration. Qualify through working parents of minor children (both parents working where required), a working single parent, the claimant/spouse/registered partner aged **at least 75**, or the specified cohabiting at-least-65%-disabled relative. Exercise relief is limited to qualifying gym/sport-facility services, registered sporting entities, sports/physical-education classes or Cantabrian federation licences, provided in Cantabria in the period for the taxpayer, spouse or minimum-entitled family members; split both expense and cap equally between claimants ([art. 2, education paragraph 13, and 14/18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

The under-36 condition for both home routes is measured **at accrual**. The acquisition/rehabilitation base includes qualifying purchase costs and, if financed, principal, interest and associated financing costs; apply 15% to at most **9,000**, a maximum nominal annual credit **1,350**. Do not treat the three claim years as permission to deduct the same payment repeatedly. The home-account bank must have signed the required government agreement. Track each year's deposits: use for the qualifying home purpose by the end of the tax period in which **ten years** have elapsed from that deposit-year accrual; no new deductible deposits after the separate ten-year account-opening horizon. The statute permits restoration/transfer in full before accrual for the stated withdrawal rule, and expressly bars a second deduction when previously credited deposits fund the home. Operative bank agreements and overlapping home/investment credits remain to verify. Environmental relief requires the habitual Cantabrian home, paid waste-fee proof and participation certified by an eligible public/other body with the required government agreement; volunteering alone is insufficient ([art. 2.19–20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

### Cantabria: depopulation municipalities and residential moves

Use the actual municipality under [Order PRE/1/2025](https://boc.cantabria.es/boces/verAnuncioAction.do?idAnuBlob=414590), its annex and applicable updates. The order's transitional provision applies its list to relevant taxable events from **1 January 2025**, and its additional provision requires annex updates **every two years**. The annex includes risk, serious-risk and specially treated municipalities. An old annual fiscal list is not a substitute. Determine residence under LIRPF art. 72 and preserve each credit's distinct duration test ([art. 2.11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Qualifying-municipality habitual-home rent | 20%, credit cap 600 I / 1,200 J; incompatible with the ordinary rent route for the same home | 2026 | [Art. 2.11.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Nursery with habitual residence in a qualifying municipality | 30%, credit cap 600 per under-three biological/adopted child; incompatible with ordinary nursery relief | 2026 | [Art. 2.11.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Employment/self-employment move from elsewhere in Spain to a qualifying municipality | 500 in the move year and next year, limited by the regional gross tax attributable to eligible employment/activity income | 2026 | [Art. 2.11.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Study-related move | 200 per student child under 25, annual income **below 8,000**, studying bachillerato, vocational training or university outside the qualifying home municipality; half per parent individually | 2026 | [Art. 2.11.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Under-40 resident of a qualifying municipality for the whole calendar year | 20% of regional gross tax, credit cap 500; each entitled joint-return contributor's amount | 2026 | [Art. 2.11.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| New resident arriving from another Spanish community | 10% of eligible move/stay costs, annual credit cap 1,000; work-related move 25%, cap 1,500; move year and next three years | 2026 | [Art. 2.16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Previously vacant home brought into letting | 500 per eligible home, allocated by ownership share; vacancy, rent, property-count and three-year letting conditions | 2026 | [Art. 2.17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |

For arts. 2.11.1–11.4, retain the required invoices/receipts and bank-payment evidence; cash is excluded. Do not impose the ordinary rent route's age gate on the municipality-specific rent provision. The rural work move requires residence in the move year and **three following years**, although the credit is claimed only in the first **two**. Joint filing allows 500 per qualifying mover, subject to the combined attributable-tax ceiling for those qualifying contributors; the mixed general/savings, minimum-spillover and base-reduction calculation is supplied in the attributable-tax worksheet under The base. Breach triggers the specified tax-and-interest recapture ([art. 2.11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [AEAT rural-move interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/gastos-traslado-residencia-municipio-afectado-despoblamiento.html)).

The separate new-resident route requires no Cantabria habitual residence during the preceding **four years**, and residence maintained for the **three years following** arrival. AEAT includes arrivals from Ceuta/Melilla but excludes direct foreign arrivals for this route. Qualifying costs are necessary family travel/moving, descendants' compulsory/bachillerato schooling, habitual-home rent and **connection/contracting costs**, excluding ongoing utilities/service consumption. Test actual expenses in each of the four claim years. For joint returns apply each contributor's own rate/cap; AEAT treats an accompanying spouse as having the work motivation of the working spouse and allocates community-property costs equally. Unabsorbed credit generated in each year has **four following utilisation years**, oldest first, and both carried/current art. 2.16 amounts take priority over other regional credits. Do not confuse that utilisation clock with the four generation years. Death does not cause the residence-breach recapture under AEAT's interpretation ([art. 2.16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [AEAT new-resident interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/compensar-gastos-desplazamiento-permanencia-nuevos-residentes.html)).

Vacant-home letting requires at least **one year** without habitation, letting, use or business allocation before the qualifying LAU lease, effective letting for at least **three years**, rent including annexes no more than **1,000 monthly**, and ownership of no more than **three homes**. The tenant cannot be spouse or a relative through the **third degree**. The statute allows a replacement lease within **six months** where the combined effective letting periods reach three years; maintain evidence of availability during the gap. Allocate the 500 credit by property participation instead of granting every co-owner the whole amount. The timing of repeated claims for one lease and compatibility with the separate new-resident/rural expense credits remain to confirm ([art. 2.17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

### Cantabria: gifts and investment

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Qualifying Cantabrian foundation/disability-association gift | 15%; Cantabria Coopera gifts 12%; aggregate gift base with the stated national gifts/historic-heritage bases at most 10% of BL | 2026 | [Art. 2.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| New/recent SME equity | 15%, annual credit cap 1,000; three-year holding/condition period | 2026 | [Art. 2.6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Social-economy membership investment | 20% | 2026 | [Art. 2.12.a](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Irrevocable social-economy economic-activity gift | 50% | 2026 | [Art. 2.12.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Irrevocable social-economy promotion/diffusion gift | 25%; these three social-economy routes share a **3,000** credit cap for either filing mode | 2026 | [Art. 2.12.c/2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |
| Qualifying investment by a new IRPF resident from abroad | 20% of eligible acquisition value including costs/taxes, excluding interest; five-prior-year non-residence, local asset/entity and six-year maintenance conditions | 2026, current asset wording | [Art. 2.15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2) |

Foundation gifts require the Cantabrian domicile, permitted purposes, regional registration, accounts rendered and the protectorate's ordered deposit. Disability-association gifts require Cantabrian domicile, Ley 49/2002 eligibility and the disability-support purpose. The 10% ceiling applies to the **combined base**, not just the regional nominal credit. The first art. 2.13 inflation provision is limited expressly to **2022**; distinguish it from the separately numbered education paragraph, which the consolidated text also labels 13 ([art. 2.4/13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

SME investment covers formation/increase subscriptions in SA, SL or their labour-company equivalents meeting the EU SME definition. Combined capital/votes with spouse and relatives through the **third degree** must not exceed **40% on any day**; hold at least **three years**, with Cantabrian registered/tax offices, genuine activity and the required Cantabrian-resident full-time employee from the first year for formation. Capital increase must be within **three years** of incorporation, add at least one full-time-equivalent employee in the **two following fiscal years** compared with the prior 12 months, and maintain that increase another **24 months**. The investor cannot hold executive/management functions or employment in the entity. Retain a public deed identifying the investment. Preserve the stated entity/ownership/role conditions for three years; breach recaptures the tax saved plus interest ([art. 2.6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

Social-economy recipients need the statutory status/registration, Cantabrian registered/tax offices and annual-average full-time general-regime employment, maintained at least **three years**. Family-through-third-degree participation must not exceed **40%**. Keep the public deed, gift certificate/value evidence and bank-payment proof. Membership equity must remain in the taxpayer's assets at least **five years**, a different clock from the entity-condition period. Do not deduct one amount under several social-economy modalities or combine the same investment with the foundation/Coopera or SME-equity routes; breach recaptures tax and interest ([art. 2.12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).

Inbound-investor assets under the current wording are non-housing/non-tourist-housing real estate **not allocated to an economic activity**, situated in Cantabria, or the specified debt/equity securities of entities with Cantabrian registered/tax offices and genuine activity. For securities, family aggregation is through the **second degree**, capital/votes at most **40% throughout maintenance**, with no executive/management role or employment. The claimant must become an ordinary Cantabria IRPF taxpayer after **five years** without Spanish residence. Invest in the arrival year or next; qualifying Spanish-entity debt/equity can also be bought in the preceding year. Hold for **six years**, permitting an onerous sale only with **full proceeds reinvested within one month**, and keep Cantabria IRPF residence through the final maintenance year. Insufficient-tax amounts have **five following claim years**, starting with arrival instead if the eligible investment preceded it. The article applies to qualifying arrivals from **1 January 2025** and is incompatible with the SME credit for the same investment; breach of residence/holding/full-reinvestment conditions loses the credit. Do not import Madrid's asset geography or family-degree tests into this provision ([art. 2.15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2)).


For the10% burden test, keep a separate denominator `R` and the underlying general/savings income, BI, BL, exempt-income and household-allocation records. **The current statute and retrieved AEAT2025 rental page do not define whether `R` is BI, BL or another net-income measure, or expressly resolve its aggregation on joint filing.** DA1 defines home/family/disability concepts but not this income. Do not import the health-credit `BL−minimum` test or set `R` equal to gross salary. Once the legally applicable `R` is established, eligibility is `eligible_rent > 0.10×R` and credit is `min(0.10×eligible_rent,300 or600)`. At `R=20,000`, rent2,000 fails and2,000.01 passes; these are **conditional comparator controls**, not certification that a particular person's denominator equals20,000. [CurrentCantabria art.2.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [exactAEATrent guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/arrendamiento-vivienda-habitual-jovenes-mayores-discapacidad.html).

### Canarias: common rules and family/education credits

Apply the region's income, asset and tax limits before calculating each credit. Under **art. 17**, `renta` in these regional provisions means **general plus savings BI**, unless the particular provision specifies a different input, such as BL or gross employment income. Personal/family circumstances are normally tested at accrual. Joint filing combines the individual entitlements under art. 2.3, subject to express joint-return limits; it does not create a second entitlement for a non-qualifying person. Art. 41 assimilates qualifying de facto partners to spouses for regional credits, with status provable by a legally admissible means, but expressly excludes that assimilation from joint filing ([arts. 2, 17 and 41](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a41)).

[Ley 9/2025 final provision 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-7560) gives its amendments **effect from 1 January 2025 with indefinite validity**; its general entry date is 1 January 2026. These are the current 2026 credit rules, not a renewal of the older DA 4 inflation measures expressly limited to 2022–2023. For rows below with the ordinary income gate, BI must be **at most 46,455 I / 61,770 J**. Do not apply that gate to a credit whose own article omits it ([arts. 7–16 quinquies and DA 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#da-3)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Higher education outside the home island | 1,800 per eligible student; 1,920 if the claimant's **BL is below 37,062**; aggregate credit limited to 40% of regional gross tax; ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 7.1.a/2–4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a7) |
| Higher education on the same island with qualifying accommodation move | 900 per eligible student, subject to the same 40% regional-tax ceiling and ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 7.1.b/2–4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a7) |
| Non-higher education | Actual eligible costs, combined credit cap 133 for the first qualifying descendant plus 66 for each additional descendant; ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 7 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a7-2) |
| Inter-island work/self-employment move | 300 per qualifying mover in the move year and next year, limited by the regional gross tax attributable to employment/activity income; ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a8) |
| Birth/adoption | First/second child 265; third 530; fourth 796; fifth/subsequent 928; ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 10.1.a/2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a10) |
| Additional birth/adoption disability amount | Disability at least 65%: additional 600 for the first/second disabled child, or 1,100 for the third/subsequent disabled child with the preceding disabled children surviving | 2026 | [Art. 10.1.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a10) |
| Claimant's disability/age | 400 for disability at least 33%, plus 160 for age **over 65** where both apply; ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a11) |
| Foster child | 330 for a full period, prorated by actual cohabitation days for a shorter period | 2026 | [Art. 11 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-2) |
| Single-parent family | One credit of 133; article-specific cohabitation/descendant facts and ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 11 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-3) |
| Authorised nursery custody, under three | 18% of eligible costs, credit cap 530 per descendant; ordinary BI gate and age-out month proration | 2026 | [Art. 12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a12) |
| Large family | General/special category 597 / 796; qualifying at-least-65% disabled spouse/descendant raises these **to** 1,326 / 1,459 | 2026 | [Art. 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a13) |
| Dependent disabled ascendant/descendant | 600 per person with disability at least 65% generating the statutory disability minimum; ordinary BI gate | 2026 | [Art. 16 quater.1–3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-6) |
| Assistance for the qualifying disabled dependant | 20% of the stated household-worker Social Security paid, credit cap 500 per taxpayer, with third-person assistance and the assistance-expense minimum entitlement | 2026 | [Art. 16 quater.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-6) |

Higher-education students must be unmarried, **under 25**, economically dependent, and have own income at most **8,000**. Tutelage/unpaid fostering is assimilated. For study outside the home island, require a full academic course or at least **30 credits**, and no qualifying in-person public offer on the home island. Virtual/distance availability does not by itself trigger that exclusion. The same-island route instead requires moving to rented accommodation, a college hall or student residence in the education centre's municipality or a neighbouring municipality, different from the taxpayer's home municipality. Report the required landlord/hall tax ID and, for a rented home, cadastral identification and annual rent. Apply the article's different conditions to each route, claim in the academic-course start year, use nearest-degree priority and divide between entitled non-joint claimants ([art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a7)).

Non-higher education covers the stated infant, basic and post-compulsory secondary stages and minimum-entitled children/assimilated dependants. Eligible costs include textbooks/materials in any format, transport, uniform and school meals, with invoices. The 133 plus 66 amounts are a combined family calculation, not 133 for every child. Apply nearest-degree priority and equal sharing between entitled claimants. The work move is **between Canary islands**, requires residence in the destination for the move year and **three following years**, but generates the 300 credit only in the first **two years**. Joint filing adds each qualifying mover's amount subject to attributable tax. The attributable-tax worksheet under The base supplies the mixed-income/minimum and base-reduction calculation; breach adds the wrongly used credit and interest under art. 8.3 ([arts. 7 bis–8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a8)).

Birth/adoption ordering counts natural/adopted children cohabiting at accrual, including the specified economically dependent children in specialised institutions. The disability addition has its **own disabled-child order** and requires uninterrupted cohabitation from the event to period end. Split equally where both parents/adopters qualify and do not file jointly. Foster relief covers the emergency/temporary/permanent Civil Code forms, excludes adoption during that period and is divided equally between entitled non-joint claimants after day proration. The single-parent definition excludes unrelated cohabitants other than minimum-entitled ascendants; eligible minor/adult-disabled children have non-exempt annual income at most **8,000**, with the specialised-institution and tutelage/fostering cases included. A changed family situation requires at least **183 days**; a non-entitled cohabiting descendant with income at most 8,000 does not itself disqualify the claimant ([arts. 10–11 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-3)).

Nursery requires authorised opening/operation, cohabitation, custody invoices and reporting the nursery tax ID and amount paid. Divide equally between entitled non-joint claimants and prorate the deduction and child cap by qualifying months in the third-birthday year. Large-family status needs the competent title at accrual and cohabitation; divide between entitled cohabiting claimants. Art. 13 is compatible with birth/adoption relief and does not prescribe the ordinary BI gate. Dependent-disability relief follows the national minimum's sharing/cohabitation rules. For its assistance contribution component, attribute costs to the named employer, except equal attribution between spouses in the community-property regime, and report the worker's NIF/NIE ([arts. 12–13 and 16 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-6)).

### Canarias: housing, rental, health and household costs

Art. 18 imposes three common controls: credits cannot exceed regional gross tax; the **same taxpayer cannot claim more than one regional credit for the same asset**; and qualifying expense bases are reduced by the relevant expense-covering public aid **if that aid is exempt from IRPF**. AEAT expressly applies the same-home exclusion even where acquisition and disability works involve different payments. It is broader than merely barring a second claim for the same invoice. Apply the particular rate/base/credit caps as well ([art. 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a18); [AEAT common limits](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/limites-comunes-obligaciones-formales-aplicar-deducciones.html)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Habitual-home acquisition | BI below 26,035: 5%; BI from 26,035 to **below 46,455**: 3.5%. Under 40: 5.5% / 4% respectively. Annual base cap 6,000 per claimant | 2026 | [Art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a14) |
| Habitual-home energy rehabilitation | 12%; annual base cap 7,000 per taxpayer and credit cap 10% of regional gross tax | 2026 | [Art. 14 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a14bis) |
| Disability home adaptation | 14%, raised to 18% where the disabled beneficiary is over 65; disability at least 65%; annual base cap 15,000 | 2026 | [Art. 14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a14ter) |
| Combined property-credit tax ceiling | Arts. 6, 14, 14 bis and 14 ter together at most **15% of regional gross tax**, in addition to each specific limit | 2026 | [Art. 14 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-4) |
| Habitual-home rent | 24%, annual credit cap 740 per claimant; cap 760 if under 40 or at least 75; ordinary BI gate and rent/BI test below | 2026 | [Art. 15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a15) |
| Qualifying dación-en-pago-linked lease | 25%, annual credit cap 1,200; ordinary BI gate; underlying art. 35 bis operation required | 2026 | [Art. 15 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-7) |
| Preparing a home for habitual-home letting | 10% of specified costs; credit cap 150 per let property for either filing mode, shared by ownership/usufruct percentage | 2026 | [Art. 15 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-8) |
| Landlord's rent-default credit insurance | 75% of eligible premium, annual credit cap 150; qualifying lease, deposit, tax compliance and rent at most 800 monthly | 2026 | [Art. 15 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-9) |
| Bringing a qualifying unused home into habitual-home letting | 1,000 once per property, allocated by ownership/usufruct; at most five qualifying homes and the retention/tenant conditions below | 2026 | [Art. 16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a16) |
| Unemployed claimant | 120; statutory unemployment, gross-work-income and other-BI tests below | 2026 | [Art. 16 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a16bis-2) |
| Eligible medical fees and disability aids | 12% combined; within ordinary BI gate, credit cap 500 I / 700 J; above that gate, credit cap 150 per taxpayer. On an ordinary-gate individual return, the claimant age/disability OR test increases500 once to600; the attained65 implementation interpretation and source-wording distinction are explained below | 2026 | [Art. 16 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-5) |
| General qualifying household-worker contributions | 20%, annual credit cap 500 regardless of worker count; entitlement categories below | 2026 | [Art. 16 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-10) |

Home acquisition uses the terms incorporated from [LIRPF art. 68.1 as effective on 1 January 2012](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20111231&tn=1#a68), with the current regional **6,000** ceiling and rates replacing the old national amounts. Eligible acquisition/rehabilitation financing costs include principal, interest and associated costs under that incorporated rule. Retain the historic habitual-occupation/retention conditions and exceptions, prior credited-home-investment threshold and exempt-reinvestment-gain reduction, rather than treating every mortgage payment as eligible. Construction, rehabilitation and extension must meet the incorporated definitions; the construction route normally has a **four-year** completion period with the statutory exceptional extension route. AEAT requires each joint-return member's own individual BI to select their rate. Use the supported ordinary individual prior-home and State-legacy computation below;the precise joint/mixed-rate and historic eligibility limits remain named ([art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a14); [AEAT home guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/inversion-vivienda-habitual.html)).

Energy rehabilitation must concern an owned habitual home and the specified energy/renewable/water-efficiency works. Require registered before/after energy certificates, invoices and the article's permitted card/transfer/named-cheque/credit-institution-deposit payments; cash does not qualify for this route. Exclude garages, gardens, pools, analogous items, furniture and appliances. A property association certifies costs actually paid and allocates them by ownership quota; apply the statutory equal-sharing provision where more than one taxpayer is entitled. Disability works require a competent certificate of strict accessibility/sensory-communication necessity. The disabled beneficiary can be the claimant or cohabiting spouse/ascendant/descendant; the latter must individually have non-exempt annual income at most **35,735**. For shared ascendants/descendants divide the base ceiling equally, with nearest-degree priority ([arts. 14 bis–14 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a14ter)).

**Acquisition-base and State-legacy interaction.** Calculate art.14 from the claimant's own qualifying acquisition/finance payments, including attributable purchase costs, traced principal, interest net of hedge receipts and qualifying finance costs. Exclude finance used for another purpose. Before the annual cap, apply the incorporated prior-home/reinvestment restriction: let `H` be investment in earlier habitual homes that actually formed a deduction base, plus the gain exempted through reinvestment; let `P` be cumulative qualifying investment already made in the new home before this year; and let `Y` be this year's qualifying new-home payments. The part of this year's investment above the remaining threshold is `E = max(0, Y − max(0, H − P))`. Use the earlier home's **deducted investment base**, not the euro tax saving or the entire earlier house's price when only part was deducted. If the current cumulative investment only reaches `H`, no credit yet arises; the amount above it becomes eligible. Also apply art.18.3's subtraction for expense-covering public aid that is exempt from IRPF, without deducting a reimbursement twice. Keep the payment, historical-threshold and grant records separate ([art.14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a14); [incorporated LIRPF68.1.1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20111231&tn=1#a68); [art.18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a18)).

For an individual claimant, cap the resulting regional annual base at **6,000**, then apply their income/age rate. For example, previously deducted old-home investment20,000 plus exempt reinvestment gain10,000 gives `H=30,000`; previous new-home payments25,000 and current payments12,000 give `E=7,000`, hence a6,000 regional base. A claimant under40 with individual BI30,000 therefore computes240 before quota limits. If previous payments are18,000 instead, the12,000 current payment merely reaches30,000, giving zero eligible base that year. The threshold is crossed once cumulatively; do not subtract30,000 anew every year. A current payment excluded solely by the6,000 annual cap is not an authorised carry-forward ([incorporated68.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20111231&tn=1#a68); [exact AEAT regional base/rates](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/inversion-vivienda-habitual.html)).

The opening **sin perjuicio** in art.14 expressly preserves the regional tranche of State-law home-investment relief. If the claimant independently satisfies [LIRPFDT18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava), calculate that legacy credit separately with its own **9,040** annual base and preserved State/regional percentages; the regional6,000 ceiling does not replace the national9,040 ceiling. Art.18's same-asset exclusion applies to the Canary credits **in that chapter**, so it does not cancel the expressly preserved State-law legacy tranche. It still prevents the same claimant from stacking Canary acquisition, energy or disability-home credits on the same home, even on different invoices. Art.14quater's **15% of regional gross quota** ceiling applies to the listed Canary property credits, not to the State-law legacy tranche. Apply the legacy regional tranche and other applicable settlement reductions, and then limit the allowable Canary credit to available regional tax as well as its own15% gross-quota limit ([arts.14,14quater,18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-4); [AEAT same-asset interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/limites-comunes-obligaciones-formales-aplicar-deducciones.html)).

For a single under40 claimant with BI30,000,12,000 payments independently eligible for both routes, no threshold/grant adjustment and regional gross quota1,000, with at least678 of available State quota after earlier deductions: State-law legacy relief is678 State plus678 regional; Canary nominal relief is240, reduced to150 by the15% gross-quota cap. Regional credits are678+150=828, leaving172; total home relief including the State678 is1,506. With regional gross quota700 instead, the Canary gross-quota cap is105 but only22 regional tax remains after678, so the Canary amount is22. These controls assume established legacy entitlement and do not confer DT18 status on a new2026 purchase.


This supplies the ordinary individual computation and independently established State coexistence. For separate returns each claimant uses their own supported payments and6,000 ceiling. It does not double a joint-return ceiling or establish a favourable priority between contributors with different age/income rates where their combined joint expenditure exceeds the unchanged quantitative ceiling. National joint9040 handling remains as already stated in the Guide. Historic cohort entitlement, refinancings used for mixed purposes and all pre-existing occupation/construction conditions still require their own evidence. A payment exceeding an annual cap does not become next year's new payment.


The ordinary rent condition is rent **strictly exceeding 10% of BI after deducting the relevant rental subsidy from that comparison income**, as AEAT explains. Separately reduce the credit's expense base for exempt expense-covering aid under art. 18.3. The home must satisfy art. 15's **more-than-one-year residence** definition; retain the named tenancy, landlord NIF, cadastral identity and annual rent. A non-tenant spouse does not acquire a credit merely through joint filing. The dación route requires the actual art. 35 bis financial-creditor enforcement transfer and continued habitual occupation under a qualifying lease with purchase option, with at least a **five-year** contractual term; it is not available for every debt settlement or ordinary lease ([arts. 15–15 bis and 35 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a3-3); [AEAT rent guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/alquiler-vivienda-habitual.html)).

Letting-preparation costs include qualifying repair/conservation/necessary preparation, lease formalisation, damage/default insurance and energy certificates, supported by invoices. The lease must satisfy LAU art. 2; report repair/conservation provider IDs and annual amounts. The insurance route additionally requires at least a **one-year** lease with the same tenant, the regional security-deposit lodging, declared property-rental income, current tax compliance, tenant ID and cadastral reference. Art. 15 ter expressly bars using the same premiums under both routes; the art. 18 same-asset rule also applies ([arts. 15 ter–15 quater and 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a18)).

The 1,000 market-entry credit requires imputed-property-income status for the **whole preceding period**, or a newly acquired previously unlet property brought into qualifying letting within **six months** of acquisition. Letting must not be an economic activity. It is **once per property** in the first letting period, with three years' effective letting, no spouse/relative through the third degree as tenant and at most **five** homes, excluding garages/storerooms. Each co-owned property counts as one towards that five-home limit regardless of the ownership percentage. A replacement lease within **six months** preserves relief if effective lease periods total at least three years. Breach recaptures the credit plus interest ([art. 16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a16)).

Unemployment relief requires benefits received, legal unemployment for **more than six months**, gross employment income **above 15,876 and at most 22,000**, and general plus savings BI excluding its employment-income part at most **1,600**. Do not apply the general BI gate or gross-pay bands from another credit. Health relief covers the stated medical/health-professional services, excluding pharmacists, and qualifying corrective devices including prescribed glasses/contact lenses. Exclude insurance premiums, reimbursable treatment and aesthetic-only services except the statutory accident/intervention repair and gender-identity treatments. Keep invoices, payer allocation, provider IDs and annual amounts; AEAT attributes community-property payments equally between spouses and otherwise requires proof of the payer. The two health expense categories share the cap; the **150 higher-income cap is not zero relief** ([arts. 16 bis–16 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-5); [AEAT health guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/gasto-enfermedad.html)).

**Medical-credit cap and claimant status.** For the medical credit, determine the claimant's age and disability at accrual, ordinarily31December. Within the ordinary combined-BI gate, calculate `min(12% × combined eligible net expense base, cap)`. The cap is500 on an individual return, increased **once by100 to600** if the claimant meets the age condition discussed below or has certified disability of at least65%. This is an **OR** test: age alone qualifies, and disability of at least65% qualifies below65. Meeting both conditions does not create two increments. An older or disabled minimum-entitled relative can generate qualifying medical expenditure but does not confer the cap increase on a younger, non-disabled claimant. A joint return uses700; the individual100 increase is not added to it. Above the ordinary income gate, apply the separate150 cap rather than adding100 to it. Eligible medical fees and corrective-appliance expenses consume one combined cap. Using the attained65 operational interpretation below, for an individual claimant with30,000 combined BI and10,000 eligible net expenses, the nominal1,200 gives600 if the claimant is65 with no disability,600 if age40 with65% disability, and600 if both age65 and65% disabled. At age64 with no qualifying disability it gives500. Keep the Guide's payer, invoice, exempt matching-aid, expense-eligibility and quota limitations. ([current art16ter and accrual rule](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-5); [Ley9/2025 DF11.15](https://sede.gobiernodecanarias.org/boc/boc-a-2025-256-4414.pdf); [exact AEAT10.5.20 implementation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_5-canarias/10_5_20-gastos-enfermedad.html)).

**Age wording and observed implementation.** Art.16ter and AEAT's2025 instructions say “mayor de 65 años”. In the public [RentaWEBOPEN2025 simulator](https://www2.agenciatributaria.gob.es/wlpl/PARE-RW25/OPEN/index.zul?EJER=2025&TACCESO=COLAB), a fictional claimant born31December1960 with no disability received600 at31December2025; a claimant born1January1961 received500. An independent reviewer separately reproduced the exact65/no-disability600 result. The attained65 treatment used here is an **inference for2026 from that2025 implementation** of the unchanged rule, rather than an observed2026 return campaign. [Ley9/2025 DF11 opening and paragraph15](https://sede.gobiernodecanarias.org/boc/boc-a-2025-256-4414.pdf) make the current provision effective from1January2025 indefinitely.

General household relief requires the habitual employer home and the specified household-system contributions. Qualify through a descendant-minimum entitlement plus work/activity income, age **at least 75**, or age **over 65** plus physical/organic/sensory disability at least **65%** or the stated cognitive/psychosocial/intellectual/developmental disability at least **33%**. Attribute payments to the employer, except equal attribution between community-property spouses, and report the employee's NIF/NIE. The same-worker/same-payment interaction with dependent-disability assistance is supplied by the expressly labelled statutory interpretation below ([arts. 16 quater–16 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-10)).

**Same worker and payment: labelled two-credit statutory interpretation.** Where the same claimant independently qualifies for both art.16quater.4 and16quinquies, compute each contribution credit on that claimant's own qualifying paid contributions, applying its separate20%rate and500annual cap. This is a combined-text compatibility inference: [the current two articles](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a1-6) each grant the amount, use matching employer/gananciales attribution, and contain neither mutual exclusion nor a rule making one contribution base residual after the other. Art.17defines income references; it does not impose a shared contribution ceiling. Art.18.1still limits aggregate regional credits to regional gross tax, and18.3reduces each affected expense base for matching exempt public aid. Art.18.2's same-*asset* restriction is not a universal same-payment ban; a household employee or disabled dependant is not an asset to which that property-credit rule can simply be transferred. This method does not remove that rule for credits actually concerning the same asset.

The exact [AEAT assistance instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/familiares-dependientes-discapacidad.html) and [household-contribution instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/cuotas-satisfechas-seguridad-social-contratacion-hogar.html) separately reproduce20%/500and matching payment attribution, without prescribing cross-article mutual exclusion or base sharing. Their express compatibility statement for the fixed600dependent amount plus art.16quater.4concerns those two components only; it is not an express agency ruling about16quinquies. The two-contribution-credit method here therefore remains labelled as our reading of the actual statutory grants and common limits, not an expressly printed same-remittance worked ruling or an observed2026filing-software result. No general anti-double-credit rule or forced50:50split is invented.
