**Binding single-employer ledger.** In2026an individually filing, non-ganancial claimant has combined general/savings BI30,000and qualifying employment income, and is the sole person entitled to the descendant and disability/assistance minima for a cohabiting descendant with certified disability of at least65%and documented need for third-person assistance. All minimum, cohabitation and other entitlement conditions pass. The taxpayer is the sole registered household employer; the same identified worker performs the qualifying household/assistance work in the employer's habitual home. Actual TGSS/payment records establish **3,000of contributions independently eligible under both articles**, with no matching exempt aid. This is one paid-contribution ledger, not two payments or an estimated annual wage amount. Retain employer registration, the worker's NIF/NIE, contribution and bank records, habitual-home/work evidence and the minimum/disability/assistance documents.

Art.16quater.4gives `min(20% × 3,000,500)=500`; art.16quinquies independently gives `min(20% × 3,000,500)=500`. Both caps bind: the combined contribution credit is **1,000**. A compulsory split of that one3,000payment into1,500perroute would instead give300+300=600and is not the selected statutory method. This is not a second500allowance for another worker within either article. Separately, the claimant on these facts qualifies for the existing fixed600dependent credit; AEAT expressly permits that amount alongside its assistance component. With regional gross tax2,000, no other deductions and all stated conditions, the ledger is600fixed+500assistance+500general contributions=**1,600**, leaving**400**regional tax. Do not separately allow each credit against the same remaining tax and thereby exceed art.18.1. If either contribution route's own eligibility fails, that route is absent; eligibility for the other is tested independently. Ganancial payment attribution remains exactly as stated above; this example does not allocate a whole3,000base to each spouse or invent a general rule for other credit pairs.

### Canarias: donations, investment and historical relief

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ecological cash donation | 10%, limited to both 10% of regional gross tax and 150 | 2026 | [Art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a3) |
| Qualifying heritage-conservation donation | 20%, limited to both 10% of regional gross tax and 150 | 2026 | [Art. 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a4) |
| Cultural/sport/research/education donation or qualifying technology capital contribution | 15%, credit limited to 5% of regional gross tax; statutory **base** caps 50,000 for the listed public/university/technology routes or 3,000 for the listed small cultural/scientific companies | 2026 | [Art. 4 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a4-2) |
| Ley 49/2002 nonprofit donation | 20% of first 150 of eligible base plus 15% of the rest, or 17.5% of the rest for qualifying recurring gifts; base at most 10% of BL | 2026 | [Art. 4 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a4-3) |
| Owner's qualifying heritage-property restoration/rehabilitation/repair | 10%, limited to 10% of regional gross tax and the combined art. 14 quater ceiling | 2026 | [Art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a6) |
| Ordinary new/recent SA/SL equity | 20%, annual credit cap 4,000 | 2026 | [Art. 9.1–3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a9) |
| University/research-created or participated company equity | 30%, annual credit cap 6,000 | 2026 | [Art. 9.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a9) |
| Labour SA/SL or cooperative subscriptions | 30%, annual credit cap 6,000, with the specified formation-employee exception | 2026 | [Art. 9.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a9) |

Ecological gifts require the listed Canary public/environment bodies or registered Ley 49/2002 bodies with the exclusive environmental purpose. Heritage gifts require the prescribed regional listing/property and eligible public, cooperating religious or qualifying nonprofit recipient. Cultural/scientific company routes require equity **below 300,000**, the listed activity and eligible purpose; the 50,000 routes have their own recipient/project categories. Obtain art. 5's donor/recipient IDs, date, amount/value, purpose, acceptance and irrevocability certificate, with the public project identity or additional competent-department certificate for a private project. A revoked donation recaptures relief and interest. Art. 4 ter is additional to the national donation credit but incompatible with arts. 3/4 bis for the same contributions to the same recipient. Recurring-gift history must satisfy the two preceding years' non-decreasing same-entity test. Its first **150** band is the current regional statute, not automatically the national donation band's amount. Non-cash bases use Ley 49/2002's definitions; detailed in-kind valuation remains to confirm. Owner heritage works need the prescribed BIC status and competent works authorisation ([arts. 3–6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a5)).

Equity relief requires formation/capital-increase subscriptions, family-through-third-degree capital/votes no more than **40% on any day**, no investor executive/management functions, at least **three years** holding, Canary registered/tax offices and genuine activity rather than principal asset management. Formation normally needs at least one full-time general-regime employee from its first fiscal year. That formation-employee condition is excepted for the labour/cooperative route; its other conditions continue. Capital increases must be within **three years** of incorporation, increase average employment by at least one full-time equivalent in the **two following fiscal years** over the prior 12-month average and maintain the increase another **24 months**. AEAT treats the three investment categories' caps as **independent**, unlike the Balearic combined cap; each entitled joint-return investor applies their own limits. The same share cannot receive both art. 9 and the technology-capital route in art. 4 bis.f, though distinct shares can follow separate routes. Retain distinct investment and employment schedules ([art. 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a9); [AEAT investment interpretation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/inversion-adquisicion-acciones-participaciones-sociales.html)).

DA 1's volcanic-event personal/desarraigo/free-use/health credits expressly identify **2021–2022** or **2021** periods; DA 4's inflation supplements identify **2022–2023**. Their presence in the current consolidation does not establish a new 2026 claim. Separately, [DL 12/2021 DA 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2021-19374) preserves arts. 14/14 bis/14 ter home-credit rights where lava destroyed the habitual home and permits continuing eligible payments to generate the corresponding credit. Do not confuse that preservation with renewing every temporary volcanic-event credit ([DL 1/2009 DA 1/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#pr)).


### Castilla y León: income tests, family and disability credits

**Check each credit's income test before calculating it.** The default test is `general BI + savings BI − the state personal/family minimum ≤ 18,900` on an individual return or `≤ 31,500` jointly. Equality qualifies. This test does **not** apply to family/birth/adoption credits in arts. 3–4, subsidised home rehabilitation in art. 7.2, rural rental rehabilitation in art. 7.3, entrepreneurship in art. 8, research gifts in art. 9.f or electric cars in art. 9.g. Do not replace this measure with salary or BL, or impose it on an exempted category ([DL 1/2013 art. 10.1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a10)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Large family | 600 generally; 1,500 with four minimum-entitled descendants; 2,500 with five, plus 1,000 for each descendant from the sixth | 2026 | [Art. 3.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a3) |
| Large-family disability addition | Add 600 to the applicable general/four-/five-child amount if a spouse or minimum-entitled descendant has disability at least 65%; this is one addition, not 600 for every disabled relative | 2026 | [Art. 3.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a3) |
| Birth/adoption | Per qualifying child: 1,010 first / 1,475 second / 2,351 third and later | 2026 | [Art. 4.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a4) |
| Rural birth/adoption | Replace ordinary amounts with 1,420 / 2,070 / 3,300 where the taxpayer resides in a municipality or minor local entity with no more than 5,000 inhabitants | 2026 | [Art. 4.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a4) |
| Birth/adoption disability | Double the applicable ordinary or rural amount if the child has recognised disability at least 33%; later recognition before age five triggers the statutory corresponding deduction in the recognition year | 2026 | [Art. 4.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a4) |
| Multiple births/adoptions | Additional 50% of the corresponding birth/adoption deduction for two children, 100% for three or more; an additional 901 in each of the following two years | 2026 events and continuing entitlement | [Art. 4.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a4) |
| Adoption expenses | 784 generally, replaced by 3,625 for qualifying international adoption; claim in the Civil Registry inscription year | 2026 | [Art. 4.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a4) |
| Work-related childcare: choose one route | Domestic carer: 30% of eligible payments, cap 322; **or** registered regional infant-school/nursery costs: 100%, cap 1,320, subject to the state-increment and public-aid adjustments below | 2026 | [Art. 5.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a5) |
| Household-worker Social Security | 15% of contributions actually paid, cap 300; minimum-entitled child under four at accrual | 2026 | [Art. 5.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a5) |
| Taxpayer disability | 300 if under 65 with disability at least 65%, or aged at least 65 with disability at least 33%; **656 instead** if aged at least 65 with disability at least 65% | 2026 | [Art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a6) |

For arts. 3–5, prorate the statutory entitlement where more than one taxpayer is entitled. Large-family status requires cohabitation and the regional title under Ley 40/2003. Birth/adoption requires the descendant minimum. Count the new child and other natural/adopted children of either parent living with the taxpayer at accrual when fixing child order. For multiple events, births/adoptions within twelve months can qualify even if not simultaneous. The 901 is the additional event entitlement shared by entitled taxpayers, not an automatic 901 per child; count the two following years from the last birth/adoption in the qualifying event ([arts. 3–5 and 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a10); [AEAT multiple-event explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/partos-adopciones-multiples.html)). The disability credit excludes users of the community's public or publicly contracted residences. Retain competent-authority disability evidence.

Childcare requires a child under four at accrual who generates the descendant minimum, and qualifying work plus Social Security/Mutuality registration by the minimum-entitled parents. A domestic carer must be registered in the household-worker system. Nursery costs cover pre-enrolment, enrolment, ordinary/extended attendance and meals for complete months at a qualifying registered regional centre. If the claimant has entitlement to the national art. 81.2 maternity childcare increment, subtract that entitlement from both the regional nursery amount and its 1,320 ceiling, with no negative credit. Across both parents, regional childcare credits plus that national increment and relevant public childcare aid cannot exceed actual eligible expense; reduce regional claims proportionately if necessary ([art. 5.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a5)).

For attribution, AEAT directs equal allocation of joint marital-property expenditure; in separate-property or unmarried cases use the claimant's actual payments. Its same-wording guidance deducts nursery grants from the nursery expense base, but does not automatically deduct a domestic-carer grant from the wage base where credit plus grant remains within actual cost. Apply the statutory sharing rule and aggregate expense ceiling as well; do not give each parent an unshared duplicate claim ([AEAT childcare guidance, updated 29 September 2026](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/cuidado-hijos-menores.html)).

Arts. 4–5 exclude analogous birth/adoption/childcare/conciliation aid requested and granted by the Junta. Classify the actual aid before denying a credit: AEAT expressly treats **Bono Concilia under Orden FAM/882/2021** and **working-time-reduction/leave aid under Orden FAM/1454/2023** as compatible with the childcare credit and excludes those amounts from its expense-ceiling calculation. By contrast, analogous Bono Nacimiento can bar birth/multiple-birth credits for the same triggering event. The guidance distinguishes simultaneous births from successive births and earlier-year benefits; a benefit for an earlier event does not automatically bar a later event's credit ([art. 10.3.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a10); [childcare](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/cuidado-hijos-menores.html), [multiple births](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/partos-adopciones-multiples.html)).

### Castilla y León: housing, investment and gifts

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Young first-home acquisition/rehabilitation in rural area | 15% of eligible payments; annual **base** cap 10,000. Under 36 at accrual; qualifying first new home or statutory rehabilitation acquired/rehabilitated from 1 January 2023 | 2026 | [Art. 7.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a7) |
| Subsidised habitual-home rehabilitation | 15% of eligible energy, sustainability or disability-access investments; **base** cap 20,000; claim in the year the approved housing-programme subsidy is paid | 2026 receipt year | [Art. 7.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a7) |
| Rural rehabilitation for letting | 15% of eligible actual rehabilitation payments; **base** cap 20,000; qualifying rural/value test, five-year rental/availability conditions and rent no more than 300 a month | 2026 | [Art. 7.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a7) |
| Young habitual-home tenant | Under 36: 20% of actual rent, cap 459; qualifying rural home: **25%, cap 612 instead**. In either route the credit cannot exceed actual rent minus all public rent aid | 2026 | [Art. 7.4–5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a7) |
| Entrepreneurship subscriptions | 20% of qualifying formation/capital-increase investment in an SA, SL or labour company; **credit** cap 10,000; acquisition between 0.5% and 45% of capital, held at least three years | 2026 | [Art. 8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a8) |
| Heritage/nature work and qualifying gifts | 15% of art. 9.a–f expenditure/gifts; the **combined bases** of those six routes cannot exceed 10% of BL | 2026 | [Arts. 9.a–f and 10.3.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a9) |
| New electric passenger car | 15% of qualifying cost; **credit** cap 4,000 per vehicle, shared between buyers and reduced by national DA 58 deduction entitlement. Acquisition value including taxes no more than 40,000 | 2026 registration year | [Art. 9.g](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a9) |

For the first-home and rural rental-rehabilitation routes, the municipality/minor local entity at acquisition or rehabilitation must have no more than 10,000 inhabitants generally, or no more than 3,000 if less than 30 km from the provincial capital; the property's acquisition-tax value must be **strictly below 150,000**. The young rural tenant uses the same population/distance limits but not that property-value condition. Use the relevant year's official population/list evidence, not a 2025 manual's list for a 2026 claim; final provision 7 uses the INE register in force at 1 January. First-home status excludes anyone who has ever held full ownership of at least 50% of another home. New construction means the first transfer within three years of the declaration of new construction, or qualifying direct construction costs. Apply the national habitual-home definition ([arts. 7 and 10.2; final provision 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#dfseptima)).

The young first-home base includes qualifying acquisition expenses, loan principal, interest and financing costs, with mortgage-hedge receipts reducing interest. The net-wealth growth test excludes interest/financing costs from the investment comparison and excludes value movements in assets still held at year end. For art. 7.1/7.3 rehabilitation, apply the incorporated VAT definition: qualifying reconstruction work must represent more than 50% of project cost and total work must exceed 25% of the relevant acquisition/market value excluding land, using the statutory two-year acquisition lookback and connected-work rules ([art. 7.1/6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a7); [LIVA art. 20.Uno.22.B](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a20)).

Subsidised rehabilitation covers solar hot-water panels meeting at least 50% of the minimum technical contribution, thermal-system energy/renewable improvements, specified water-saving/sanitation works and disability access/sensory-communication adaptations for the taxpayer, spouse or an occupying relative through the third degree. It requires an approved state/regional housing programme; keep the managing body's bank-transfer proof of the grant. The statutory claim year is the grant-payment year. For rural rental rehabilitation, maintain qualifying letting for five following years to non-spouse/non-ascendant/non-descendant/non-third-degree relatives, lodge the legal deposit and satisfy the 300 monthly ceiling, including contractually recharged housing expenses. During vacancies, advertise through the property, media/Internet, estate agency or agent and keep the property unoccupied with utility evidence; AEAT identifies Orden EYH/668/2019 for these conditions ([arts. 7.2–3 and 10.4.e](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a7); [AEAT rural letting](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/rehabilitacion-viviendas-medio-rural-destinadas-alquiler.html)). For a young tenant, the aid rule caps the **resulting credit** at net rent: `min(rate × rent paid, route cap, max(0, rent paid − public rent aid))`; it does not simply change the percentage base to net rent.

Entrepreneurship requires a financed investment project in Castilla y León and the qualifying company's regional registered/tax domicile described by AEAT. In the investment year or the next, increase employees measured as persons/year, economically dependent self-employed contracts and/or family-collaborator registrations against the prior-year comparison, and maintain the corresponding increase for three years. The maximum computable **project investment**, distinct from the taxpayer's 10,000 credit ceiling, is `100,000 × added annual employee units + 50,000 × additional qualifying dependent-contractor contracts + 50,000 × additional family-collaborator registrations`. Obtain the investee's certificate of destination, ownership percentage and employment conditions. A capital-only intermediary must put funds into a qualifying regional company within six months; apply the aggregate capital-percentage test, employment conditions and no reduction of the regional workforce ([art. 8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a8); [AEAT investment conditions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/fomento-emprendimiento.html)).

Reconcile **all investors' qualifying subscriptions** to the certified project maximum once,not once per investor. If aggregate eligible financing stays within that maximum,each entitled taxpayer receives `min(20%×their own qualifying subscriptions,10,000)`,aggregating that taxpayer's investments before the personal cap;the same personal cap applies on joint filing. One added annual employee unit gives a100,000project maximum:subscriptions30,000/20,000yield6,000/4,000;60,000/40,000yield10,000/8,000. At90,000/30,000,total financing exceeds100,000,and the cited law/instructions do not prescribe a priority or proportionate removal of excess:retain that precise allocation question,rather than invent a split or reject every investor ([art8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a8);[exactAEATcalculation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_8-castilla-leon/10_8_10-deduccion-fomento-emprendimiento.html)).

The art. 9 gift/work routes are: **(a)** owner-paid authorised work on eligible protected regional heritage buildings meeting the incorporated heritage conditions; **(b)** authorised/favourably reported work by owners on regional Natura 2000 natural assets; **(c)** heritage-conservation gifts to eligible public, religious or Law 49/2002 heritage-purpose bodies; **(d)** regional Natura 2000 gifts to public bodies/institutions; **(e)** gifts to registered regional foundations with cultural, assistance or ecological purposes; **(f)** gifts to regional public universities or predominantly research/development/business-innovation institutions for qualifying regional projects. Obtain the required authorisations and donation certificates. The default BI-minus-minimum ceiling applies to a–e, but not f; the combined 10%-of-BL base cap applies to **all a–f** ([arts. 9–10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a9)).

The vehicle must be a **new passenger car** that is pure electric, range-extended electric or plug-in hybrid with electric range **over 40 km under WLTP**. It cannot be used in anyone's business/professional activity; claim only in its registration year and retain it for four years. Subtract the claimant's actual national DA 58 entitlement from the regional cap before applying the cap, with no negative deduction; do not assume that a national headline maximum is the actual entitlement. Retain the invoice proving acquisition, date and price. Later loss of art. 7.1–3/8/9.g conditions requires regional recapture plus late-payment interest in the breach year ([arts. 9.g and 10.2.j/3.d/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a10)).

### Castilla y León: preserved housing and old family balances

DL 1/2013's repealing provision expressly preserves **DT 5 of DL 1/2008**, introduced by Ley 1/2012 art. 11. That separate first-new-home credit is **7.5%**, annual base **9,040**, for a regional habitual home whose new-construction project approval was obtained **1 September 2011–31 December 2012**. It runs only in the first-payment year and the next four years, not indefinitely for an old mortgage. Include qualifying acquisition/financing payments net of hedge receipts; apply the statutory previous-deduction/reinvestment exclusions. It covers acquisition/construction, not enlargement or rehabilitation. A 2026 claim requires evidence that 2026 falls inside the particular five-year window; old project approval alone is insufficient ([DL 1/2013 repealing provision, paragraph 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#ddunica); [Ley 1/2012 art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2012-4385); [AEAT preserved credit](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/adquisicion-vivienda-nueva-construccion-residencia-habitual.html)).

**Preserved/current home calculations remain separate.** Ley1/2023's transition preserves earlier art7.1 rights under their entitlement-year requirements,with their annual base increased to10,000. Do not reject an otherwise valid pre2023continuing claim solely because the new-cohort headline starts2023. Calculate current/preserved art7.1 as15%of its eligible annual payments,base at most10,000,and independently survivingDT5as7.5%of its own eligible payments,base at most9,040,within its first-payment-year-plus-four window. The operative provisions give no cross-credit base offset or common home-investment cap. If the same documented home/payments independently satisfy both routes,add their separately capped credits,subject to available regional quota; this is statutory analysis,not a claimed official combined example. At12,000eligible payments,first payment2023and all independent conditions met,the credits are1,500+678=2,178;at6,000they are900+450=1,350. If only art7.1's income test fails,retain the independently eligibleDT5amount;its statutory requirements do not import that chapter's income ceiling ([Ley1/2023transition](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2023-6456);[currentart7/repealingprovision](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#a7);[preservedDT5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2012-4385);[AEATDT5calculation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_8-castilla-leon/10_8_9-adquisicion-vivienda-nueva-construccion.html)).

Keep nationalDT18entitlement separate. Ordinary same-home first acquisition/construction payments before2013already exhaust DT5's five-year window before2026,so do not addDT5to every national legacy claim. An independently qualifying continuing ruralart7.1claim can still combine its10,000base with the national transition's own9,040base:at12,000independently eligible payments,1,500rural+1,356national total=2,856,before quota limits. A2021first payment endsDT5in2025;a2022first payment can endin2026. This does not reconstruct every historic cohort or grant eligibility without its acquisition-year evidence ([LIRPFDT18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava);the preserved and regional provisions above).

Do not grant a new refundable/carry-forward family credit for 2026 by copying the former additional provision: that provision was repealed from **1 January 2023**. The surviving transitional provision preserves specified **2019–2022** art. 3–5 balances for three following periods, then a regional payment-request route. A 2022 balance's last return-offset year was **2025**, not 2026. A payment request submitted in calendar 2026 concerns that old entitlement and is distinct from the 2026 return. The 2025 manual applies current-year credits before the old balance; it does not create a 2026 carry right ([DL 1/2013 transitional provision](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCL-h-2013-90254#dt); [AEAT ordering](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-castilla-leon/orden-aplicacion-deducciones-sobre-cuota-autonomica.html)).


### Extremadura: 2026 income gates, family and care

**Apply the 2026 income limits, then test the rural exception.** Ley 2/2026 final provisions 2 and 7 apply the amended IRPF rules from **1 January 2026**. For the credits identified below, the ordinary income ceiling is `general BI + savings BI ≤ 30,000 individual / 55,000 joint`. The 2025 manual's 19,000/24,000 and 28,000/45,000 limits are not the 2026 limits. Art. 12 bis waives the BI ceilings for **arts. 3, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11 ter and 11 quater only** if the taxpayer habitually resides in a municipality/minor local entity with **fewer than 3,000 inhabitants** and is either a legal large-family member or a legally separated/unmarried ascendant with two children, no maintenance entitlement and the whole descendant minimum. This is not a blanket waiver for every credit: new art. 3 bis birth/adoption and art. 11 nonies veterinary relief retain their own ceilings ([DL 1/2018 art. 12 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-14); [Ley 2/2026](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-17839)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dependent employment | 75 if gross employment income no more than 12,000 and the sum of other net income, capital gains/losses and imputed income no more than 300 | 2026 | [Art. 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a2) |
| Multiple birth/adoption | 300 per child born/adopted in the period; ordinary BI ceiling, subject to art. 12 bis exception | 2026 | [Art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a3) |
| Birth/adoption | 500 first child / 1,000 second / 1,200 third and later in the event year and each of the following two years; BI no more than 30,000 individual / 55,000 joint; compatible with multiple-event credit | 2026-effective entitlement | [Art. 3 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a3-12) |
| Foster children | 250 per child for at least 183 cohabitation days; 125 for more than 90 but fewer than 183; qualifying temporary/permanent/emergency fostering | 2026 | [Art. 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a4) |
| Care of disabled ascendant/descendant | 150 per qualifying person; **220 instead** if a dependency benefit is recognised but still not actually received at 31 December | 2026 | [Art. 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a5) |
| Work-related childcare through age 14 | 10% of qualifying costs, cap **400 per family unit**; age at accrual no more than 14; ordinary BI ceiling/limited rural exception | 2026 | [Art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a6) |
| Widowhood | 100; **200 instead** with a minimum-entitled descendant having no income of any kind; ordinary BI ceiling/limited rural exception; incompatible with art. 2 employment credit | 2026 | [Art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a7) |
| School materials | 30 per cohabiting descendant of compulsory-school age who generates the descendant minimum; ordinary BI ceiling/limited rural exception | 2026 | [Art. 10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-2) |
| ALS diagnosis | 4,000 for the diagnosed taxpayer or qualifying spouse/partner/ascendant/descendant route, shared equally between entitled taxpayers for the same person | 2026 | [Art. 11 octies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-21) |
| ALS regional grant | Credit linked to the statutory average rates applied to the grant included in BL; only a qualifying regional ALS grant actually included in general BI | 2026 | [Art. 11 septies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-20) |
| Veterinary services | 30% of eligible actual paid costs, cap 100 per return, increased to 200 for assistance-dog-linked costs; BI no more than 30,000 individual / 55,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 11 nonies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-22) |

Birth/multiple-event relief requires cohabitation and entitlement to the national descendant minimum; divide equally when several cohabiting taxpayers qualify. Split a married couple's foster entitlement on individual returns, and split the qualifying registered-partner fostering entitlement. The additional provision preserves specified simple/permanent/pre-adoptive fostering continuing from the former legislation; do not silently substitute a new adoption credit for an old foster entitlement ([arts. 3–4 and additional provision](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#da-5)).

Disabled-relative care requires disability at least 65% or the statutory judicial/representative-support status; uninterrupted cohabitation for at least half the tax period, certified by competent public services; and the relative's general/savings income, **including exempt income**, no more than twice annual IPREM, with no legal obligation to file Wealth Tax. At the same kinship degree, share equally; the closer degree takes priority. If only one cohabiting claimant meets the BI ceiling, that claimant takes the full credit. The 2025 AEAT guidance measures twice IPREM as `2 × 8,400 = 16,800`; the preserved state IPREM amounts and the 2026 budget extension support that continuing monetary benchmark, while the taxpayer's own BI ceiling is the separately amended 2026 figure ([art. 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a5); [AEAT disabled-relative guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-extremadura/cuidado-familiares-discapacidad.html); [state IPREM provision](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2022-22128#da-90), [2026 budget extension](https://www.sepg.pap.hacienda.gob.es/sitios/sepg/es-ES/Presupuestos/PGE/PGE2025Prorroga/Paginas/PGE2025Prorroga.aspx)).

Childcare requires work and relevant Social Security/Mutuality registration by both parents and entitlement to the descendant minimum. Eligible care includes a registered household worker or authorised nursery, leisure centre, urban camp, sports centre, play centre or similar facility; retain invoices for centre costs. Share entitlement for the same descendants and respect the 400 **family-unit** cap. Widowhood relief excludes a taxpayer convicted by final judgment of gender violence against the deceased spouse ([arts. 6–7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a6)).

For the fixed ALS credit, the spouse/registered partner must cohabit or be dependent on the claimant; an ascendant/descendant must generate the corresponding minimum. This is separate from the grant-neutralisation credit, whose mixed general/savings, minimum-spillover and base-reduction method is supplied in the attributable-tax worksheet under The base, with its precise signed-income boundary. Veterinary costs require statutory pet ownership or the qualifying assistance-dog user/legal-representative relationship, an authorised provider and an invoice in the taxpayer's name. Cover health-related consultations, vaccination, parasite treatment, diagnostic tests, surgery, medicines, hospitalisation, emergencies and specialist care; exclude merely aesthetic, recreational or accessory expenditure without a health need. The 200 ceiling is not added to a separate 100 ceiling ([arts. 11 septies–nonies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-22)).

### Extremadura: home purchase, tenants and landlords

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Young first habitual home / terrorism-victim route | 6% of eligible protected-home acquisition/rehabilitation payments **excluding interest**; 10% in a municipality/minor local entity below 3,000, without protected-home classification requirement there; incorporated annual acquisition base ceiling 9,040 | 2026 | [Art. 8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a8), [historical LIRPF art. 68.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68) |
| Habitual-home rent | 30% of actual rent, cap 1,000; qualifying rural home below 3,000 inhabitants: **1,500 cap instead** | 2026 | [Art. 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a9) |
| Letting a previously empty home | 100% of declared **reduced net property income**, cap 1,200 per taxpayer, while the conditions remain met | 2026 | [Art. 9 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a9-8) |
| Rural rehabilitation for letting | 15%; annual **base** cap 9,040 per return; total base per taxpayer/property limited to the lower of 180,000 and eligible rehabilitation cost, excluding recognised public aid | 2026 | [Art. 9 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a9-9) |
| Rural habitual-home acquisition/rehabilitation | 10%; annual **base** cap 9,040; total base limited to 180,000 or qualifying property cost if lower, reduced by regional acquisition/rehabilitation grants and relevant previous-home/reinvestment amounts | 2026 | [Art. 11 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-12) |
| Rural residence | 15% of regional gross tax for habitual residence in a municipality/minor local entity below 3,000 inhabitants; ordinary BI ceiling/limited rural-family exception | 2026 | [Art. 11 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-13) |
| Young first-home mortgage interest | 25% of eligible interest with a **base** cap 1,000, hence maximum ordinary credit 250; under 36 at accrual, first habitual home and ordinary BI ceiling/limited rural-family exception | 2026 | [Art. 11 quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-15) |

Art. 8 requires regional residence, first-home status, normally age under 36 at accrual, the statutory protected-home status outside the qualifying rural route and its BI test. A terrorism victim, or in statutory priority the qualifying spouse/partner or cohabiting children, uses the same relief without the age ceiling; this is not a second simultaneous art. 8 credit. Apply the acquisition/habitual-home/rehabilitation and wealth-growth rules incorporated as effective **31 December 2012**, but use the regional 6%/10% and its express interest exclusion. The historical BOE revision published 28 December 2012 already displays the repeal effective January 2013; the linked October revision preserves the relevant pre-repeal home text. Current national abolition does not abolish this regional incorporation ([arts. 8 and 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a8); [historical art. 70](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a70)).

Rent requires one of: age under 36, legal large-family status, the specified two-child sole-ascendant situation, or disability at least 65%/qualifying judicial-support status. In a joint return, the age condition can be met by one spouse or the relevant parent. The property must be the taxpayer's actually occupied regional habitual home, with rental transfer tax paid or exemption justified; no home-investment deduction entitlement that year; and neither the taxpayer nor another family-unit member owning full title or a real use/enjoyment right in another home within **75 km**. Apply its BI ceiling/rural-family exception and share the deduction equally where several taxpayers qualify for the same property ([art. 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a9)).

The empty-home landlord must own/usufruct the regional property, have had it empty for at least a year before the contract, evidenced by art. 85 imputed property income, let it as the tenant's habitual home for at least three years, hold ownership/usufruct in **no more than three homes** counting any ownership percentage, and declare the letting as property income rather than business income. Garages/storerooms are excluded from that property count. Apply relief to the claimant's declared reduced net income, not gross rent or an assumed flat profit percentage. A tenant's death or other statutory habitual-home exception can preserve the three-year condition. Recalculate prior claims on breach; the law also excludes relief where a relevant tax-control procedure ends in regularisation ([art. 9 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a9-8)).

Rural rental rehabilitation excludes a property-development or rental business within LIRPF art. 27. The property must be in an eligible municipality/minor local entity below 3,000 at **31 December**, or a qualifying differentiated settlement in the official regional census. Apply the VAT art. 20.Uno.22 rehabilitation definition and maintain habitual-home letting to non-spouse/non-ascendant/non-descendant/non-third-degree relatives for five following years. For non-owner-caused vacancies, retain evidence that it is unoccupied, available and advertised on property portals or through agencies; annual offered rent cannot exceed **30% of the municipality's latest INE mean household income at accrual**. Apply the statutory day proportion rather than assume uninterrupted full-year credit. Include qualifying rehabilitation financing costs, net of hedge receipts; exclude recognised public aid. AEAT directs aid reductions at both total and annual base levels and allocation across rehabilitation years for a one-payment grant. Allocate joint rehabilitation cost by ownership unless another allocation is proved. Breach triggers recapture ([art. 9 ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a9-9); [AEAT rural rental guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-extremadura/inversiones-rehabilitacion-viviendas-zonas-rurales-alquiler.html)).

**Rural rental rehabilitation: grant ledger and completion-year day worksheet.** Establish the same claimant/property's eligible rehabilitation/finance costs and recognised public aid from invoices, financing/payment records and the award/certified-year schedule. Following [AEAT's exact base instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-extremadura/inversiones-rehabilitacion-viviendas-zonas-rurales-alquiler.html), first subtract the full recognised grant from total qualifying project investment, then apply the180,000total ceiling; do not subtract the grant again from an already net total. Separately subtract the grant share attributable to the current rehabilitation year from that year's eligible payments, then apply the9,040annual base cap per return and the remaining project-total ceiling. A single grant payment must be allocated across the actual rehabilitation years; the cash receipt date does not put the whole grant against that year's costs. The published instruction does not specify universal equal-year or cost weights. Use the supported award/certified-year allocation and reconcile its shares to the single grant; do not invent a favourable split where those documents do not establish one.

**Documented two-year grant example.** A sole claimant has14,000of independently eligible paid rehabilitation costs in2025and14,000in2026. A recognised12,000grant is paid once in2026; the award and certified-year schedule establish6,000attributable to each year's eligible costs. These year shares are stated documentary facts of this fixture, not a universal rule that every two-year grant is divided equally. Total net project investment is28,000−12,000=**16,000**, below180,000. Current2026net payments are14,000−6,000=**8,000**, so the annual base is8,000, assuming the total ceiling is not previously consumed and no other property uses the return's annual cap. Without this annual grant subtraction,14,000would instead hit9,040. Do not deduct all12,000from2026payments, which would give2,000, or treat16,000as the current annual base. The2025year's net-cost allocation is not proof that its credit was claimable before completion or that an unused2025amount carries into2026.

**Actual day framework, explicitly bridged from2025implementation.** The [official completion instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_10-extremadura/10_10_15-inversiones-rehabilitacion-zonas-rurales.html) ask separately for finish date, actual rented days and offered-but-vacant days after completion. In the public [RentaWEBOPEN2025, version1.19](https://www2.agenciatributaria.gob.es/wlpl/PARE-RW25/OPEN/index.zul?EJER=2025&TACCESO=COLAB), a fictional individual with10,000eligible annual investment and works finished31March2025received **986.18** for200rented+75offered days: `15% × min(10,000,9,040) × 200/275`. All275days rented instead gave1,356. With8,000annual net investment and the same200/75days, it gave **872.73**. A1January2025finish with300rented+64offered gave1,117.58; entering300+65was rejected because the day total must equal the days **after** completion. These actual separators support annual cap before the fraction, advertised vacancy outside the numerator, and the after-completion denominator rather than365. This is an inference for2026from the observed2025implementation of the unchanged9ter/agency instructions, not an observed2026return campaign or an expressly printed algebra ruling.

Apply that bounded framework to the2026grant fixture with works completed **31March2026**. Assume1April–17Octoberinclusive are **200**actual habitual-home letting days, and18October–31Decemberinclusive are **75**non-owner-caused vacant days, all unoccupied, available and advertised on property portals/agencies on qualifying terms. The tenant's early departure must preserve the statutory habitual-home status under an evidenced qualifying necessary-move exception; the landlord must continue the required five-year letting/advertised-vacancy undertaking and recapture on breach. For this example, assume the municipality's latest actual INE mean household income at accrual is an independently established30,000, so the annual advertised-rent ceiling is9,000; a700monthly offer annualises to8,400and passes. These are hypothetical verified-input assumptions, not a claimed income statistic for a named municipality. Preserve the real31Decemberpopulation/census evidence, dated INE release and offer terms, lease/nonrelationship/habitual-use evidence, dated finish certificate, rent/keys/advertisement records, proof the gap is not owner-caused, declared property income, grant allocation and sufficient regional quota. The credit is `round_to_cents(15% × 8,000 × 200/275)` = **872.73**. Counting advertised vacancy as actual rent would give1,200; using365would give657.53. Advertising preserves the qualified-gap treatment; it does not turn a vacant day into an actual rented day in this observed method.

**Timing boundary retained.** The same2025simulator rejected a31December2024completion with an explicit requirement that works finish in2025. Current9ter/agency text also speaks of annual rehabilitation/finance payments and the following five-year maintenance period, without an express completion-year-only entitlement sentence in the provisions read. The supplied positive example finishes in the claim year and avoids that conflict. Treatment of payments or financing in a different year from completion, pre-completion claims, zero post-completion days, and unearmarked/unequal grant-year weights remain precisely unconfirmed; no automatic carry, denial or blanket zero is substituted. The existing municipality, rehabilitation definition, five-year conditions and ownership/cost-allocation rules remain applicable.

The rural **own-home** credit is distinct: art. 11 bis requires acquisition/rehabilitation from **22 March 2022**, the commencement date of Ley 3/2022, and a municipality/minor local entity below 3,000 at 31 December. Include eligible acquisition costs, loan principal/interest and financing costs net of hedge receipts, with the cumulative and annual base caps. Reduce for prior relieved home investment and exempt reinvestment gain as the statute specifies; relief starts only after the required previous-investment/reinvestment threshold is exceeded. Former matrimonial-home payments can remain eligible where common children and the custodial parent continue using it as their habitual home. Apply the net-wealth increase test excluding finance costs and unrealised changes in still-held assets. Occupy permanently within twelve months and maintain habitual residence for three years, subject to the listed necessary-move/death exceptions. Construction must finish within four years of starting investment; enlargement must add permanently habitable area. Rehabilitation must principally reconstruct the structure/façade/roof or analogous elements and exceed 25% of relevant acquisition/market value excluding land, with the two-year lookback. This route does **not** acquire art. 8's first-home/age/BI conditions or art. 9 ter's differentiated-settlement extension merely because all three concern rural housing ([art. 11 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-12); [Ley 3/2022 final provision 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2022-5853)).

**Choose the own-home route before stacking claims:** arts. 8 and 11 quater cannot be combined with art. 11 bis. The tenant credit separately requires no home-investment deduction entitlement. Apply the overall regional tax floor of zero; these rules do not create a refund for unused ordinary regional credit ([art. 12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-4)).

### Extremadura: investment, gifts and moving residence

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Qualifying company subscription | 20% of eligible investment, annual credit cap 4,000 | 2026 | [Art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-3) |
| Money gifts for qualifying culture/art or sports sponsorship | 20%, **combined cap 500 per taxpayer** | 2026 | [Art. 11 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-18) |
| Move habitual residence to Extremadura | 50% of regional gross tax in the move year and next two years; **75% instead** where the moving taxpayer is under 36 at accrual; maintain residence continuously at least three years | 2026 | [Art. 11 sexies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-19) |

Subscriptions must be formation/capital-increase subscriptions in an SA, SL, labour SA/SL or cooperative with regional registered/tax domicile and genuine non-patrimonial economic activity. The investor plus spouse and relatives through the third degree cannot exceed **40% of capital or votes on any day of the calendar year**. Hold the investment for at least three years and perform no executive/management functions. A formation company must employ one full-time employee or at least two part-timers whose combined hours meet full time, registered and maintained at least 24 months. A capital-increase company must have been formed within the preceding three years; increase average employment during the following two tax years by at least one qualifying equivalent against the preceding twelve months, then maintain that increase for a further 24 months. Use a public deed identifying investors and amounts; breach requires repayment with interest in the breach-year return ([art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-3)).

Gifts must be **money**, pure, simple, irrevocable and documented in writing. Eligible cultural beneficiaries have regional tax domicile and carry out the statutory film/audiovisual/multimedia, performing, visual/design, publishing, heritage, traditional-cultural or applied-art activities in the region. Eligible sponsored sportspeople have regional tax domicile and the statutory sporting activity. Apply the exclusion for donations by qualifying participants and specified relatives where the individual/aggregated ownership threshold is at least **50%**; gifts to the donor's spouse/partner/ascendant/descendant sportsperson are excluded. Cultural recipients report donor identity, amount and eligible activity CNAE electronically in **January of the following year** ([art. 11 quinquies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-18)).

The move must establish regional residence under national art. 72 and have the statutory family/personal/professional or non-business expenditure/investment basis. If family-unit members previously resided in different communities, the member with the greatest individually attributed BL must move to Extremadura for joint-return relief; the moving member must meet the under-36 test for 75%. Recapture if the conditions fail. Do not import a salary-only restriction or an income ceiling absent from art. 11 sexies, and calculate the percentage on regional gross tax before the final zero floor ([art. 11 sexies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-19)).


### La Rioja: family, childcare and household spending

**Choose the current or preserved housing route first.** For the credits that expressly use an income test, the recurring test is **general BL subject to tax no more than 18,030 individual / 30,050 joint, and savings BL subject to tax no more than 1,800**. These are separate BL limits, not a ceiling on combined BI. Apply them to art. 32.3/6/9/10/11/12 and the enhanced legacy housing rates, not automatically to every regional credit. Rural relief uses the actual statutory municipal list: current [Annex I](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#ai) for the named current credits, and [Annex II](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#ai-2) for the preserved second-home credit. Do not replace either list with another community's population threshold ([Ley 10/2017 art. 32 and DT 1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Birth/adoption | 600 first / 750 second / 900 third and later child; add 60 to each child's amount for a multiple birth/adoption | 2026 | [Art. 32.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Childcare after establishing residence in an Annex I small municipality | 30% of eligible nursery/infant-centre/contracted-carer spending, **600 cap per child**; recurring BL test | 2026 | [Art. 32.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Foster children | 300 for at least 183 cohabitation days; 150 for more than 90 but fewer than 183; qualifying urgency/temporary/permanent fostering or adoption-purpose guardianship | 2026 | [Art. 32.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Young child and small-municipality residence | 100 **per qualifying month per child**, shared between entitled parents; maintain residence for three consecutive years from the first entitlement month, subject to statutory exceptions | 2026 | [Art. 32.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Ordinary infant-school expenses | 20% of eligible unsubsidised costs, **600 cap per child**; recurring BL test | 2026 | [Art. 32.6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| New Internet contract for emancipated young taxpayer | 30% of invoiced/paid annual costs; **40% instead** for a qualifying single-parent family or Annex I residence; recurring BL test | 2026 | [Art. 32.9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| New domestic electricity/gas contract for emancipated young taxpayer | 15% of invoiced/paid annual costs; **20% instead** for Annex I residence, **25% instead** for qualifying single-parent family; recurring BL test | 2026 | [Art. 32.10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Sport/exercise services | 30%; **100% instead** for the statutory over-65/disability-at-least-33% route; annual cap 300, shared with the eligible expenditure between entitled taxpayers | 2026 | [Art. 32.17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| ALS diagnosis/treatment expenses | 50% of eligible costs; annual cap 2,000, with equal sharing of costs and cap between entitled taxpayers | 2026 | [Art. 32.18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Diagnosed coeliac disease | 250 per qualifying person: taxpayer, spouse or minimum-entitled ascendant/descendant; equal sharing for the same person | 2026; enacted retroactive from 2025 | [Art. 32.20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |

Birth/adoption requires cohabitation at accrual. On individual returns, each cohabiting parent takes half the amount determined by **that parent's** pre-existing child count. Where the parents' child order differs, the joint-return deduction is the sum of those individually determined halves, not automatically the larger parent's whole amount. Fostering must be formalised by the competent regional judicial/administrative body; share equally on individual returns, exclude a same-year adoption by that family, and apply the statutory aggregation of different short placements where their combined stay exceeds 90 days ([art. 32.1/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4)).

For art. 32.3 rural childcare, establish residence in an Annex I municipality during the year and retain it at accrual; the parent(s) must work outside the family home during the relevant care period, cohabit with the minimum-entitled child and use a regional centre for at least half the established day, or an employed and Social Security-registered household carer. Deduct only the period meeting the work condition. Include qualifying registration, attendance, extended hours and meals, excluding grants and exempt employment-in-kind costs. Allocate both cost and the 600 cap equally between minimum-entitled parents; if only one qualifies for the descendant minimum, use the whole eligible cost and cap. The ordinary art. 32.6 nursery route uses regional residence, the recurring BL test, the minimum and a regional infant centre, without importing the rural route's outside-home work requirement. AEAT permits ordinary nursery costs only through the month before the child turns three and explains shared-custody/death cases. **Same-expense computation, labelled interpretation:** arts. 32.3 and 32.6 establish separate credits and separate 600-per-child ceilings, without a mutual exclusion. Where the same unsubsidised nursery invoice independently meets both routes, calculate each credit on its own eligible base and add the results; do not replace them with a single 600 combined cap. This is an interpretation of those own provisions, corroborated by the actual AEAT public calculation described below ([art. 32.3/6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4); [AEAT rural childcare](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-rioja/gastos-escuelas-infantiles-centros-infantiles-contratado.html), [ordinary nursery](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-rioja/hijo-0-3-anos-escuelas-inf.html)).

For the 100 monthly rural-child amount, residence and a qualifying statutory age-0-to-3 descendant must exist on the **last day of each month**. Split between entitled parents, rather than grant 100 to each. AEAT excludes the child's/parent's death month even when death occurs on the last day. Recapture with interest if the three-year residence condition fails without death or a necessary-move exception, such as separation, work relocation or obtaining qualifying employment. **Published administrative age method:** although art. 32.5 and the static instructions say “0 a 3”, the official Renta 2025 OPEN 1.19 monthly-credit dialog expressly requires the child to be “menor de tres años” on the last day of the month. Apply that under-three month-end test: a child born 15 June 2023 gives five age-eligible months in 2026, January–May, and 500 for a sole entitled parent or 250 each on two entitled parents’ individual returns. A third birthday on 30 June has the same five-month result; a third birthday on 1 January gives no age-eligible month that year. The birthday month is excluded, rather than extending relief through the completed third year. This is the published AEAT administrative reading of the unchanged provision, not a new enacted definition or an inference imported from the nursery credit ([art. 32.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4); [AEAT monthly-child guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-rioja/hijo-0-3-anos-residentes-municipios.html)).

**Separate nursery-credit example and source bridge.** For one child under three throughout the year, a sole minimum-entitled parent with qualifying outside-home work, qualifying residence established during the year in a current Annex I municipality and retained at accrual, qualifying half-day regional nursery care, BLG 18,000 and savings BL 0 pays 1,000 of unsubsidised eligible nursery fees. Both sets of conditions hold: rural credit `min(30% × 1,000,600) = 300`; ordinary credit `min(20% × 1,000,600) = 200`; total 500 before the available regional-quota limit. With two minimum-entitled parents who each meet both routes’ conditions and file separately, divide the eligible cost and each credit’s cap equally: 150 rural plus 100 ordinary for each parent. A Social Security-registered household carer can satisfy the rural route but does not become an infant centre for the ordinary route. The official fictional 2025 test returned 300+200=500 and reduced regional quota 724.30 to 224.30; it also displayed the monthly credit’s express under-three footnote. The 2026 method rests on current arts. 32.3/5/6, whose wording is unchanged by Ley 9/2025, and that observed administrative implementation. Use the **current 2026 Annex I**, amended by that law, rather than automatically reusing the 2025 municipal list. No 2026 calculator parity or court adjudication is claimed. Retain the article 32.16 incompatibility with the regional Patronage Law fiscal credit ([own current statute](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4); [Ley 9/2025 art. 1 and commencement](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-991); [official public Renta2025OPEN](https://www2.agenciatributaria.gob.es/wlpl/PARE-RW25/OPEN/index.zul?EJER=2025&TACCESO=COLAB)).

Internet and electricity/gas require a taxpayer **under 36 at period end**, an owned/rented habitual home, a contract subscribed during the year at least **six months before accrual** and maintained through accrual, and the entitled taxpayer named on it. Each route is usable only once per taxpayer/home, regardless of later tenure changes. Share where several cohabitants qualify. No contract holder can claim when a holder carries on economic activity in that home, even if it is also the habitual residence. Apply the recurring BL test. The enhanced rates replace the ordinary rate; they are not added together ([art. 32.9–10](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4)).

Sport costs concern the taxpayer, spouse or minimum-entitled people, for services performed in La Rioja during the period: gyms/sports facilities, registered regional sports bodies, sports/physical-education classes and regional federation licences. ALS expenses cover qualified health professionals, prescribed treatment and symptom relief for that same family group. Both require a **full/ordinary invoice**, not a simplified receipt. Coeliac relief needs a definitive diagnosis certificate with the practising doctor's full identification, registration number/speciality and issue/diagnosis dates ([art. 32.17–20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4)).

### La Rioja: housing, transport, agriculture and patronage

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Habitual-home acquisition/construction/rehabilitation in Annex I municipality | 5% of eligible payments; **base** cap 9,040 and **credit** cap 452 per return; qualifying acquisition/rehabilitation/construction begun from 1 January 2017 | 2026 | [Art. 32.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Habitual-home investment by under-36 taxpayer | 15%, annual **base** cap 9,000; recurring BL test; preserved pre-2013 routes take priority where applicable | 2026 | [Art. 32.11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Habitual-home rent by under-36 taxpayer | 10% of unsubsidised rent, cap **300 per contract**; Annex I home: **20%, cap 400 per contract instead**; same caps for individual/joint filing | 2026 | [Art. 32.12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Eligible new electric/alternative-fuel vehicle | 15% of eligible cost; 300 cap per return for statutory motor-vehicle classes, 225 for assisted bicycle; acquisition price no more than 50,000; one vehicle per taxpayer, registration-year claim, subject to national-credit suspension below | 2026 | [Art. 32.7 and DA 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#da-3) |
| Non-assisted bicycle | 15%, cap 50 per bicycle, at most two bicycles per family unit; joint marital-property spouses split on individual returns | 2026 | [Art. 32.13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Starting agricultural self-employment | 1,000 in start year and each of next two years, subject to continued residence/activity for 12/24/36 months respectively; per eligible taxpayer on a joint return | 2026 | [Art. 32.8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Agricultural professional-organisation dues | Eligible enrolment/periodic/extraordinary dues, annual cap 100 per qualifying taxpayer, including a joint return | 2026 | [Art. 32.19](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| General qualifying patronage / public or protected regional heritage gifts | 15%; **combined cap 500** for art. 32.14.a/b | 2026 | [Art. 32.14.a–c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Gifts to qualifying regional cultural companies | 20%, cap 500; company's own funds **below 300,000** | 2026 | [Art. 32.14.d](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Author/creator/heir's qualifying cultural property gift, gratuitous usufruct or temporary deposit | 20% of qualifying statutory value, cap 500, subject to official acceptance | 2026 | [Art. 32.14.e](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |
| Owner/usufructuary's qualifying regional heritage work | 15%, cap 500; registered regional heritage asset, no business-investment expenditure | 2026 | [Art. 32.15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4) |

For the current housing credits, apply the incorporated pre-2013 habitual-home/construction/rehabilitation rules where specified, including permanent occupation and retention, and the net-wealth increase test. The regional test excludes financing interest/costs from the investment comparison, unrealised movements in still-held assets and wealth increases subject to inheritance/gift tax. The 15% youth-home base includes qualifying acquisition costs, loan principal/interest and other eligible financing costs, net of interest-rate hedge receipts. Previous relieved home expenditure and exempt reinvestment gains must be exceeded before a new deduction starts and reduce the base as the statute specifies. Art. 58 bis includes construction and permanent enlargement, normally completed within four years; it excludes repairs, improvements and independently acquired annexes, but can include at most two garages bought with the home. Apply its documented insolvency/exceptional extension procedure rather than invent a construction deadline extension ([art. 32.2/11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4), [art. 58 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a5-12)).

The young tenant must actually occupy the regional habitual home, satisfy the recurring BL test, hold a tenancy with the corresponding transfer-tax return submitted and have **no entitlement to a home-investment deduction in that year**. On a joint return one spouse can meet the age test. Joint marital-property spouses split even if only one is named on the lease; entitled claimants otherwise share the amount equally while respecting the **per-contract** ceiling. Retain evidence of rent actually paid and deduct subsidies from the expense base ([art. 32.12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4)).

Vehicle classifications matter. The statutory list includes M1 passenger cars, N1 light goods vehicles and the specified L1e/L2e/L6e/L7e/L3e/L5e classes, plus assisted bicycles. M/N eligibility includes the specified homologated alternative gas fuels, BEV and range-extended electric vehicles; L-class vehicles/bicycles must be exclusively electric and homologated. Electric motorcycles require the statutory lithium-battery threshold and at least 70 km electric range; assisted bicycles require lithium batteries and the incorporated UNE standard. No business/professional use is allowed. **DA 3 suspends the regional credit while national LIRPF DA 58 remains operative, except for N1, L1e, L2e and assisted bicycles.** National DA 58 currently runs through 31 December 2026. Do not grant the suspended regional M1 credit just because the particular car fails national eligibility; the suspension is statutory, not merely a same-purchase election ([art. 32.7/DA 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#da-3); [LIRPF DA 58](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-11)). Keep invoices and classification/homologation evidence; the assisted-bicycle registration-year implementation and the statute's unusual motorcycle battery unit remain unconfirmed.

Agricultural startup uses the Social Security special-regime registration date. Exclude collaborating family self-employed workers, capital-company shareholders and someone who ceased agricultural self-employment in the preceding two years. Maintain regional residence and activity for the statutory period. Apply this credit **last** among regional credits; an unused amount carries for the **next three periods**, not indefinitely. Professional-organisation dues require the organisation's certificate and qualifying status under Ley 19/1977 ([art. 32.8/19 and DA 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#da-7)).

Patronage requires pure, simple, irrevocable gifts and the eligible activity/beneficiary conditions. Arts. 32.14.a/b cover the regional patronage strategy and qualifying publicly owned or individually protected/inventoried regional heritage. Material contributions use Law 49/2002 art. 18 valuation. Cultural-company gifts require regional tax domicile and the listed film, performing, visual, publishing, heritage, traditional or applied-art purposes. Cultural-property gifts/usufructs/deposits by authors/creators/heirs require acceptance by the competent culture department and statutory valuation. Choose the a/b group **or** d/e group: a/b are incompatible with d/e. The **sum of all art. 32.14 credits cannot exceed 30% of regional gross tax**; the separate item caps still apply. The cultural/heritage credits cannot make regional liquid tax negative, and the statute also provides incompatibility with the regional Patronage Law fiscal credit while that credit is operative; its complete cross-credit scope remains a specialist interaction to confirm. Recipients report the prescribed donor/amount/valuation/project data in **January of the following year**, subject to the national-reporting coordination and copy requirements ([art. 32.14–16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#a3-4)).

### La Rioja: preserved home deductions and expired interest relief

| Preserved route | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pre-2013 habitual-home rehabilitation | Young taxpayer: 5%, or 7% if recurring BL test met; other taxpayers: 2%; preserved eligibility and joint base limit below | 2026 payments under preserved entitlement | [DT 1.a / DT 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#dt) |
| Pre-2013 habitual-home acquisition/construction by young taxpayer | 3%, or 5% if recurring BL test met; young means under 36 at period end; residual base below | 2026 payments under preserved entitlement | [DT 1.b / DT 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#dt) |
| Preserved rural second home | 8%, annual credit cap 450.76, one qualifying second home per taxpayer, in Annex II municipality different from habitual-home municipality | 2026 payments under preserved entitlement | [DT 1.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#dt) |
| Preserved disability adaptations | 15%, with national DT 18.1.c/2 eligibility and competent technical certification; shared rehabilitation/adaptation base below | 2026 payments under preserved entitlement | [DT 1.d / DT 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#dt-2) |

Establish actual preserved entitlement first: pre-1 January 2013 acquisition or qualifying payments before that date, completion deadlines, and the prior-deduction requirement with its statutory previous-home/reinvestment exception. For rehabilitation and second-home rehabilitation the works must finish **before 1 January 2017**; construction normally has the incorporated four-year completion period and only proved statutory extensions. The regional youth current-home credit does not replace DT 1.a/b for a taxpayer required to remain in those preserved routes. Apply national DT 18 and the incorporated pre-2013 concepts/wealth test. Disability works additionally need the competent technical accessibility/sensory-communication certification ([DT 1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#dt)).

**The preserved base is not automatically another 9,040 on top of national home relief.** Under DT 2.2, the maximum annual base for preserved acquisition and rural second-home deductions is `max(0, 9,040 − the taxpayer's national transitional home-deduction base)`, disregarding the national disability-adaptation base for that subtraction. DT 2.3 separately imposes a **combined annual base cap of 9,040** on preserved rehabilitation and disability adaptations. Apply these limits, the route-specific rate and the second-home 450.76 credit cap, with the prior-home/reinvestment rules. A joint return does not make an over-age person's payments eligible for the youth deduction ([DT 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#dt-2); [AEAT preserved acquisition guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-rioja/cantidades-invertidas-adquisicion-construccion___.html)).

DA 2's 15% mortgage-interest credit, with 5,000 maximum base per home, expressly covers **2023, 2024 and 2025**. It is not a 2026 credit under the current text. Do not roll the 2025 manual's interest relief into 2026 or treat it as cumulative with the current small-municipality/youth-home and national transitional routes that its incompatibility provision identifies ([DA 2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2017-13750#da-2)).


### Murcia: separate income tests for housing, families and care

**Use the separate housing, care and energy income tests.** Most listed ceilings refer to **general BI plus savings BI**, but the tenant, old-home and household-care routes use different measures. A strict `below` limit excludes equality; a `no more than` limit includes it. Do not transfer a 30,000/50,000 family-credit limit to a 40,000 disability test or the family-unit 34,000 childcare-contributions test ([DL 1/2010 art. 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

| Credit | Amount, limit and income test | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Young habitual-home investment | 5% of eligible payments, **credit cap 300** per return; age **40 or below** at accrual; combined BI **below 40,000**, savings BI no more than 1,800; residual home base below | 2026 | [Art. 1.Uno](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| First-cycle infant education | 20% of eligible expenses less public grants; cap 1,000 per minimum-entitled child; combined BI no more than 30,000 individual / 50,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 1.Tres](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| School books/materials | 120 per minimum-entitled descendant, **credit reduced by relevant grants**; combined BI no more than 20,000 individual / 40,000 joint, or 33,000 / 53,000 for a legal large family | 2026 | [Art. 1.Ocho](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Birth/adoption | 100 first / 200 second / 300 third and later; combined BI no more than 30,000 individual / 50,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 1.Nueve](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Taxpayer disability | 150 for recognised disability at least 33%; combined BI no more than **40,000 for either individual or joint filing** | 2026 | [Art. 1.Diez](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Household-worker contributions: young-child care | 20% of eligible employer contributions, cap 400; relevant months with a minimum-entitled child under 12; **family-unit** combined BI no more than 34,000 | 2026 | [Art. 1.Once.1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Household-worker contributions: ascendant care | 20%, cap 400; qualifying over-65 minimum-entitled ascendant; claimant's combined BI no more than 34,000 | 2026 | [Art. 1.Once.3–4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Unpaid fostering of older/disabled person | 600 per qualifying person; cohabitation **more than 183 days**, with regional no-aid and residence certificates | 2026 | [Art. 1.Doce](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Habitual-home tenant | 10% of unsubsidised qualifying rent, cap **300 per contract**; general BI minus personal/family minimum **below 40,000** and savings BI no more than 1,800 | 2026 | [Art. 1.Trece](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Working woman: child/dependent | 300 first / 350 second / 400 third and later child under 18; 400 per qualifying dependent; combined BI no more than 20,000 individual / 40,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 1.Catorce](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
