| Large-family replacement/enlarged habitual home | 10%, **15% instead** for special-category family; annual **base** cap 5,000, at most fifteen claim years from acquisition | 2026 | [Art. 1.Quince](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Single-parent family | 303; claimant's BI plus relevant exempt child-maintenance payments no more than **35,240** | 2026 | [Art. 1.Dieciseis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Extracurricular foreign languages | 15%, cap 300 per qualifying child; combined BI **below** 25,000 individual / 40,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 1.Diecisiete](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| New high-speed Internet connection | 30% of initial/monthly fees, cap 300; habitual residence in municipality below 15,000 inhabitants at 31 December; contract year only | 2026 | [Art. 1.Dieciocho](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Child's prescribed lenses/contact lenses/cleaning solutions | 30%, cap 100; minimum-entitled child under 12 | 2026 | [Art. 1.Veintiuno](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Sport/healthy activities | 30%, **100% instead** for over-65 route; cap 150 per taxpayer; combined BI no more than 25,000 individual / 40,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 1.Veintidos](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Rare-disease treatment/care | Eligible actual costs, cap 300; taxpayer, spouse or minimum-entitled ascendant/descendant; invoice required | 2026 | [Art. 1.Veintitres](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Veterinary services | 30%, cap 100; combined BI no more than 25,000 individual / 40,000 joint; invoice for the taxpayer's domestic animal | 2026 | [Art. 1.Veinticinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |

The infant-education credit covers custody, food and exclusively school-use clothing at authorised, registered first-cycle centres; pre-enrolment/enrolment count as custody. Reduce the expense base for relevant public aid, retain invoices through the limitation period, require cohabitation/minimum entitlement and share equally on individual returns where several parents/guardians/adopters qualify. School books/materials cover the second infant cycle, primary and compulsory secondary stages. The paying ascendant must qualify for the minimum; share among entitled claimants and subtract each child's relevant public aid from the **120 credit**, not simply from an expense base to which an invented rate is applied. Birth relief uses each parent's pre-existing child count; joint filing with different orders adds the individually calculated halves ([art. 1.Tres/Ocho/Nueve](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

Household-care relief requires the registered household employer to contract and contribute for the worker, receive employment/economic-activity income and satisfy the route's other family conditions. In the under-12 route the relevant spouse/partner must also meet the parent/family-unit and earned-income conditions; use the family-unit income ceiling. In the older-person route use the claimant's own ceiling. Both 400 credits can coexist for the same claimant **only with different workers for the different care functions**, combined cap 800. The unpaid-fostering credit requires a person over 65 or disabled at least 33%, no relevant regional fostering aid and the specified certificates. Its fourth-degree-relative exclusion concerns the over-65 fostering case. Divide entitlement equally among simultaneous qualifying claimants ([art. 1.Once/Doce](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

The working-woman route requires qualifying employment/self-employment registration. Child relief is proportionate to days worked. A dependent is an ascendant over 75 or an ascendant/descendant disabled at least 65%, cohabiting **more than 183 days**; one person cannot generate two of this route's child/dependent amounts. Single-parent relief excludes cohabitation with outsiders except qualifying minimum-entitled ascendants. Its eligible children are minors or disabled adults with non-exempt annual income no more than 8,000, including the statutory dependent specialised-centre cases and assimilated guardianship/fostering. Other cohabiting non-qualifying descendants do not destroy relief if their non-exempt income is no more than 8,000. Where family circumstances change, apply the statutory at-least-183-day cohabitation rule. Include the qualifying children's exempt maintenance in the 35,240 ceiling ([art. 1.Catorce/Dieciseis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

Language tuition must be extracurricular foreign-language learning by minimum-entitled children in the listed infant/primary/secondary/baccalaureate/vocational stages; share between cohabiting entitled parents and retain payment evidence. Sport covers the taxpayer, spouse and minimum-entitled relatives for gyms/sports centres, federation membership, qualified coaching, Pilates and yoga, but excludes the statutory periodic social/nonprofit sports-club dues. Retain compliant invoices. Internet must be for exclusive habitual-home use, not economic activity; exclude a simple supplier switch where the earlier contract was made in a prior year and a new line alongside a continuing prior-year line. Apply the 300 cap across simultaneous contracts, split between entitled cohabitants and use it only once per taxpayer/home ([art. 1.Diecisiete/Dieciocho/Veintidos](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

### Murcia: housing conditions and preserved entitlement

For the 5% young-home route, include eligible acquisition/rehabilitation and financing payments. Acquisition must be a qualifying new home: first transfer after the declaration of new construction within three years. Apply the statutory rehabilitation conditions and habitual occupation/three-year retention with necessary-move/death exceptions. The maximum annual base is `max(0, 9,040 − national transitional home-deduction base)`, excluding the national disability-adaptation base from that subtraction, and the **regional credit is still capped at 300**. Apply the incorporated previous-home/reinvestment, joint-filing and net-wealth rules. The statute's wealth-test sentence repeats “end” rather than supplying a valid beginning/end comparison; AEAT's explanation expressly invokes the national wealth-increase rule. Do not execute an impossible year-end-versus-itself comparison ([art. 1.Uno](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1); [AEAT home explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/inversion-vivienda-habitual-jovenes-edad-anos.html)).

The tenant must be aged **40 or below**, a legal large-family member or disabled at least 65%; one spouse can satisfy the age test on a joint return. The regional home must actually be occupied as habitual residence. The tenancy's transfer-tax form must be submitted by IRPF accrual, except where its own filing period has not yet ended, when filing within that period suffices. Pay the landlord by card, bank transfer, named cheque or account deposit and retain invoice/receipt; cash does not qualify. Neither claimant nor family-unit member can hold **more than 50%** of full ownership or a real use/enjoyment right in another home. No home-investment deduction entitlement is allowed that year. Joint marital-property spouses share even with only one named tenant; two entitled taxpayers share the amount and the **per-contract** 300 cap ([art. 1.Trece](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

Large-family home acquisition must occur within five years of attaining legal large-family status or a later birth/adoption; an adjoining property physically united into a larger home can qualify even with separate registry titles. Sell the former habitual home within five years unless the case is an adjoining-property enlargement. New useful floor area must be **more than 10% greater**, using the combined area for the adjoining-property case and cadastral evidence. The 5,000 annual base includes qualifying acquisition and financing costs, net of mortgage-hedge receipts. Apply the ten/fifteen-percent rate for at most fifteen years; recapture with interest on failure of the sale/enlargement condition. It is incompatible with the ordinary young-home deduction for the same home; AEAT distinguishes the preserved old-home route ([art. 1.Uno.9/Quince](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1); [AEAT home explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/inversion-vivienda-habitual-jovenes-edad-anos.html)).

The preserved **1998–2000** claimant cohort has 2% relief for qualifying payments, or **3%** if general BI minus personal/family minimum is **below 24,200** and savings BI no more than 1,800, with the old statute's new-home/other conditions. The subsequent old youth cohorts use the current art. 1.Uno requirements, including current age, rather than obtain lifetime youth status from a prior claim. Keep national pre-2013 transitional home relief separate. The incorporated original-law base/cap is supplied below; do not substitute the current youth route's 300 cap ([DT 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#dtunica-2); [AEAT old-home guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/regimen-transitorio-deduccion-inversion-vivienda-habitual.html)).

**1998–2000 preserved-home base and cap, source-derived incorporation method.** Current DT1.1 expressly requires the **rest of original Ley9/1999 art1.Uno**, not just its new-construction condition. The [original operative text](https://www.boe.es/diario_boe/txt.php?id=BOE-A-2000-6844) includes acquisition/qualifying rehabilitation costs borne by the taxpayer and, for borrowing, principal repayment, interest and other financing costs. It also imports the previous-home/reinvestment, joint-filing and net-wealth conditions. An acquisition must be the first transfer after the declaration of new construction, within three years of that declaration; protected rehabilitation has its own original/successor qualification. Establish the old regional claim for this same home and the actual continuing habitual-home conditions. Current DT1.1 supplies today's 2%/3% income test, so do not reuse the original 2,500,000/250,000-peseta income bands.

For the ordinary sole-owner continuing-home case, record total qualifying current payments `T`, and the amount `N` constituting the national home-deduction base. Under the original national-reference ceiling/subtraction wording, use `B=max(0,min(T,9,040)−N)` with an identified reconciliation of the national and regional amounts, rather than deducting the same payments twice. The 9,040 reference comes from the currently preserved national investment-home limit: [DT18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava) keeps the relevant [68.1 wording](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68). This is a reasoned application of the old provision's national-law reference to the surviving 2026 limit, not a claim that the 1999 text printed 9,040 euros. Do not use the national percentage credit as `N`: it is the **base** being subtracted. Keep national eligibility and actual amount used separately established.

The original combined credit ceiling is **41,000 pesetas**, increased to **46,000** if the declarant is under 30 at the relevant accrual. [EU2866/98 art1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOUE-L-1998-82362) fixes one euro at166.386pesetas; [Law46/1998 arts11/26](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-29216#a11) supplies redenomination and rounding. The converted whole monetary ceilings are **246.41 / 276.47 euros**. They are not the current youth-credit 300 limit. Apply `min(rate×B, applicable converted ceiling)` and the available regional-quota limit, retaining the incorporated combined-cap/joint/other-condition evidence. This calculation concerns the surviving habitual-home claim, not a newly granted second-home10% deduction from the repealed original cohort law.

**Separating controls.** A sole owner aged50 has a proved qualifying regional claim in2000 for this same new home, continues to satisfy the habitual-home and original requirements, and has20,000 qualifying2026 principal/interest costs. If the verified national home base is6,000, `B=9,040−6,000=3,040`. With current generalBI minus minimum20,000 and savingsBI1,000, the3%gate passes: **91.20 regional credit**, below246.41. If the verified national base is0, `B=9,040`, giving271.20before the original cap and **246.41 after it**. At the2%rate the same base gives **180.80**. If total qualifying payments are8,000 and national base6,000, the remaining regional base is2,000 and the3%credit **60**, not91.20: reconcile the actual payment pool before the subtraction. A national base9,040 exhausts the common ceiling and leaves0 regional base. Each national amount is a stated verified input; the examples do not establish eligibility, waive a claim requirement or recommend giving up a more valuable national credit. These are statutory-chain computations, not printed2026 agency examples.

The [AEAT transitional summary](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/regimen-transitorio-deduccion-inversion-vivienda-habitual.html) states the rates/new-home condition and compatibility with the separate large-family route, but omits these incorporated base/cap details. That omission is not an express administrative contrary cap or repeal. No live2026software parity or case-specific allocation across different homes/owners is asserted; those need their actual records and applicable rules. Preserve the statutory incorporation if a worksheet appears to award more.

### Murcia: water, renewable energy and electric transport

| Credit | Calculation and limits | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Domestic water-saving devices | 20% of qualifying acquisition/installation, **base cap 300 / credit cap 60**; prior regional recognition required | 2026 | [Art. 1.Cuatro](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Renewable-energy installation | 50% / 37.5% / 25% according to the BI bands below; credit cap 7,000, proportional sharing by investment; unused credit carries at most two following periods | 2026 | [Art. 1.Cinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Eligible electric vehicle | 30% / 22.5% / 15% according to BI bands below; credit cap 7,000 **per vehicle**, qualifying base caps by vehicle type; carry at most three following periods | 2026 | [Art. 1.Diecinueve](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Charging infrastructure | AEAT confirms **100%** of eligible net base, maximum base 4,000; statutory ownership/operation conditions and at most three following-period carry | 2026 | [Art. 1.Veinte](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1), [AEAT calculation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/gastos-instalacion-infraestructuras-recarga-vehiculos-electricos.html) |

For energy, individual combined BI **below 35,000** gives 50%; **35,000 to below 45,000** gives 37.5%; **45,000 to below 60,000** gives 25%; at least 60,000 gives no credit. Joint bands are **below 50,000 / 50,000 to below 75,000 / 75,000 to below 95,000**, with no credit from 95,000. Vehicle rates for those corresponding bands are **30% / 22.5% / 15%**, except that the enacted individual vehicle text overlaps at **exactly 35,000**: its first band says “no more than” 35,000 and its next says “at least” 35,000. AEAT's table assigns 22.5% at equality. **For the published administrative worksheet, use 22.5% at exactly 35,000 individual combined BI.** This supplies the agency route; the enacted 30%/22.5% overlap and any claim or rectification seeking the alternative statutory reading remain unresolved. It is not a legislative correction or a binding ruling ([art. 1.Cinco/Diecinueve](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1); [AEAT vehicle table](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/gastos-adquisicion-vehiculos-electricos.html)).

Water-saving devices must satisfy Ley 6/2006 art. 4 and be installed in the habitual home with prior regional recognition. Energy installations must be in a regional habitual home or a regional non-business rental home, exclusively for self-consumption; photovoltaic surplus without compensation is excluded. File the required responsible declaration where applicable, use an authorised installer and compliant invoice, pay by card/transfer/named cheque/account deposit and exclude cash and relevant public aid. Where several taxpayers invest in the same home, share credit and cap proportionately to amounts invested. A homeowners' association must certify each owner's participation and contributions. Apply renewable-energy relief **last** among regional credits; carry unused quota-limited entitlement for at most two following periods. DA 9's prior-recognition deadline of 30 June 2024 concerns **2023 investments**, not a new approval deadline for 2026 expenditure ([art. 1.Cuatro/Cinco and DA 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#da-7)).

Vehicles must be qualifying BEV/EREV/PHEV/fuel-cell models in M1, specified L3e/L4e/L5e electric motorcycles of at least 3 kW and 70 km range, or L1e mopeds; no economic-activity use. Eligible bases, including taxes/costs and **less public acquisition aid**, are capped at **45,000 for a car, 53,000 for an eight-/nine-seat BEV, 10,000 for a motorcycle or 3,000 for a moped**. Direct purchase, leasing and renting can qualify. A new/imported vehicle normally needs first Spanish registration in the entitled person's name; renting can use the company's registration. Dealer demonstrators up to eighteen months old can qualify under the statutory provenance rule. Retain the relevant contract and regional tax domicile in the acquisition year. Hold title five years, except death/total loss, or keep renting at least two years. AEAT allocates direct-purchase relief to the claimant's registration year and leasing/renting relief to contract year, includes the contractual payments/finance costs/purchase option in their acquisition value, and explains equal sharing of jointly contracted vehicle base/credit/cap and optional joint-marital-property sharing. It limits each taxpayer to one vehicle per year ([art. 1.Diecinueve](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1); [AEAT vehicle implementation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/gastos-adquisicion-vehiculos-electricos.html)).

Charging requires the taxpayer's infrastructure in an owned property or communal garage, for private-use vehicles, an invoice and continued infrastructure ownership/operation for at least two years. Eligible costs are those incorporated from MOVES III incentives programme 2. AEAT confirms 100% relief, completion/invoice-year timing, ownership rather than mere tenancy/usufruct, payer/marital-property attribution, one infrastructure per taxpayer/year and three following-period carry. **Follow the statute's express public-aid subtraction:** AEAT's page both states that subtraction and later says qualifying MOVES aid does not reduce the base. The latter sentence conflicts with art. 1.Veinte.2 and is not used here. The source-derived shared-infrastructure worksheet below supplies an administrative interpretation; the competing cap-first reading remains unresolved. AEAT orders non-carry credits first, then the vehicle credit, charging credit and renewable-energy credit last ([art. 1.Veinte](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1); [AEAT charging guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/gastos-instalacion-infraestructuras-recarga-vehiculos-electricos.html)).

For a **shared charger, use this source-derived administrative interpretation: allocate the supported eligible net costs to each entitled claimant first, then apply that claimant's 4,000 limit**. Attribute ganancial costs equally; otherwise use each co-payer's actual supported payment. Apply 100% to `min(own allocated eligible net costs,4,000)`, retaining the statutory aid subtraction above. AEAT defines the base by the claimant's amounts paid, attributes those amounts between payers and states 4,000 “por infraestructura de recarga y contribuyente”; its vehicle guidance expressly prorates the base and credit caps, while its charger guidance specifies proration of the deduction without expressly prorating the 4,000 cap. This combined-text reading is an interpretation, not an express worked administrative ruling or a legislative resolution. Net3,000 ganancial gives1,500 each; net4,000 paid75:25 gives3,000/1,000. Shared eligible net6,000 paid equally gives3,000 each; paid75:25 gives4,000/1,500. Each claimant must independently prove ownership, payment and all other existing conditions; occupancy alone does not qualify. Retain each claimant's own available-quota and three-following-period carry ledger: if the equal-share claimant has2,000 available quota, use2,000 and carry1,000. The competing common-cap-first interpretation gives2,000 each for equal shared net6,000, or3,000/1,000 for75:25; that cap-order interpretation and any dispute or challenge remain unresolved. No live2026return-software parity is claimed ([regional authority](https://agenciatributaria.carm.es/renta); [currentart1.Veinte](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1); [AEAT charger amounts, attribution and cap](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-region-murcia/gastos-instalacion-infraestructuras-recarga-vehiculos-electricos.html)). For a vehicle at exactly35,000individualBI,the published AEAT worksheet is `min(22.5% × eligible base,7,000)`, subject to the existing entitlement, vehicle-base, sharing and available-quota rules. For a qualifying solely owned passenger car with20,000eligible base after aid and no sharing, this gives4,500; with only3,000available regional quota, use3,000and carry1,500for at most the next three periods under the existing ordering. The competing literal30%reading would give6,000; that statutory challenge remains distinct from the supplied4,500administrative amount. Eligible base32,000gives7,000under either reading. No live2026return-software parity or resolution of the legislative overlap is claimed ([currentart1.Diecininueve/Veinte](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1);the exactvehicle/charging instructions already cited).

### Murcia: gifts and qualifying equity

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Qualifying cultural/social/scientific/environmental patronage money gifts | 50% of eligible regional gift base **after subtracting the base used for national gift relief** | 2026 | [Art. 1.Dos.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Qualifying biomedical research money gifts | 50%, with the same national-base subtraction | 2026 | [Art. 1.Dos.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Inventoried regional cultural property gift | 15% of statutory value: book value, or Wealth Tax value if absent, in either case no more than market value | 2026 | [Art. 1.Dos.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| New/recent company subscription | 20%, cap 4,000; statutory holding, employment and no-executive/no-employment conditions | 2026 | [Art. 1.Seis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Expansion-market subscription | 20%, cap 10,000; qualifying capital increase through the specified market segment; ownership no more than 10%, at least two-year holding | 2026 | [Art. 1.Siete](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |
| Social-economy membership investment | 20%, cap 4,000; qualifying entity, public deed and at least five-year investment/entity-condition maintenance | 2026 | [Art. 1.Veinticuatro](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1) |

Money gifts must be pure/simple, sent by **bank transfer**, and made to qualifying project holders/biomedical public or registered Law 49/2002 bodies for the specified regional purposes. Cultural-property recipients must be eligible regional public cultural bodies, qualifying regional universities/research/art institutions or registered exclusively cultural nonprofit bodies. Obtain the beneficiary certificate identifying donor/recipient, project eligibility, amount/date, transfer details or authentic property-delivery document, purpose and irrevocability. The statute provides incompatibility with the regional Patronage Law fiscal credit while operative; specialist cross-credit scope remains unconfirmed. Subtract a national **gift base**, not the smaller amount of national tax credit, when calculating the two regional money-gift bases ([art. 1.Dos](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

For a new/recent SA/SL/labour company/cooperative subscription, aggregate investor/spouse/third-degree relatives' capital/votes at no more than **40% throughout three following years**. Maintain regional registered/tax domicile, a genuine non-patrimonial activity and at least one full-time general-regime employee through those three years. A capital-increase company must have been formed within the preceding three years and increase average employment by at least **two equivalents** during the specified 24-month window against the previous twelve months, maintaining that increase another 24 months. The investor can sit on the board but cannot exercise executive/management functions or have an employment relationship with the company for **ten years**. Hold shares three years, use a public deed identifying investors/amounts and make the required prior regional communication. The expansion-market route instead requires the regional domicile/non-patrimonial activity and no-more-than-10% ownership throughout a two-year hold, plus public deed/prior communication. They cannot relieve the same investment twice ([art. 1.Seis/Siete](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).

For social-economy membership, aggregate the same family circle at no more than 40%, use a qualifying Law 5/2011 entity with Murcia registered/tax domicile and at least one full-time general-regime employee, execute the public deed, and maintain the contribution and entity conditions five years. Apply its statutory recapture/interest rules on a later breach. Do not combine this credit with either of the other equity credits on the same investment ([art. 1.Veinticuatro](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-10542#a1)).


### Galicia: claim year, income measure and family relief

**Check the income measure and claim year for each credit.** Several family/care credits use `general BI + savings BI − personal/family minima`, whereas the tenant credit's ceiling uses BI **before** those minima and the new school credit uses prior-year **family income per computable member**. Birth-year amounts and the two following years also differ. A current regional source citation does not turn a 2025-only transition into a 2026 rule ([DL 1/2011 art. 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Birth/adoption, event year | If combined BI minus minima is **no more than 22,000**: 360 first / 1,200 second / 2,400 third and later. From **22,000.01**: 300 per child, or 360 per child for a multiple birth | 2026 events | [Art. 5.Dos.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Birth/adoption, following two years | At no more than 22,000: 360 / 1,200 / 2,400; at 22,000.01–31,000: 300 per child; above 31,000: no continuing credit | 2026 continuing entitlement | [Art. 5.Dos.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Birth/adoption additions | Event-year amount increased by 20% for the statutory below-5,000/merged municipality route; birth/adoption amounts doubled for the child's recognised disability at least 33% | 2026 | [Art. 5.Dos](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Two minimum-entitled descendants | 250, doubled if claimant or qualifying descendant disabled at least 65%; incompatible with regional large-family route | 2026 | [Art. 5.Tres.1/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Legal large family | Up to two children: 250, or 400 for special-category title; three children: 500, then 250 per further child. Statutory disability doubling and equal sharing apply | 2026 | [Art. 5.Tres.2–4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5), [regional finance ministry's increment explanation](https://www.conselleriadefacenda.gal/es/sala-prensa/notas-de-prensa/-/asset_publisher/WMAKK58kdtGt/content/hoxe-entran-en-vigor-os-orzamentos-de-galicia-para-este-ano-2024?inheritRedirect=false) |
| Foster child | 300 per qualifying formally placed child; exclude same-year pre-adoption placement followed by adoption | 2026 | [Art. 5.Cuatro](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Work-related child care | 30%, cap 400, **600 instead with two or more children**; child aged three or below at accrual; combined BI minus minima no more than 22,000 individual / 31,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 5.Cinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Disabled taxpayer aged at least 65 needing third-person help | 10% of eligible third-person payments, cap 600; disability at least 65%, certified need, no public/concerted regional residence; combined BI minus minima no more than 22,000 individual / 31,000 joint | 2026 | [Art. 5.Seis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Habitual-home rent | Age **35 or below**: 10%, cap 300 per contract; **20%, cap 600 instead** with two or more minor children. Double the statutory amount/cap for tenant disability at least 33%; BI before minima no more than 22,000 | 2026 | [Art. 5.Siete](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| New home Internet connection | 30% of initial/monthly high-speed charges, cap 100 across contracts; connection-contract year only, exclusive home use, no economic activity | 2026 | [Art. 5.Ocho](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| School textbooks/materials | 15%, cap 105 per eligible descendant; prior-year family income per computable member no more than 30,000; publicly funded school and no incompatible same-course aid | 2026 | [Art. 5.Veinticinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |

Birth/adoption requires cohabitation at each relevant accrual date. Where both parents cohabit with the child, split the deduction equally; check child order under the descendant-minimum criteria and the child's disability evidence. The event-year rural increment is not silently added to the separately specified following-year amounts. The event year has no 31,000 ceiling for its ordinary 300 amount, while the following years do. Large-family status requires the title at accrual; two-child and large-family routes are alternatives. Apply the disability increase only to the entitled claimant's share where the claimant alone is disabled, rather than doubling the non-disabled spouse's share automatically. Current fostering does not import the former 183/90-day schedule that was removed; preserve formal regional placement evidence and split qualifying married/registered-partner claims on individual returns ([art. 5.Dos–Cuatro](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); [AEAT birth](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/nacimiento-adopcion-hijos.html), [large family](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/familia-numerosa.html)).

Childcare requires work plus Social Security/Mutuality registration by both parents and the household carer's own registration, or qualifying infant-school care; share the amount for the same descendants. The young tenant's lease must be **after 1 January 2003** and the deposit must be lodged with IGVS, or the taxpayer must hold the prescribed certified complaint about the landlord's failure to supply proof. A joint return can meet age through one spouse or the relevant parent. Split the claim between entitled taxpayers while respecting its per-contract ceiling. For Internet, exclude a mere change of provider where the original contract was made in another year, or a new connection while retaining an earlier-year connection ([art. 5.Cinco/Siete/Ocho](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

For school relief, cover primary, compulsory-secondary and special-education textbooks/materials at publicly funded schools. The minimum-entitled descendant rule has an express exception where the paying parent lacks that minimum. Use actual payments and prorate the **105 cap** where several taxpayers qualify. Prior-year family income is general plus savings BI for members filing IRPF, or the authority's imputed-income certificate for non-filers; use the computable-family rules, including shared-custody households, eligible under-25 cohabitants and the relevant new-spouse/partner cases. Count a member twice for qualifying disability at least 33% or specified permanent-incapacity pension. Exclude the non-cohabiting separated parent except shared custody and the aggressor in gender-violence cases ([art. 5.Veinticinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); [AEAT school guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/adquisicion-libros-texto-material-escolar.html)).

The **26 March 2026 Order** lists eligible exercise books, paper/folders, writing/drawing/craft materials, school-required reading books/dictionaries/basic calculators, bags/cases and PE sports clothing/shoes. It excludes general electronics, ordinary uniforms, meals/transport/extracurricular services and non-curricular recreation. Its material-spending ceiling is **300 per tax period**, with **150 for sports materials**, distinct from the 105 per-descendant tax-credit ceiling. The labelled per-descendant interpretation below follows the Council's express clarification on that unit and replaces the earlier claimant-return pool. The advisory scope, final-text omission and separate legality objection remain explicit. Detailed invoices and non-cash payment evidence are required for 2026, including qualifying authorised instant-payment platforms. The **July–December invoice window** and authority's cash-payment exception concern **2025**, not 2026. A same-course textbook/materials grant or solidarity textbook fund blocks the credit for the affected descendant; it does not automatically block an unassisted sibling ([Order arts. 2–4/additional provision](https://www.xunta.gal/dog/Publicados/2026/20260327/AnuncioG0597-260326-0002_gl.html); [Ley 5/2025 additional provision 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-3385); [AEAT implementation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/adquisicion-libros-texto-material-escolar.html)).

**School costs by child and payer.** Keep a separate expense record for each child and actual payer, splitting statutory textbooks from the Order's school materials and identifying PE clothing/footwear within materials. The sports 150 limit is **inside** the total 300 material limit; it is not an extra 150 on top. For one child and one claimant, eligible material base is `min(300, other eligible materials + min(150, eligible PE items))`; add eligible statutory textbooks, then calculate 15% and cap the credit at 105. Thus 100 textbooks,200 other materials and 200 PE items give 300 material base and 400 combined base: credit 60, before the available-quota limit.

**Binding expense ceilings: labelled per-descendant interpretation.** Apply the 300 material and 150 PE expense ceilings separately to each enrolled eligible descendant in the following sole-payer worksheet. This interpretation is supported by the [Consello Consultivo's opinion 085/2026 of 25 March 2026](https://www.knosys.es/ccgalicia/knpag?lang=GAL&x=index), consideration five, article 4: the Council expressly recommends clarifying that the expense limit applies to each enrolled descendant under [statute 5.25.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5). Search the official database for year 2026 and `material escolar`, then select 085/2026. The [final Order's art 4](https://www.xunta.gal/dog/Publicados/2026/20260327/AnuncioG0597-260326-0002_gl.html) retains 300/150 per tax period without adding those per-child words. The opinion concerns the draft and supplies a primary interpretive basis; it is not a binding tax ruling, an adopted amendment or proof of a 2026 software outcome. This replaces the earlier claimant-return pool reading. Do not aggregate different children's materials into one 300 pool or transfer invoices from one child to another.

For each child `c` with a sole entitled actual payer, let `T[c]` be qualifying statutory textbooks, `O[c]` other eligible materials and `P[c]` eligible PE items. Calculate `M[c] = min(300, O[c] + min(150, P[c]))`, then `D[c] = min(15% × (T[c] + M[c]), 105)`. Sum the independently qualifying child credits, subject to the available regional quota. Required reading books listed as materials stay inside M; they are not relabelled as statutory textbooks to escape its ceiling. All school, minimum-entitlement/payment exception, prior-year income, invoice, payment and incompatible-aid gates above still apply.

**Binding two-child controls.** With one sole entitled payer, sufficient quota and all gates established, each of two children has 200 other materials and 100 PE, with no textbooks. Each child's material base is 300 and credit 45; the result is **90**, rather than the 45 produced by the replaced cross-child pool. If each child instead has 200 other materials and 200 PE, each still has 300 admitted materials after the separate 150 PE sublimit and 300 total cap; the combined credit stays 90. On one joint return, the same actual child records produce 90 under this per-descendant interpretation. [LIRPF 84.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a84) does not multiply a child's monetary ceiling by the spouses; each child's actual expenditure is counted once.

**Textbooks already exhaust one child's credit.** One sole entitled payer has two eligible children A/B, sufficient quota and all gates established. ChildA has 700 qualifying textbooks and 200 other materials; child B has 200 other materials and no textbooks or PE. Apply the expense ceiling to each child's own ledger: A has 200 admitted materials and 900 combined base, with `min(900×15%,105)=105`; B has 200 admitted materials and `200×15%=30`. The combined credit is **135**. No cross-child 300 pool exists under the selected interpretation, so the earlier hypothetical 100/200,150/150 and 200/100 pool assignments are not elections or competing outputs of this worksheet. They described the superseded claimant-return premise. Do not transfer expenditure or unused credit between children.

**Several actual payers.** Once each claimant's eligible paid expense base is established, the distinct sharing method remains `D[t,c] = min(15% × E[t,c], 105 / n[c])`, where `n[c]` is the number of taxpayers entitled for that child and `E[t,c]` is claimant t's actual eligible expenditure. [AEAT's published instruction](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/adquisicion-libros-texto-material-escolar.html) confines proration to the 105 credit ceiling. Do not divide actual paid expenses again. The preserved nonbinding-cost control has two children and two entitled individually filing claimants for each: A pays 200 textbooks for child 1 and 100 materials for child 2; B pays 100 textbooks for child 1 and 200 materials for child 2. Child 2's combined materials are 300 and there is no PE. A claims 30+15=45, B 15+30=45, total 90; each child/claimant credit cap is 52.50. This result does not require a disputed allocation of a binding expense ceiling. The Council's per-descendant clarification does **not** grant an additional 300/150 pool for every paying parent. How a binding single-child material/PE ceiling is apportioned among different actual payers remains a precise limit; this worksheet does not invent equal expense splitting or duplicate the child's admitted cost. The [completion help](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_11-galicia/10_11_24-adquisicion-libros-texto-material-escolar.html) asks for each child's already-eligible amount and entitled-person count, without settling that upstream allocation.

**Separate legality issue.** In consideration four, the same Council opinion objects that the draft's 300 ceiling reduces the statutory 15%/105 relief without sufficient legislative authority and invokes the reservation of law. Its conclusion expressly excepts that legality objection. The final Order nevertheless enacts 300/150 and its preamble refers to the Council opinion. An advisory objection does not itself annul the Order. The supplied calculation retains its enacted ceilings; an authoritative contrary unit interpretation, challenge to the cap's validity or consequential allocation among multiple paying claimants requires its own determination. No right to ignore 300/150, statutory amendment, court annulment, express final-text per-child wording or 2026 software parity is asserted. The cited AEAT manual is for 2025; the current statute and Order supply the 2026 substantive rules, with the 2025-only invoice/cash exceptions kept separate.


### Galicia: home works, previously empty rentals and aid

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Renewable heating/hot-water installation | 5% of qualifying payments, cap 280 per taxpayer; habitual home, exclusive self-consumption, OVI registration and documentary requirements | 2026 | [Art. 5.Trece](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Designated historic-centre rehabilitation | 15% of eligible investment, credit cap 9,000; designated area, permissions and statutory reconstruction/value test | 2026 | [Art. 5.Catorce](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Residential energy-efficiency works | 15% of eligible works with **base cap 9,000 per taxpayer**, plus qualifying certification fees/registration charges up to a **single 150 cap shared by ownership** | 2026 completion year | [Art. 5.Dieciocho](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Model-village acquisition/rehabilitation | 15%; annual **base** cap 9,000 for habitual home / 4,500 for other eligible residential use | 2026 | [Art. 5.Veinte](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Preparing an empty property for housing rental | 15%; **total base 9,000 per home**, ordinarily **annual base 3,000 per home**, with distinct excess-base and insufficient-tax carry rules below | 2026 lease-constitution year and qualifying carry | [Art. 5.Veintidos](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Bringing an empty home into housing rental | 500 per home in the first qualifying lease year, proportionate to ownership; monthly rent including annexes no more than 700 | 2026 | [Art. 5.Veintitres](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Specified 2025 wildfire aid, high-level sport aid, ALS/phenotype aid or thalidomide aid | Statutory average-rate credit on the qualifying aid included in BL after general-BI inclusion; each route has its own recipient/source conditions | 2026 inclusion where applicable, with historic transitions separate | [Art. 5.Dieciseis/Diecinueve/Veinticuatro/Veintiseis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |

Renewable heating/hot-water payments must be made by card, transfer, named cheque or bank-account deposit to an authorised installer; cash is excluded. Register the installation through the **Oficina Virtual de Industria (OVI)** and obtain its verification code. Before the return-filing period expires, submit the analysed budget, invoices and payment proof through OVI, plus the community representative's ownership/contribution certificate for shared property. State the installation code on the return. For efficiency works, obtain permissions and improve **both** the CO₂-emissions and non-renewable primary-energy ratings by one letter. By the completion-year return deadline, upload the registered post-works certificate, competent technician's improvement report, invoices and payment proof to the regional energy-efficiency registry; include its certificate number on the return. Shared-building representatives also certify each owner's contributions. Apply non-cash works payments and choose between this credit and renewable heating/hot-water relief; they are incompatible ([art. 5.Trece/Dieciocho](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

Historic-centre and model-village rehabilitation require authorised principally structural/façade/roof or analogous reconstruction costing **more than 25%** of the relevant acquisition value where acquired in the prior two years, or otherwise market value, excluding land. Where land value is unknown, use the statutory cadastral land/building apportionment. Historic-centre status must be proved under the implementing designation order. Model-village homes must sit in a formally qualifying Law 11/2021 project and be used by the taxpayer habitually or occasionally; the base includes eligible acquisition/rehabilitation and financing costs, net of mortgage-hedge receipts. The same model-village investment cannot also receive the heating/hot-water or efficiency-works credit ([art. 5.Catorce/Veinte](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

Preparing an empty rental can cover repair/conservation and necessary letting-readiness costs, including certification and contract formalisation. Require a regional property empty and unused/unlet/non-business for **at least a year before works**, completion within **two years**, and a qualifying housing lease within **six months after completion**. Value cannot exceed **250,000**, measured at the completion-year accrual date by cadastral reference value or market value if no certifiable reference value exists. Exclude a spouse/third-degree-related tenant. The taxpayer must own no more than **three rentable homes**, wherever located, excluding garages/storerooms and the habitual home; disclose provider NIFs and amounts. Retain invoices and payer evidence; AEAT prorates the property base/cap by eligible owners' actual payments ([art. 5.Veintidos.1–4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); [AEAT empty-home works](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/gastos-derivados-adecuacion-inmueble-vacio-vivienda.html)).

**Empty-rental works carry registers.** Keep an expense-base register separately from the tax-credit register, by home, claimant and origin year. On the first qualifying lease-year calculation, establish each claimant's supported share of actual eligible costs and the shared **9,000 total home base**. For one home, at most **3,000 of that property base** enters a year's15% calculation; expense excluded by that annual base ceiling remains expense base, available in the **next two years**. It is not already a tax credit. Allocate the property base and cap by the eligible owners' actual payments, as the [exact AEAT instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/gastos-derivados-adecuacion-inmueble-vacio-vivienda.html) require. Joint filing does not double the property caps. If two or three qualifying property claims coexist, the statute supplies the **6,000 combined annual base** and **four following years** for excess base; retain each property's9,000 total limit ([art5.Veintidós.2–5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

For each year's allowed expense base `B`, compute a new nominal tax credit `C=15%×B`. Remove `B` from the expense-base register when it enters this computation, even if regional tax is insufficient. The resulting unused **credit**, rather than that expense base a second time, belongs in the credit register. The credit-year amount not absorbed for insufficient regional quota can be used in the **next four years** while the stated rental conditions remain satisfied. A tax credit first computed from an allowed2026 base therefore has a2030 last-use year; one first computed on an allowed2028 excess-base tranche has a2032 last-use year. These are separate clocks: carrying an already computed2026 credit into2027 does not restart its2030 expiry. This is the staged application of the expense-base limit in paragraph3 and the insufficient-quota rule in paragraph7, not a second deduction for the same expenditure ([art5.Veintidós.3/7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

After the regional part of State deductions and the other regional deductions, let `R` be the remaining regional quota. Add the current-year nominal works credit to the still-valid earlier works-credit balances, then use at most `R`; the sum cannot create negative regional tax. If `R` covers all those amounts, clear the older credit balances and current credit in full and carry only the unused expense base. This supplies an ordinary computation even when both registers coexist; they are not alternative treatments of one amount. The [AEAT ordering instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/gastos-derivados-adecuacion-inmueble-vacio-vivienda.html) also say that, when this works deduction and the separate500-per-home market-entry deduction are both due, the smaller of the two deductions is used first, then the other against the remaining quota. This is a rule between the two deduction types; it does not expressly prescribe oldest-credit-first within one deduction's competing vintages.

For a sole claimant, one qualifying property and9,000 eligible costs with first lease in2026: enter3,000 base and450 nominal credit in2026, leaving6,000 expense base. If `R2026=100`, use100 and carry350 tax credit, last use2030. In2027, put another3,000 of the original expense base through15%, generating450; if `R2027=800`, use the older350 and current450 in full, leaving3,000 expense base and no tax-credit carry. In2028, the last3,000 generates450; `R2028=450` uses it all. Total relief is1,350, exactly15% of9,000. The2026 credit350 was never added back into the expense base. If all regional quota is zero instead, the3,000 annual expense tranches still generate450 in2026/2027/2028; record the separate450 credit vintages and their2030/2031/2032 expiry years, subject throughout to the continuing-rental requirements.

For a fresh year in which works relief450 and market-entry relief500 both qualify and only600 regional quota remains after earlier deductions, AEAT's smaller-first rule uses450 works then150 market-entry, leaving350 of the latter as its own four-year credit balance. Conversely, works900 across independently qualifying property bases and market-entry500 uses500 market-entry then100 works, leaving800 works-credit carry. Keep the two types and each property's ongoing conditions separate. These controls assume no competing earlier credit vintages.

**Binding vintage ledger, labelled own-statute interpretation.** Paragraphs 22.7 and 23.5 expressly make a still-valid earlier quota-limited credit deductible during its four following years, until exhausted. Exercise that earlier carry right first within the same deduction type, charge it against the actual remaining quota, and then use the newly generated credit. Its residual is then unabsorbed because quota is exhausted, rather than voluntarily omitted while quota is available. For the supplied ordinary case, use the earlier expiry first and retain each origin and expiry in the return working papers. This is a disclosed reasoned application of the concurrent rights in these own provisions, **not a legislated universal FIFO rule**, an express agency vintage-allocation ruling or a formal election/change-of-election procedure. The sources name no compulsory current-first rule. They also do not establish that every other allocation is impermissible; a claim that a different order is legally compulsory remains a precise interpretation question ([own 22.7/23.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); [AEAT works balances](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/gastos-derivados-adecuacion-inmueble-vacio-vivienda.html), [fixed-credit balances](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/arrendamiento-viviendas-vacias.html)).

**Active 2026 works-credit separator.** A sole owner first leases one qualifying home in 2025 after 9,000 eligible, personally paid, invoiced costs. All existing vacancy, completion, value, tenant and continuing-rental conditions hold. Initial rent is 800 per month, so the separate 500 credit is unavailable; this does not disqualify the works credit. The 2025 permitted 3,000 base creates 450 credit; only 100 quota is available, leaving 350 of 2025 credit, last use 2029, plus 6,000 expense base. In 2026 another 3,000 of that original expense base creates 450 new credit. After other applicable deductions, available quota is 500. Apply the valid 2025 credit 350 first, then 150 of 2026 credit: carry **300 of 2026 credit, last use 2030**, plus the separately remaining 3,000 original expense base, last use 2027 under the single-home two-following-year rule. The 2026 aggregate usage 500 is the same under a current-first allocation, but that alternative would leave 300 of older 2025 credit expiring 2029. The supplied method identifies its actual selection and cannot report both favourable balances. For the earlier generic example with older 350 expiring 2030 and current 450 expiring 2031, the same disclosed selection leaves current 300 expiring 2031; the alternative current-first allocation leaves older 300 expiring 2030.

**Active 2026 fixed-credit separator.** A sole owner first lets home A in 2025 and home B in 2026, each previously empty for a year, on independent qualifying housing leases at 600 per month including annexes, to unrelated tenants. Both continuing-rental conditions and the maximum-three-home condition hold, and no works costs create a competing works credit. The 2025 fixed 500 credit used 200 of quota, leaving 300 with 2029 expiry. Available 2026 quota is 600: exercise that old 300 first, then 300 of home B's new 500; carry **200 of 2026 fixed credit, last use 2030**. Do not generate another 500 for home A merely because its lease continues. These are distinct claimant/home/credit-origin entries, not the works expense-base register.

**Binding multi-home expense-base selection, same labelled interpretation.** Paragraph 22.5 keeps amounts excluded by the combined 6,000 base available within four following years. Use the supported earlier-expiring eligible expense tranches first, within each home’s 3,000 annual and 9,000 total ceilings and the 6,000 combined annual ceiling, then admit current tranches to remaining capacity. The retained current expense is carried because the statutory base ceiling excludes it. The source grants those dated rights without specifying a compulsory within-base vintage priority. This method does not reclassify an already generated tax credit as expense, extend an existing expiry merely because the claim is carried again, or treat an omitted/unsupported invoice as automatically authorised carry ([own 22.2/3/5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); [AEAT property/base capture](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_11-galicia/10_11_20-gastos-derivados-adecuacion-inmueble-vivienda.html)).

A sole claimant has only three rentable homes A/B/C, each wholly owned, and no public aid. All qualifying works, vacancy, value, tenancy and continuing-rental facts are documented. A and B each incur 9,000 eligible costs, complete works and first let in 2025. C incurs 3,000 eligible costs, completes works and first lets in 2026. All rents initially exceed 700, isolating works relief from fixed 500 relief. There is enough regional quota to absorb every nominal works credit in this expense-base example. The 2025 two-home calculation admitted 3,000 for A and 3,000 for B; their remaining 6,000 each are genuine expense balances excluded by the combined ceiling, with 2029 expiry under 22.5. In 2026 each old home has a 3,000 annual tranche and current C has 3,000, so 9,000 competes for 6,000 capacity:

|2026 property tranche|Origin/last expense-use year|Eligible before combined ceiling|Base selected|Expense excluded by combined ceiling|
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
|A, earlier balance|2025/2029|3,000|3,000|0 of this annual tranche|
|B, earlier balance|2025/2029|3,000|3,000|0 of this annual tranche|
|C, current cost|2026/2030|3,000|0|3,000|

The selected 2026 base 6,000 generates **900** credit, fully used. After that use, A and B each retain 3,000 expense with the original 2029 expiry; C retains 3,000 expense expiring 2030. A current-first allocation would instead select C 3,000 and only 3,000 of A/B, leaving 9,000 of older expense expiring 2029. It would produce the same 900 current credit but a different expense ledger. In the supplied chronological selection, using A/B's last 3,000 each in 2027 and C’s 3,000 in 2028 gives 900 and 450 nominal credits, respectively; the total base across all years is 21,000 and total relief 3,150. None of the individual 9,000 lifetime property ceilings is exceeded. If the allowed 2028 C-base credit cannot be absorbed for quota, it is a **2028 credit** with 2032 last use; the original expense amount does not remain deductible a second time.

The ledgers need actual invoices/payment and grant records, property and claimant identity, qualifying first-lease year, original statutory exclusion, prior return/working-paper balances, amounts already used and continuing conditions. Neither an unclaimed grant nor a voluntary omission proves a carry trigger. Establish entitlement and any required original-claim correction instead of inventing a balance. The two rental credits began in 2025 under [Ley 5/2024 art. 2/DF3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2025-2145); [Ley 5/2025 art. 2/DF3.2a](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-3385) makes the current works lease-year wording effective from 1 January 2025. The supplied hypothetical 2026 carry examples therefore use 2025/2026 origins, not fictional 2024 credits. Their last-use years do not restart on each annual use. The existing AEAT smaller-first rule **between the two deduction types** remains separate; the within-type fixtures above isolate one type. Mixed-type balances where a disputed reading of the amount used for that comparison changes priority, or equal-expiry property entries with different continuing-entitlement facts, require that precise additional attribution assessment. These are source-derived hypothetical calculations with stated upstream quotas, not official worked examples or real taxpayer records. No observed 2026 software result is asserted.

Early lease termination can preserve entitlement if a new qualifying housing contract starts within six months and the combined contracts total at least three years ([art.5.Veintidós.6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

The separate 500 empty-home credit instead requires a year empty **before the lease**, the 700 monthly rent ceiling including annexes, no related tenant and no more than three owned/usufruct rentable homes excluding the habitual home/garages/storerooms. Share by property participation. It has the same statutory replacement-contract rescue where total lease duration reaches three years and a four-following-year insufficient-tax carry while qualifying rental conditions remain satisfied. Do not import the works credit's 250,000 value ceiling or 3,000 expense base into this fixed credit ([art. 5.Veintitres](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

Aid routes are distinct. Wildfire relief concerns specified Decreto 76/2025 aid for the 2025 events, not any disaster payment. High-level sport requires formal regional recognition and non-economic-activity sporting income. ALS includes the specified diagnosis/phenotype aid; thalidomide relief has its separate recipient/event period and retroactive 2023 effect. Each requires actual general-BI inclusion and the statutory BL/average-rate calculation. The attributable-tax worksheet under The base supplies mixed general/savings, minimum-spillover and global-base-reduction attribution. Common employment-cost allocation to thalidomide aid, the stated signed-income boundary and historical correction administration remain precisely reserved; no flat percentage is substituted ([art. 5.Dieciseis/Diecinueve/Veinticuatro/Veintiseis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); [Ley 5/2025 final provision 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-3385)).

### Galicia: investment and research gifts

| Credit | Amount or calculation | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ordinary new/recent company subscription | 30%, cap 6,000; qualifying enhanced investment **45%, cap 9,000** under the statutory innovation/research routes | 2026 | [Art. 5.Nueve](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| New/recent company capital, loan or personal guarantee | 30%, **combined cap 20,000**; qualifying enhanced investment **45%, cap 35,000**, subject to the separate conditions | 2026 | [Art. 5.Diez](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Specified expansion-market shares | 15%, **total credit cap 4,000**, divided equally between investment year and following three years; no-more-than-10% ownership and at least three-year holding | 2026 entitlement and scheduled instalments | [Art. 5.Once](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Qualifying research/innovation money gifts | 25%, capped at **10% of regional gross tax**, not a 10%-of-income expense base | 2026 | [Art. 5.Doce](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Qualifying agricultural entity investment/finance | 20%, combined cap 20,000; five-year investment/finance/guarantee conditions | 2026 | [Art. 5.Quince](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |
| Exclusively qualifying declared-interest project company | 20% of eligible formation/capital-increase subscription, **cumulative base cap 10,000 per entity across years** | 2026 | [Art. 5.Veintiuno](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5) |

Choose the applicable investment route: arts. 5.Nueve/Diez/Once are incompatible on the **same investment**, the agricultural route has its own incompatibility with those routes, and declared-interest project capital cannot receive another art. 5 credit on the same capital investment. Both enhanced rates mean **30% + 15 percentage points = 45%**, with the enhanced route's cap, not a 15% uplift of the computed credit. AEAT describes the ordinary and enhanced category caps as independent; do not add both caps to relieve the same investment twice. Enhancement requires a proved innovative-SME seal, registered IEBT technological-employment initiative or qualifying university/research participation ([art. 5.Nueve/Diez](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); [AEAT subscription route](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/inversion-adquisicion-acciones-participaciones-sociales.html), [financing route](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-galicia/inversion-adquisicion-acciones-participaciones.html)).

The Nueve subscription route requires an SA/SL/labour company/cooperative; aggregate claimant/spouse/third-degree relatives' capital or votes between **1% and 40%** throughout three following years, with the statutory two-member labour/cooperative upper-limit exception. Maintain regional registered/tax domicile, genuine non-patrimonial economic activity and at least one full-time general-regime employee habitually resident in Galicia throughout three years. For a capital increase, formation must be within the preceding three years and regional-resident average employment must rise by at least one equivalent during the specified 24 months against the preceding twelve, then be maintained another 24 months. Use a public deed and hold shares three years ([art. 5.Nueve](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

The Diez financing route differs. Its qualifying full-time regional-resident employee can be hired within two years for at least a year; its capital-increase employment increment is maintained for another **twelve**, not twenty-four, months. Labour/cooperative exceptions apply as the statute states. Loans/guarantees need no shareholding, but any existing ownership must respect 40%, and the loan/guarantee amount must be **more than 1% of company net assets**. Loans must run **more than five years**, with principal amortisation no more than 20% annually; guarantees span the entire secured operation and at least five years. The investor may sit on the board but, except for the statutory labour/cooperative cases, cannot manage or have an employment relationship for ten years. Use the public deed, three-year share hold and regional domicile/activity conditions. Do not import Nueve's stricter employee timetable into Diez or waive Diez's finance/management conditions ([art. 5.Diez](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

Expansion-market relief requires a qualifying subscribed capital increase, a non-patrimonial regional registered/tax-domiciled company, a public deed and maintenance of ownership/domicile/activity conditions through the three-year hold. The 4,000 is a **total** credit spread into four equal annual parts, not a new annual 4,000 claim. Agricultural relief includes qualifying forestry/joint-management, exclusively agricultural/cooperative/land-recovery entities, reserves, loans/guarantees and the statutory participation accounts. Execute a public deed; retain investment for five years from the day after the deed, with financing maturity at least five years, at most 20% annual principal amortisation and guarantees maintained five years. Apply its ten-year no-management/no-employment condition with the stated labour/cooperative exceptions; non-cash valuation remains a specialist gap. Declared-interest entities need the exclusive statutory project purpose and formal declaration under Law 2/2024, not merely a beneficial local project ([art. 5.Once/Quince/Veintiuno](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).

Research gifts must be money to eligible Galician university-linked/public-promoted research centres or qualifying Law 49/2002 research-and-knowledge-dissemination bodies under the incorporated EU definition. Keep documentary proof; beneficiaries report donor identities and amounts to Atriga in the **first twenty days of the following year** ([art. 5.Doce](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5)).


### Comunitat Valenciana: applicable text, income tests and family credits

Use **Ley 13/1997 art. 4** for 2026. The health/sport amendments in **DL 1/2026 arts. 2–3** and the music credit in **DL 2/2026 art. 2** expressly apply to accruals from **1 January 2025**, so they are already available throughout 2026, including the increased income limits. Both decrees were validated by the Corts. For the six provisions replaced by **DL 14/2025 art. 65**, that text was effective from **30 December 2025 through 2 July 2026**; **Ley 3/2026 art. 82** replaces it from **3 July 2026**. Its disability-event wording and tenant compatibility cross-reference differ as described below. Do not apply the year-end wording automatically to an earlier short period ending on death ([current art. 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4); [DL 14/2025 art. 65/final provision 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2025-90320); [Ley 3/2026 art. 82/derogation/final provision 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-15683); [DL 1/2026](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2026-90037), [validation](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2026-90079); [DL 2/2026](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2026-90041), [validation](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2026-90069)).

Here **B = general BL + savings BL**, without subtracting personal/family minima. `I/J` means individual/joint filing. The following factors phase the stated **credit amount or credit limit**, not every expenditure base or percentage indiscriminately. At an upper endpoint the factor is zero. Test each claimant's filing position; do not double a joint-return limit without authority.

| Test | Income ceiling and factor | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ordinary gate and factor F | B ≤ **30,000 I / 47,000 J**; I: `F=clamp(1-(B-27000)/3000,0,1)`; J: `F=clamp(1-(B-44000)/3000,0,1)` | 2026 | [Art. 4.Cuatro first paragraph/Cinco.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Special-category large/monoparental family factor S | B ≤ **35,000 I / 58,000 J**; I: `S=clamp(1-(B-31000)/4000,0,1)`; J: `S=clamp(1-(B-54000)/4000,0,1)` | 2026 | [Art. 4.Cuatro second paragraph/Cinco.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Health, sport and music factor H | B ≤ **60,000 I / 78,000 J**; I: `H=clamp(1-(B-54000)/6000,0,1)`; J: `H=clamp(1-(B-72000)/6000,0,1)`; phase the respective caps | 2026, amendments retrospective to 2025 accruals | [Art. 4.Uno.ac–ae](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |

F applies to **a–i** (general-category d only), **k/l, n/ñ, u/v/w/y/ab**. S replaces F for special-category d. It does not impose a general 30,000/47,000 ceiling on j/m/o, gifts, t, x, z, aa or the additional provisions: those use their own conditions. For rural relief, art. 4.Seis defines an at-risk municipality by entitlement to the **Municipal Cooperation Fund against depopulation in the accrual budget year or preceding year**, under Law 5/2023. Do not replace the statutory list with an assumed population threshold ([art. 4.Cuatro–Seis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

| Credit | Amount before applicable phasing/sharing | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Birth/adoption | **600 first / 750 second / 900 third and later child**, in the event year and each of the next **two** years; F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.a.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Fostering/delegated adoption guardianship | Same **600/750/900** for a full year, prorated by qualifying days; F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.a.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Multiple birth/adoption | **246 per qualifying multiple event**, not per child; F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Disability at birth/adoption/fostering/delegated guardianship | **246** if the only qualifying disabled child/ward; **303** if living with another person generating this credit; F | 2026, disability wording change 3 July | [Art. 4.Uno.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Large or monoparental family | **330 general category** with F; **660 special category** with S | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.d](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Under-three regular nursery/first-cycle infant care | **15%**, cap **297 per child**, phased by F; cap prorated by under-three days and divided equally between entitled declaring taxpayers | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.e](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Work/family reconciliation | **460 per qualifying child/ward annually**, monthly proration and Social Security ceiling; F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.f](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Disabled taxpayer aged at least 65 | **197**, disability at least **33%**, no exempt benefit by reason of that disability; F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.g](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Older/disabled ascendant | **197 per qualifying ascendant**, F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.h](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Indefinitely employed household carer | **50%** of paid household-worker contributions for qualifying months; minor cap **660 one / 1,100 two or more or monoparental family**; ascendant cap **330 one / 550 two or more**; add qualifying child/ascendant caps, phase the combined cap by F, and share only between two entitled employers hiring carers for the same relatives | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.i](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Two or more minimum-entitled descendants | **10% of regional tax remaining after the other statutory credits**; aggregate family BI test below | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.t](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| School materials | **110 per qualifying child/ward**, F and unemployment/fostering-day proration | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.v](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Residence in an at-risk municipality | **330**, plus **132/198/264** for one/two/three or more minimum-entitled descendants; no ordinary F income gate | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.aa/Seis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |

Birth/adoption/foster relief requires descendant-minimum entitlement. For the second/third-and-later fostering or delegated-guardianship amount, the preceding children counted for ordering must have been held for **more than 183 days**. Exclude a child adopted during the year from the foster route; use the birth/adoption route where entitled. Share the applicable amount equally between entitled declaring taxpayers. The birth/adoption amount continues for two following years; the fostering amount follows actual qualifying days. Multiple adoption requires at least two adoptions on the same date. The event credits a/b/c are compatible with one another and d under the express statutory conditions ([art. 4.Uno.a–d](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For c, disability is at least **65%**, or intellectual/mental disability at least **33%**. For accruals through **2 July 2026**, DL 14/2025 expressly requires permanent recognition of that intellectual/mental disability; Ley 3/2026's replacement c omits that adjective from **3 July**, although the separate permanent-recognition wording remains in h/i/l/n. Retain the competent disability certificate and apply the judicial/pension assimilations in DA 6 where they actually fit ([DL 14/2025 art. 65](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2025-90320); [Ley 3/2026 art. 82](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-15683); [DA 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#dasexta)).

For d, require the recognised large/monoparental category at accrual and cohabitation with the other family members; share equally between entitled declaring taxpayers. For e, the parents/adopters/carers must undertake employment or self-employment producing employment/economic income. Use regular custody in a qualifying nursery/first-cycle infant centre, not occasional babysitting ([art. 4.Uno.d/e](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For f, count the **third-birthday month through the month before the fifth birthday**, provided the descendant-minimum and other conditions hold at each month end; a birthday on the month's last day has the same result. Work registration on any day satisfies that month's registration condition. Start with `460 × qualifying months / 12`, apply F, and limit the deduction to gross Social Security/alternative-mutual dues accrued **from the third birthday through the day before the fifth birthday**, ignoring contribution bonuses. The contribution ceiling is global for the whole deduction: count gross dues accrued in the current tax period within the applicable third-to-fifth-birthday intervals, without counting the same dues twice for overlapping children; monthly testing determines each child’s entitlement. For two children qualifying all year and 500 of eligible dues, the ceiling gives `min(920,500) = 500`, before any other applicable limit. Thus a third birthday on 31 March gives ten age-eligible months, March–December: `460 × 10 / 12 = 383.33` before F and the contribution ceiling. A fifth birthday on 31 March gives two months, January–February: `460 × 2 / 12 = 76.67` before those limits ([art. 4.Uno.f, Cuatro and Cinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4); [AEAT reconciliation calculation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_15-comunitat-valenciana/10_15_6-conciliacion-trabajo-vida-familiar.html)).
