The normal claimant is the working mother/female foster carer; the father succeeds to the pending right on her death or exclusive custody, and a male foster carer can qualify where there is no female foster carer. Share equally if several declaring taxpayers qualify. Adoption/guardianship instead uses the **fourth and fifth years after Civil Registry registration**, irrespective of the minor's age; fostering uses those years after the administrative resolution and requires the arrangement still to exist at accrual. ([art. 4.Uno.f](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4); [AEAT reconciliation guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunitat-valenciana/conciliacion-trabajo-vida-familiar.html)).

For h, the direct ascendant by blood, affinity or adoption must be **over 75**, or **over 65** with disability at least **65%** or permanently recognised intellectual/mental disability at least **33%**; non-exempt annual income cannot exceed **8,000**, and an IRPF return with income **over 1,800** blocks the claim. Require cohabitation for at least half the period; a dependent disabled ascendant in a specialist institution counts under the statute. Determine personal/family circumstances at accrual. Equal-degree entitled claimants share equally; the nearer degree takes priority unless its non-exempt income is at most 8,000, in which case the next degree can qualify. For i, require an indefinite household-employment contract, actual contributions, claimant employment/economic income, and a qualifying under-five minimum-entitled child or qualifying older/disabled minimum-entitled ascendant on the last day of each relevant month. **i is incompatible with e, f and h**. ([art. 4.Uno.h/i](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For i, calculate `min(50% × eligible paid contributions, F × (child cap + ascendant cap))`. Count contributions for months whose last day meets the statutory care and work-income conditions. Use a child cap of 660 for one qualifying under-five child, or 1,100 for two or more or a qualifying monoparental family; use an ascendant cap of 330 for one, or 550 for two or more. Use zero for a care category that does not qualify. Add the category caps for mixed care, including where the same worker cares for both generations. Apply the 50% rate once to the eligible contribution amount. For one child and one ascendant the cap is 990; for two children and one ascendant it is 1,430. A sole registered employer who pays the contributions keeps the whole applicable cap even if both parents claim the descendant minimum. If two taxpayers each employ carers for the same qualifying relatives, split the cap equally. The same cap amounts apply on a joint return. The credit is incompatible with e, f and h ([art. 4.Uno.i, Cuatro and Cinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4); [AEAT household-worker calculation and example](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunitat-valenciana/contratar-manera-indefinida-personas-afiliadas.html)).

The two-descendant credit t requires **aggregate BI no more than 30,000** for the entitled parents, qualifying descendants and other members of the relevant joint family units. This is not the claimant's B or salary. Apply 10% after the other regional deductions and statutory state-credit allocations, excluding t itself. School relief v requires a minimum-entitled child/ward in primary, compulsory-secondary or special education at a public/publicly funded private school, and unemployment with registration as a jobseeker of the claimant or cohabiting partner. Prorate by qualifying unemployment and fostering days; combined parents' unemployment days cannot exceed period days. Share equally between entitled claimants. For aa, share only the descendant addition where appropriate; it is incompatible with a/b/c **for the same descendant**, whereas the basic 330 is not excluded by that sentence ([art. 4.Uno.t/v/aa](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

### Comunitat Valenciana: homes, rent, works and small mobility

| Credit | Amount and principal limit | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Landlord offering a reference-price tenancy | **5% of gross qualifying rental income**, annual **base cap 3,300**; derived maximum credit **165** | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.j](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| First habitual home, age at most 35 at accrual | **5% of acquisition payments excluding interest**; ordinary gate and F apply | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.k](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Habitual-home acquisition by a disabled taxpayer | **5% of acquisition payments excluding interest**; disability at least **65%** or permanently recognised intellectual/mental disability at least **33%**; ordinary gate and F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.l](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Home acquisition/rehabilitation financed by a qualifying Generalitat grant | **112 per claimant**, or the alternative eligible urban-regeneration grant × regional general average rate under m | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.m](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Habitual-home tenancy/qualifying use assignment | **20%, cap 800**; **25%, cap 950** if one enhanced condition; **30%, cap 1,100** if two or more; phase cap by F, then relevant day/sharing rules | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.n](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Rental because of employment in another municipality | **10%, cap 224**, phased by F and prorated for days/sharing | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ñ](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Qualifying renewable installation | **40% habitual home/collective building installation**, **20% qualifying second home**; annual **base 8,800 per home**, net of public financing; unused credit carries **four following years** | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.o](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Increased habitual-home mortgage interest | **50% of positive statutory interest increase**, cap **100**, phased by F; no national home-investment deduction | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.u](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Conservation/quality/sustainability/accessibility works | **20%**, or **50% for accessibility serving disability at least 33%**; annual **base 5,500**, ownership allocation and F apply | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.w](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Culture-programme pass | **21%**, annual **base 165**; B strictly **below 50,000** | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.x](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Qualifying new urban bicycle/electrification kit/personal-mobility vehicle | **10%**; one vehicle per taxpayer per year; category base ceilings below, aid adjustment and F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.y/Cinco](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |

For j, the housing tenancy must begin during the period; where a previous tenancy lasted less than three years, the new tenant must differ. The monthly rent cannot exceed the official private-rental reference price and the deposit must be lodged before period end. Arts. j/k/l are expressly compatible. **Art. 4.Dos applies its net-wealth increase condition to j as well as k/l/m/o**: compare closing and opening checked wealth, disregard changes in value of assets retained at year end, and require the statutory increase covering the investment. Do not silently remove j from that express list. For m, require actual application of the grant money to the habitual home. If grant income is spread over several years under national timing rules, split the 112 across those years. The alternative average-rate route is confined to the specified demolition/replacement/reurbanisation/temporary-rehousing programme. Do not use the same grant money again in k/l; the distinct regional-general-rate aid method is supplied in the attributable-tax worksheet under The base ([art. 4.Uno.j–m/Dos](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For n, the enhanced conditions are **age at most 35**, the specified disability (65% or permanently recognised intellectual/mental 33%), and statutory gender-violence-victim status. Require an actually occupied habitual home, a contract **after 23 April 1998** lasting at least a year, and a receipt separating the use-assignment rental component where applicable. For at least half the tax period, neither the taxpayer nor the relevant family unit can own, individually or together, the whole ownership or real right to use another home **less than 50 km** away, unless judicial/administrative restrictions prevent its use. The statute preserves the specified over-65 owner's EVHA social-letting/collaborative-housing exception. A victim's aggressor spouse and the former shared home receive the specific exclusions in n. No entitlement to a home-investment deduction for the same period is allowed. Prorate the cap by tenancy days and days satisfying the enhanced circumstances; divide it equally between entitled declaring taxpayers, including community-property spouses paying with common funds even if only one is named on the lease ([art. 4.Uno.n](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For ñ, the rented regional home must be **more than 50 km** from the previous home, the employment in a different municipality, and the rent not reimbursed by the employer. Apply the ordinary income gate, day proration and shared cap. From **3 July 2026**, n expressly confirms compatibility with **ñ**. The operative DL 14/2025 wording through **2 July** instead refers to **o**; do not substitute ñ into that earlier cross-reference. The compatibility of n/ñ for such a short period is listed as unresolved ([DL 14/2025 art. 65](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2025-90320); [Ley 3/2026 art. 82](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-15683)).

For o, require the relevant registered grid self-consumption, certified off-grid solar/wind low-voltage installation, or solar/biomass/geothermal thermal installation; exclude installations legally mandatory under the building technical code and business-use property. Keep the invoice, registration/certification and non-cash payment evidence. Allocate collective installations by the community ownership coefficients. Track the **four following periods** for insufficient-tax carry independently from each year's 8,800-per-home investment base. For u, compare actual current/prior-year mortgage interest, not principal. Where the loan began in the preceding year, the statutory positive comparison is `current interest × days from the day after granting through prior 31 December / 360 − prior interest`, capped at actual current interest before applying the 50% and 100/F credit cap. This is not an assumed annualisation of last year's interest ([art. 4.Uno.o/u](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For w, require ownership or a real right to use the habitual home/building and works within the incorporated state/regional housing-plan criteria. Exclude ordinary garages, gardens, pools/sports annexes unless the statutory disabled-access exception applies, o-qualifying renewables and publicly financed expenditure. Apply the 5,500 base by ownership share and retain the provider NIF and non-cash payment evidence. For x, the pass must be issued by an entity adhering to the specified programme ([art. 4.Uno.w/x](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For y, the category maximum under **Order 5/2020 plus 10%** gives **550 urban conventional bicycle / 770 family cargo bicycle / 1,540 electric urban bicycle / 660 electrification kit / 495 personal-mobility vehicle**. Electric bicycles/kits must meet the authority's 250 W/25 km/h cut-off, approximately 50 km range, 36 V lithium-ion battery with minimum 9 Ah and the cited EPAC standard; personal-mobility vehicles require maximum 25 km/h, minimum 30 km range and minimum 7.8 Ah lithium-ion battery. Only the invoice-named purchaser claims; apportion the vehicle base if several taxpayers are entitled. A joint return does not create extra vehicles, but each actual acquiring taxpayer retains their one-vehicle entitlement. Article y excludes publicly financed cost from the category ceiling. The AEAT page's example subtracts aid from the enhanced category ceiling, but another sentence describes subtracting aid from actual cost and then capping: the differing-result aid-order case remains unconfirmed. Do not use a more favourable ordering without resolution ([art. 4.Uno.y](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4); [AEAT vehicle specifications, limits and conflicting aid wording](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunitat-valenciana/adquisicion-vehiculos-nuevos-pertenecientes-categorias-2020.html)).

**Preserved housing routes.** DT 1 retains former art. 3 bis for taxpayers entitled under national **DT 18**; the preserved article refers to the national complementary percentages rather than creating an unrestricted new-purchase credit. Apply the national grandfathering/base calculation in this Guide's legacy-home section and keep this route distinct from the additional regional k/l/m credits; detailed combined-claim allocation remains unresolved ([regional DT 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#dtprimera); [preserved art. 3 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202&b=10&tn=1&p=20021231#a3bis)).

**DA 13 remains a payment-year route for works actually performed in 2014/2015**, not a credit for newly performed 2026 works and not an automatically expired balance. Qualifying 2026 payments for those old works receive **10% for 2014 works / 25% for 2015 works**. The annual base is `4,500 × clamp(1-(B-23000)/2000,0,1)` I or `4,500 × clamp(1-(B-37000)/3000,0,1)` J, subject to a **5,000 cumulative base per home** and ownership shares. The AEAT implementation therefore limits positive claims to B below **25,000 I / 40,000 J**. Require qualifying habitual-home conservation/quality/sustainability/accessibility works, provider NIF and non-cash payment; exclude the specified annexes, o-eligible renewables and publicly financed cost. It can coexist with w for works from different eligible performance years; do not count the same works twice ([DA 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#dadecimotercera); [AEAT payment-year and accumulated-base guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunitat-valenciana/obras-conservacion-mejora-calidad-2014-2015.html)).

### Comunitat Valenciana: gifts, investment, health, sport and music

| Credit | Amount and principal limit | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ecological donations | **20% of first 250 + 25% of excess** eligible gifts | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.p](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Inventoried cultural-property gift, cash for its repair, or owner's qualifying work | Same **20% first 250 / 25% excess** within each statutory route; q.2/q.3 share the combined BL ceiling below | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.q](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Gifts promoting Valencian language | **20% first 250 / 25% excess** | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.r](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Other declared-social-interest cultural/scientific/non-professional-sport patronage, usufruct or loan for use | **25%** of statutory eligible value; first **250 at 20% instead** if the national art. 68.3.a gift deduction applies | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.s](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Formation/capital-increase subscription or qualifying cooperative capital | **30%, cap 6,600**; enhanced **45%, cap 15,000**; unused credit carries **three following periods** | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.z](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Fertility treatment | **100**, qualifying woman aged **40–45**, authorised clinic/centre; F | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ab](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Complex chronic/rare disease treatment and care | Actual eligible cost up to **100**, or **150** large/monoparental family; cap × H | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ac.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Acquired brain injury/Alzheimer treatment and care | Actual eligible cost up to **100**, or **150** large/monoparental family; cap × H | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ac.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Non-cosmetic dental treatment; mental-health care | **30%**, separate **150** caps for each category, each × H | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ac.3/4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Prescription lenses/non-premounted glasses/contact lenses and solution | **30%, cap 100 × H** | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ac.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Sport/healthy activities | **30%** ordinarily; **50%** if claimant over 65 or disability at least 33%; **100%** if over 75 or disability at least 65%; cap **150 × H** in every case | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ad](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |
| Music promotion/training | Eligible expenditure itself, cap **150 × H**, rather than a 30% sport rate | 2026 | [Art. 4.Uno.ae](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4) |

For p, use eligible regional public bodies or qualifying registered environmental nonprofits with the exclusive statutory purposes. For q, verify the cultural inventory and eligible patronage recipient, including the incorporated comparable-EU route. For r, verify the public/local/university/art-school recipient or qualifying Valencian-language census entry at the end of the period. The **combined bases of q.2/q.3/r/s cannot exceed 30% of total BL**; p and q.1 are not added to that particular list. Retain the recipient certificate required for p/q.1/q.2/r/s: donor/recipient identity and NIF, legal beneficiary status, date/amount or valuation, destination, and the required document/evidence and irrevocability statements. Revocation triggers repayment of the enjoyed relief plus statutory interest ([art. 4.Dos/Tres](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4); [AEAT combined ceiling](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunitat-valenciana/requisito-conjunto-determinadas-deducciones-autonomicas-donativos.html)).

For s, use actual cash; for contributed property/rights, the statutory book/Wealth Tax basis; for real-property usufruct, **4% of cadastral value annually**, day-prorated; for securities usufruct, the relevant dividends/interest; and for other usufruct, legal interest applied to its statutory value. Cultural assets require the competent valuation board. Values cannot exceed market value. A loan for use uses **4% annually** of the competent asset valuation, or of cadastral value for the specified premises, day-prorated. Keep the social-interest declaration and do not substitute an invoice price for an incorporated statutory valuation ([art. 4.Uno.s](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For z, eligible recipients are SA/SL/labour companies or cooperatives, with regional registered/tax domicile and genuine non-patrimonial activity maintained for **three years**. Exclude continuation of the same activity under different ownership. Require at least one full-time general-regime employee for three years; for capital increases, the company must ordinarily have been formed within the preceding **five years**, or **seven** for the specified strategic/own-technology startup category, and increase average full-time-equivalent employment by at least one during the specified following **24 months** against the preceding twelve, maintained another **24 months**. The employee and increase requirements are waived for labour companies and worker cooperatives. Execute the public deed identifying investors and amounts. A post-formation cooperative contribution may instead use a secretary's certificate approved by the president, with notarised signatures or the specified qualified digital signature; one annual certificate can list all subscription/payment dates. Hold the investment three years. The enhanced rate requires an innovative SME/research-body participation, an at-risk municipality tax domicile or qualifying regional emerging-company status. Keep the **three following utilisation periods** separate from the three-year retention obligation ([art. 4.Uno.z](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

The health categories ac are mutually compatible. They cover the claimant, spouse and persons generating descendant/ascendant minima, with equal **base** sharing if two declaring taxpayers pay for another person's care. Exclude medical-insurance premiums, Social Security/replacement-body reimbursable benefits and the specified charitable contributions already using national gift relief. Medical services must be supplied by registered health establishments/professionals. Keep the invoice, non-cash payment and competent clinician report identifying the correct statutory category. Apply `min(category percentage × eligible allocated expenditure, category cap × H)` for percentage routes; for ac.1/ac.2 use actual eligible allocated cost up to the H-adjusted cap. Do not phase the percentage as well as the cap ([art. 4.Uno.ac](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

For ad, eligible spending includes qualifying gym/club/federation/recreation memberships, mandatory equipment for federated sport, sports trainers and the listed traumatology, nutrition/dietetics, physiotherapy and podiatry services. Apply the enhanced percentage by the **claimant's** age/disability. Expenditure can concern the claimant, spouse and minimum-entitled ascendants/descendants; share the base equally where two taxpayers qualify for another person's costs. Require invoice and non-cash payment. For ae, include registered public/private music conservatories/schools, membership of FSMCV-integrated music societies, official training/master/course/seminar fees, instruments/scores within the specified teaching/promotion context net of public grants, and concerts organised by the eligible listed entities. Music covers the claimant, spouse and minimum-entitled **descendants**, without importing ad's ascendants. Share the base equally where required and retain non-cash payment evidence ([art. 4.Uno.ad/ae](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

**Additional-provision routes and old balances.** DA 17 provides the regional general average rate multiplied by taxable qualifying Generalitat COVID ERTE/reduced-hours aid or the specified Order 5/2020 bicycle aid. Its COVID-research and crisis-funding donations retain their own **20% first 150 / 25% excess** schedule; do not import the newer ordinary-gift 250 tranche. Use the named programme/recipient and retain the exact award, taxable allocation and gift evidence; mixed-income tax attribution remains unresolved ([DA 17](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#da)).

The separate **DANA DL 12/2024** credits generate new entitlements only for **2024/2025**, not 2026 expenditure. Previously established home-repair unused credit can carry for **four following periods**; qualifying equity unused credit for **three**. Preserve originating amount, use and expiry: 2024/2025 home balances expire after **2028/2029**, equity after **2027/2028**, respectively. Check continuing original conditions and available regional tax. The ordering of overlapping carried DANA and ordinary investment credits remains unresolved ([DL 12/2024 arts. 4.5/5.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=DOGV-r-2024-90200); [AEAT home balances](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunitat-valenciana/dirigidas-personas-afectadas-dana/destinar-cantidades-paliar-danos-materiales-habitual.html), [equity balances](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunitat-valenciana/dirigidas-personas-afectadas-dana/aportaciones-fondos-propios-entidades-que-economicas.html)).

**Payment evidence.** DA 16 requires credit/debit card, bank transfer, named cheque or deposit into a credit-institution account for the listed e/k/l/m/n/ñ/o/p/q/r/s/v/w/x/y/z cash payments and DA 17.c/d. Health/sport/music expressly incorporate the same permitted means. A receipt for cash does not satisfy these provisions. Keep this payment test separate from the income, wealth and retention conditions ([DA 16](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#dadecimosexta); [art. 4.Uno.ac–ae](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4)).

## The base

Build a deduction ledger with: legal article/year, entitlement facts, eligible expenditure net of required subsidies/reimbursements, ownership/payment allocation, income-test inputs, nominal credit, article cap, interactions, quota used, unused credit, legally authorised carry-forward/expiry and conditions still to maintain. An unverified input remains an unresolved fact; it is not silently replaced with individual filing or zero entitlement.

1. Establish BI, BL, family unit and minimum entitlements from the underlying IRPF calculation. Compare each credit with its own income definition and threshold. A minimum subtracted for a particular regional eligibility test is not also deducted from every other income test.
2. Compute state and regional gross tax using their own scales and minima. Obtain the regional total before credits, including its savings component where relevant. Apply the state/regional allocation of shared national credits under LIRPF arts. 67 and 77.
3. Calculate the regional credits' eligible bases, rates, annual/per-child/per-home/per-taxpayer caps, shared claims and express incompatibilities. Apply Madrid art. 14 to the remaining amount after the other ordinary credits; apply art. 17 bis after the other regional credits. Do not calculate either as a percentage of gross salary.
4. Regional liquid tax cannot be negative under art. 77.2. Tax not available to absorb a credit is not automatically a refund. Preserve unused amounts only when the particular law permits it, such as Madrid art. 17 bis or Cataluña art. 612-12; do not carry unused ordinary Madrid birth/rent relief into a later year without authority.
5. Add the two liquid-tax halves and then apply the relevant subsequent credits, withholding and instalments at their statutory positions. The result after regional credits is **cuota líquida autonómica**, not a separate regional cuota diferencial ([LIRPF arts. 67, 77 and 79](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a79)).

### Regional attributable tax: separate general and savings calculations

Use this worksheet for the social-housing, work-move and public-aid credits identified below. Keep each credit's entitlement, year, income ceiling, eligible claimant and settlement position already stated in its regional catalogue. The computation covers zero income, general-only income, savings-only income, mixed general/savings income, personal/family minimum spillover and ordinary base reductions. It does not turn an unestablished eligible-income numerator into zero.

**Calculate the quotas first.** Let `G` be general BI after its integration/compensation calculation, `B` the nonnegative general BL actually subject to the general scale, and `S` savings BL. Apply the joint-return reduction where due, then the other base reductions in their statutory order and permitted basket, before calculating tax. A pension reduction reduces the general base within its own limits; it does not automatically reduce savings income. The permitted remainder of the compensatory-pension reduction and joint-return reduction follows its own savings rule. Personal/family minima are a later **tax calculation**, not another subtraction from `G` or `B` ([LIRPF arts.48–50 and84](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a50)).

For each tax half `j`, determine its own legally applicable minimum `Mj`, general scale `Fj` and savings scale `H`. Its general minimum portion is `min(B,Mj)`; its savings minimum portion is `min(S,max(0,Mj−B))`. Thus `QGj=Fj(B)−Fj(min(B,Mj))` and `QSj=H(S)−H(min(S,max(0,Mj−B)))`, calculated in cents. Use `QGC+QSC` for total regional gross quota. State and regional minimum amounts can differ, so calculate their spillovers separately. If `B=0`, general quota is zero; if `0<B≤Mj`, that half's general quota is zero while savings quota can remain positive. Do not tax `B−Mj` at the bottom of the scale or subtract the minimum twice ([arts.56,63,66,74 and76](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a56)).

For a credit expressly using general average rates, calculate `rE=100×QGE/B` and `rC=100×QGC/B`, separately, with two decimal places. The official return implementation **discards excess rate decimals**; for example,8.8691…% is8.86%,not8.87%. If `B=0`, there is no positive general-tax amount to allocate and the general-income credit is zero; do not divide by zero or use the savings rate. The worked controls below use these expressed rates only for credits whose wording calls for them. A quota-attribution cap instead retains the quota ratio until the attributed tax is calculated ([arts.63.2/74.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a74); [official public RentaWEBOpen2025](https://www2.agenciatributaria.gob.es/wlpl/PARE-RW25/OPEN/index.zul?EJER=2025&TACCESO=COLAB), version1.19, checked27October2026).

**Establish the numerator before distributing base reductions.** `N` is the selected qualifying income actually included in general BI: reduced net rental income for social housing; reduced net employment/activity income of the entitled mover(s) for work moves; or the qualifying aid's included income amount. Deduct the income category's legally applicable expenses and reductions first. An activity grant is not automatically its gross cash amount: the Balearic emergency-aid input and Galician wildfire instructions require its attributable reduced net activity income. Keep the grant's income classification and any exemption separate from its credit. For an ordinary positive allocation with `0≤N≤G`, the selected amount in general BL is `A=round_to_cents(N×B/G)` if `G>0`; if no positive general base remains, its attributable general quota is zero. The minimum is already reflected in `QG`; do not subtract a second share of the minimum from `A` ([AEAT Balearic emergency-aid instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_4-illes-balears/10_4_19-determinadas-subvenciones-declaracion-zona-afectada.html); [Galician wildfire instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_11-galicia/10_11_22-subvenciones-incendios.html); controlled public-return calculations cited above).

| Provision and its actual tax measure | Ordinary attributable-tax calculation, before final available-quota settlement | Primary basis |
|---|---|---|
| Aragón social-housing landlord: regional general quota corresponding to the qualifying reduced net rental income | First calculate attributed quota `T=round_to_cents(QGC×N/G)`, then `30%×T`, rounded to cents. Keep the quota ratio until `T` is calculated; do not substitute the displayed regional average rate. Savings quota is excluded from `T` | [DL1/2005 art.110-13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOA-d-2005-90006#a11013); [exact AEAT rental numerator](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_2-aragon/10_2_12-arrendamiento-vivienda-social.html) |
| Castilla-La Mancha work move: regional quota from employment/activity income of entitled contributors | `min(500×eligible mover count,round_to_cents(QGC×N/G))`. Test the initial-period BL ceiling separately; it is not the attribution denominator | [Ley8/2013 art.12quater](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-6); [AEAT individual/joint method](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_7-castilla-mancha/10_7_16-traslado-vivienda-habitual.html) |
| Cantabria rural work move: the corresponding regional employment/activity quota | `min(500×eligible mover count,round_to_cents(QGC×N/G))`; retain the current article's separate territorial, residence and timing conditions | [DL62/2008 art.2.11.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCT-c-2008-90028#a2); [AEAT cap and joint aggregation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-cantabria/gastos-traslado-residencia-municipio-afectado-despoblamiento.html) |
| Canarias inter-island work move: the corresponding regional employment/activity quota | `min(300×eligible mover count,round_to_cents(QGC×N/G))`; its BI eligibility ceiling remains a separate test for the origin year | [DL1/2009 art.8](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a8); [AEAT cap and joint scope](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-canarias/trasladar-residencia-habitual-otra-isla-ae.html) |
| Asturias ALS aid; Extremadura ALS aid; Galicia wildfire, high-level-sport, ALS/phenotype and thalidomide aid: general-BI inclusion, BL amount and plural average rates | `round_to_cents(A×(rE+rC)/100)`. Use the sum of separately expressed state and regional **general** rates, with each provision's own eligible aid and income-category rules | [Asturias14quaterdecies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a1-18); [Extremadura11septies](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2018-8159#a1-20); [Galicia5.Dieciseis/Diecinueve/Veinticuatro/Veintiseis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2011-18161#a5); exact AEAT aid inputs and public-return controls |
| Illes Balears declared civil-emergency aid: aid in general BI and its amount in BL | `round_to_cents(A×(rE+rC)/100)`. Although art.3ter uses a singular average-rate expression, the official implementation uses both general rates; a regional-only rate gives a different amount | [Art.3ter](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-12); [AEAT input](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_4-illes-balears/10_4_19-determinadas-subvenciones-declaracion-zona-afectada.html); public-return controls |
| Cataluña612-13 thalidomide aid: aid in general BI and BL, plural average rates | Once the aid's legally attributable employment-income numerator `N` is established, apply the same `A` step and `round_to_cents(A×(rE+rC)/100)`. Other general income and savings income do not change that general-basket denominator into total BL | [Ley11/2026 art.37.1,612-13](https://www.boe.es/ccaa/dogc/2026/9706/f00001-00146.pdf#page=42); [LIRPF63.2/74.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a63) |
| Comunitat Valenciana urban-regeneration housing grant under4.Uno.m, and DA17.a/b qualifying aid: the specified aid amount times the regional general average rate | `round_to_cents(qualifying aid amount×rC/100)`. These clauses use the aid amount itself; do **not** insert the `B/G` reduction factor used by the BL-worded aid credits. Keep the112 alternative and its timing/incompatibility rules | [Art.4.Uno.m](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#a4); [DA17.a/b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1998-8202#da); [exact AEAT DA17.a input](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_15-comunitat-valenciana/10_15_27-ayudas-decreto-ley-32020.html) |

For joint work-move claims, `G`, `B` and `QGC` belong to the joint return. Sum only the reduced net work/activity income of contributors actually entitled to that move credit in `N`; a non-entitled spouse's income still enters the joint bases and quotas. Apply the one combined attributed-tax ceiling to the sum of the eligible movers'500 or300 amounts. Do not give every mover the entire joint quota or remove the joint-return base reduction from the tax calculation. The actual credit is then subject to the existing regional settlement rule, including sufficient remaining regional tax under LIRPF77.2.

**Discriminating numerical controls.** With general BI21,000, general BL20,700, savings BL1,000 and selected net income1,000, current ordinary under65 minima/scales give regional general quota1,660.50 in CLM,1,480.25 in Cantabria and1,564.21 in Canarias. The work-move tax ceilings are respectively79.07,70.49 and74.49; savings tax is not included in those numerators. With Aragón general quota1,629.94, the attributed quota is77.62 and the social-housing credit23.29. These calculations preserve the300 base reduction, rather than treating general BI and BL as interchangeable. Each assumes the catalogue's separate entitlement and income gates pass.

For included aid1,000 in the same21,000/20,700 bases, `A=985.71`. Asturias general average rates8.02% state and7.83% regional give156.24; Extremadura's current2026 scale gives8.02%+6.62% and144.31; Galicia's8.02%+7.47% give152.69. An aid input1,000.64 gives Galicia `A=986.35` and152.79, distinguishing cent-rounding of the liquidable amount from prematurely rounding a rate or retaining an unrounded aid base. Valencia's current2026 regional general rate7.42% instead gives74.20 on aid1,000, not73.14. For Balearic emergency aid, general BI25,000/BL23,500, included aid1,000 and savings BL10,000 give `A=940`, rates8.85%+8.86% and166.47. Removing savings changes neither those general rates nor the aid credit when no other income condition changes.

If general BL3,500 is below the applicable minimum and savings BL10,000 remains, both general rates and the general-income attribution are zero even though savings tax is positive. If general BI and BL are both zero, no general-income quota can support these credits. Savings-only income cannot create qualifying employment, rental or aid income. A taxpayer whose numerator or eligibility is unknown is a different case; do not replace that missing fact with zero. The allocation and rounding methods were checked against fictional official2025 OPEN controls; the figures above are recomputed under current2026 law. Where2026 scales differ, the2025 programme amounts are not carried forward. No2026 return campaign was tested. The numerical allocation controls do not establish the separate programme or rental-entitlement facts.

**Distinct2026 Balearic war-aid base.** Art.3quinquies.1 uses the regional general rate and the included liquidable-base aid amount. Its separate paragraph2 expressly prescribes, for an activity grant, the grant's share of the activity's total gross receipts multiplied by that activity's reduced net income: `Nactivity=grant/gross activity receipts×reduced net activity income`; its stated credit is `Nactivity×rC/100`, rounded to cents. Do not replace that explicit paragraph2 base with the gross grant or add a second `B/G` factor to it. For gross receipts100,000 including grant20,000, positive reduced net activity income30,000 and regional general rate8.86%, the paragraph2 credit is531.60. This is derived from the current2026 enactment, rather than from an earlier inflation-aid campaign excluding business receipts ([art.3quinquies.1–2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-15)).

**CLM individual signed-loss branch.** For an individual entitled mover, combine that person's reduced net employment and activity income in `N`; an activity loss is not omitted merely because the move was for employment. If a loss in another general-income category leaves positive `G` and `B` but makes that positive aggregate `N` exceed `G`, the CLM move attribution is limited to `QGC`: the credit before available-quota settlement is `min(500,QGC)`. It does not absorb savings quota. This is a legal application of the unchanged employment/activity-quota ceiling in art.12quater(1)(b), supported by actual fictional Renta2025 OPEN1.19 controls, rather than a tested2026 return campaign. For net wages20,000, net activity loss13,000 and savings1,000, `N=G=B=7,000` and `QGC=137.75`, so the credit is137.75. Replacing the activity loss with a general-capital loss13,000 gives `N=20,000>G=7,000`; the credit remains137.75, leaving95 savings quota. Adding a positive general gain1,000 makes `G=B=8,000` and `QGC=232.75`; the credit is232.75, still leaving95 savings quota. The under65 personal minimum is5,550. A further control retains `N=20,000`, `G=8,000` and savings1,000 and adds a valid individual pension contribution1,000: `B=7,000`, `QGC=137.75`, credit137.75 and regional liquid quota95. The first three controls have no global base reductions; the fourth confirms the separate pension-reduction step. No other credits apply. The current2026 scale and unchanged cap reproduce these amounts. Do not extend this individual CLM result to signed joint claims, other regions, an unestablished or nonpositive selected numerator, or shared wage/thalidomide expenses ([enacted cap, Law2/2021 DF9.Dosbis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2021-11513); [older BOE consolidation, operative art.12quater unchanged by the2026 amendment](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-1368#a1-6); [actual2026 amendment, art.18 and DF6](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/verArchivoHtml.do?ruta=2026%2F03%2F30%2Fhtml%2F2026_2465.html&tipo=rutaDocm) ([official PDF, physical pp.15–19 and24 read](https://docm.jccm.es/portaldocm/descargarArchivo.do?ruta=2026/03/30/pdf/2026_2465.pdf&tipo=rutaDocm)); [pension reduction, LIRPF51–52](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a52); [AEAT cap and individual/joint income wording](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_7-castilla-mancha/10_7_16-traslado-vivienda-habitual.html)).

**CLM joint return with one entitled mover and positive selected income above general BI.** Article 12quater(1)(c) selects employment/activity income belonging to the members who generate the deduction; a non-entitled spouse’s activity loss is not added to that selected numerator. Joint taxation nevertheless accumulates both spouses’ income: current employment and activity results offset within general income under LIRPF 48(a), before adding a positive non-transfer general gain under 48(b). The married-joint reduction is 3,400, applied to general BI first; the basic under 65 personal minimum is 5,550 once for the joint family, rather than twice (LIRPF 84(1),84(2).2–3,84(5),57 and61). Retain each member’s income attribution under LIRPF 11. For exactly one entitled mover, where that member’s combined positive employment/activity `N` exceeds positive joint general `G` and general `B` is positive, the work-move attribution is limited to `QGC`; the credit before available-quota settlement is `min(500,QGC)`. It does not absorb savings quota.

Separate fictional signed joint control: both spouses are under 65 and resident in CLM; only the wage-earner qualifies for the move credit. That member has net wages 20,000; the other spouse has net professional activity −9,000. There is also a taxable general non-transfer gain 1,000 and savings income 1,000, with no other base reduction, extra minimum or credit. Joint `G=12,000`; after 3,400 joint reduction, `B=8,600`. The single 5,550 personal minimum gives `QGC=289.75`; savings quota is 95. The selected eligible-member `N` remains 20,000, so the credit is 289.75 and regional liquid quota 95. Combining the non-entitled spouse’s loss into `N=11,000` would instead give 265.60 by a directly rounded aggregate ratio and is not the observed one-entitled-mover result. These are current-law calculations using the unchanged CLM cap/scale, supported by an [actual fictional Renta2025 OPEN1.19 joint control](https://www2.agenciatributaria.gob.es/wlpl/PARE-RW25/OPEN/index.zul?EJER=2025&TACCESO=COLAB). They are not a tested 2026 return campaign. With both spouses entitled, art.12quater(c) provides 500 per mover and one attribution limit for the combined eligible employment/activity income; that is one joint attributable-tax ceiling, rather than a sum of separately capped member deductions. Exact signed aggregation and cent rounding in that both-entitled population remain unresolved: with both spouses entitled on the otherwise unchanged first case, selected combined `N=11,000` gives an actual 2025 credit 265.61 versus 265.60 from final-cent aggregate rounding. Raising only the general non-transfer gain to 2,000 gives `G=13,000`, `B=9,600`, `QGC=384.75` and actual credit 325.55 versus 325.56 from that ratio. Those results do not certify an intermediate or per-member rounding algorithm ([enacted joint-member cap, DF9.Dosbis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2021-11513); [AEAT entitled-member and joint combined-limit wording](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_7-castilla-mancha/10_7_16-traslado-vivienda-habitual.html); [LIRPF 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a11), [48](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a48), [57/61](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a57) and [84](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a84); current 2026 cap/scale source chain as in the individual branch above).

**Wages and thalidomide aid: establish employment expenses before the regional numerator.** Compute the employment category under LIRPF 19 before allocating an included aid amount to a regional credit. Deduct the applicable article 19(2)(a)–(e) expenses, then the ordinary 2,000 under 19(2)(f), limited to employment income left after those other expenses. That ordinary amount is annual, not an allowance for each payer or receipt; joint filing does not multiply it by the number of family members. The article 20 reduction tests employment income after 19(2)(a)–(e), before 19(2)(f), and cannot make the resulting net employment income negative. Its other-income gate and current bands still apply. These are category rules, not a direction to allocate the ordinary 2,000 or article 20 reduction between wages and aid ([LIRPF 19–20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a19), [84(2)](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a84)).

The separate mobility increase is 2,000 in the residence-change year and the next: the claimant must be unemployed and registered with the employment office, then accept employment requiring an actual move to another municipality. The active-worker disability increase is 3,500, or 7,750 if the claimant needs third-person assistance, has reduced mobility or has disability of at least 65%; a disability of at least 33% and actual active employment must coexist. Receiving aid classified as employment income does not itself establish active employment. RIRPF 11(2) attributes each increase **exclusively to the employment generating it**, rather than to all employment receipts. For the applicable income ceiling, first apply any valid gross-income reductions, then deduct the expenses exclusive to that employment and the share of remaining expenses corresponding to the accepted contract's duration or the days of active employment with disability. This timing rule for the ceiling does not prescribe a gross-wage/aid allocation of the ordinary 2,000. The fixed increase is not itself prorated for a short eligible period, but eligible-period income limits its use. Recompute the mobility ceiling in each year: no income from the qualifying job in the second year gives no mobility expense that year. When both increases apply, their combined ceiling uses the union of the employment income generating one or both rights; do not count the same wages twice. Joint filing allows one mobility increase and one disability increase corresponding to the highest degree of disability among family members who qualify as active workers with disability; an inactive relative does not raise that amount. Each increase remains subject to its eligible members' income ceiling ([LIRPF 19(2)(f)](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a19), [RIRPF 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a11); [AEAT 2025 expense instructions, applying these unchanged provisions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c03-rendimientos-trabajo/rendimiento-neto-trabajo-integrar-base-imponible/fase-2-determinacion-rendimiento-neto/particular-analisis-gastos-articulo-19_2_f-lirpf.html)).

A control with already established taxable aid 396,000, full-year eligible active employment 15,000, salary-only compulsory contributions 1,500, no gross-income reduction and no other article 19(2)(a)–(e) expenses gives employment income 409,500 before 19(2)(f). The ordinary 2,000 and eligible disability increase 3,500 leave 404,000. Article 20 does not apply because its pre-19(2)(f) measure is 409,500. The 3,500 belongs entirely to the qualifying employment, not proportionately to aid. This does **not** establish how much of the ordinary 2,000 reduces the aid numerator. A separate ceiling control follows AEAT’s published 2025 example under the unchanged provisions applicable in 2026. The claimant has a recognised 34% disability throughout the year. A January job ends, leaving 1,000 of eligible income; the claimant is then unemployed and registered with the employment office, receives 6,000 of unemployment benefit, and accepts a new December job requiring a residence move to another municipality. The December job supplies 800 of eligible income. Both eligible-income amounts are already net of the expenses used in the enhancement ceiling. Nominal mobility/disability increases of 2,000+3,500 are limited to 1,800, not 5,500 or 7,800. The 6,000 unemployment benefit cannot enlarge that ceiling. These are expense controls, not a computed regional thalidomide credit.

**Balearic thalidomide enactment and rate boundary.** Law 4/2026 DF 67(2) added article 3 sexties. DF 72 brought the law into force on 14 June 2026, the day after its BOIB publication, while article 3 sexties expressly reaches fiscal 2023 onward. The July 21 corrigendum changes an unrelated DF 2 law citation. The new provision says to apply a singular average tax rate to the aid amount included in liquidable base, following general-BI inclusion; neither that operative text nor ATIB's enactment notice specifies whether the rate is combined state/regional or regional-only. It supplies no fixed percentage. Retain that exact question and the shared ordinary-expense/reduction question before computing this new credit; do not substitute a 2025 campaign formula or infer a rate from another aid credit ([Law 4/2026 DF 67(2)/DF 72](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-15579), [corrigendum](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOIB-i-2026-90195), [ATIB notice](https://atib.es/General/Novedad.aspx?idTexto=16987&idTipoTexto=1&lang=es)).

**Precise remaining boundaries.** Apart from the individual CLM branch and the separately supplied one-entitled-mover joint cap branch just identified, the worksheet does not prescribe allocation where signed category income or losses make the selected numerator exceed general BI; do not silently cap that numerator or impose a favourable loss priority. CLM joint claims outside that supplied one-entitled-mover positive-N-above-positive-G branch, including exact both-entitled signed aggregation and cent rounding, and the other regions remain unconfirmed. For thalidomide aid, RD574/2023 receipts are non-exempt employment income without the30% irregular-income reduction. If the aid is the only employment receipt, its employment numerator is that category's properly computed reduced net income. If wages and aid share the ordinary employment-expense allowance or employment-income reduction, the retrieved thalidomide provisions and instructions do not prescribe the share assigned to aid: that ordinary numerator remains to confirm, including for Cataluña. The general/savings, minimum and global-BL-reduction calculation above remains available once that numerator is established. Balearic3sexties' newly enacted singular average-rate wording still needs direct clarification of combined versus regional-only general rate; do not certify that new article solely by analogy with the implemented emergency provision. Historical correction administration remains separate ([LIRPF19–20](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a19); [AEAT147637](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/irpf/novedades-impuesto/novedades-publicadas-informa-2024/novedades-publicadas-informa-mes-septiembre.html); [Galicia thalidomide input](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-ayuda-presentacion/irpf-2025/10-cumplimentacion-irpf-deducciones-autonomicas/10_11-galicia/10_11_25-ayudas-recibidas-personas-afectadas-1985.html); [Balearic3sexties](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2014-6925#a3-17)).

### National credits are separate

The October 2026 amendment added the national habitual-home rental credit in **LIRPF art. 68.6**, deducted entirely from the **state** half by art. 67.1.a. The amendment takes effect on **8 October 2026**; the article measures rent paid in the tax period, rather than creating a regional credit. For BI below **33,007.20**, the credit is **10%** of eligible rent, with maximum base **11,630** where BI is at most **23,007.20**. Above that and below 33,007.20, maximum base is `11,630 − 1.163 × (BI − 23,007.20)`. For at least half the period neither the taxpayer nor a family-unit member can own, individually or jointly, the whole full ownership or a real use/enjoyment right in another home within **50 km**, except where an administrative/judicial decision prevents its use. Verify this property test before applying the formula ([RDL 29/2026 art. 6.Segundo.Tres–Cuatro and final provision 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823); [LIRPF arts. 67–68](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68)).

Do not stack the old national rental transition in DT 15 for the same home and period when art. 68.6 has been applied: the amendment expressly adds that exclusion. Calculate the other national credits below at their own positions.

### National startup subscription credit

**Check wealth before claiming this credit.** [LIRPF art. 70](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a70) requires the checked value of the taxpayer's wealth at period end to exceed its checked opening value by at least the investment amount. Disregard both increases and decreases in value during the period of assets that remain in the taxpayer's wealth at period end. Keep the opening/closing wealth reconciliation and the subscription evidence; investee certification and sufficient state tax do not replace this additional condition. A transfer from an existing savings account into new shares does not itself increase total checked wealth. For an otherwise eligible 20,000 subscription, checked opening and closing wealth both 100,000 gives no increase and no credit, rather than 10,000. If checked closing wealth is 120,000 against opening 100,000 after the required value-change exclusions, the 20,000 increase satisfies this gate; with all other conditions and enough state quota the 50% credit is 10,000. A 20,000 paper gain in retained assets does not supply the required increase. These stated whole-wealth cases do not prescribe an unverified partial-increase allocation ([AEAT wealth-check instructions, published 2025 manual under unchanged article 70](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c16-deducciones-generales-cuota/deduccion-inversion-empresas-nueva-reciente-creacion/limite-deduccion-comprobacion-situacion-patrimonial.html)).

Apply [LIRPF art. 68.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68) to a **subscription at incorporation or qualifying capital increase**, not a secondary purchase of existing shares. Compute `eligible_cost ×50%` after the annual 100,000 base limit and exclusions, then cap use at the available state quota under art. 67. Obtain the investee's certification; no automatic carry-forward of unused ordinary credit is granted.

The investee must be an eligible SA/SL/labour-company form, unlisted on both regulated and multilateral markets throughout holding, carrying on a genuine activity with means rather than managing a property/financial portfolio. Opening own funds for the acquisition period must not exceed **400,000**, assessed at art. 42 commercial-group level where required. Subscribe at incorporation or within its following **five years**, **seven** for a qualifying Ley 28/2022 emerging company. Hold for **more than three and less than twelve years**. Combined direct/indirect ownership/votes with spouse and relatives through the second degree cannot exceed **40% on any day** of the holding calendar years; the restriction has the statutory emerging-company founding-shareholder exception. The investee cannot be a continuation of the taxpayer's same earlier activity under another ownership.

Do not use the same subscribed amount for both this national credit and a regional subscription credit. Split actual eligible amounts and document the elected route; a single invoice does not prove two distinct bases. For a disposal reinvestment election under art. 38.2, only qualifying new subscription **above the whole amount obtained on the old disposal** enters the new credit base. Compare the full statutory amount, not just the exempt gain. Record holding conditions and recapture under the actual law rather than treating the initial certificate as permanent qualification.

### Donations and membership contributions

Under [Ley 49/2002 arts. 16–19 and 24](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2002-25039#a19), identify the qualifying beneficiary and gift type and obtain its certificate. Cash, qualifying assets, irrevocable membership contributions without service consideration and the specified usage rights have different base valuations under art. 18. A purchase or membership fee buying a service is not automatically a gift. The statute permits only its defined symbolic consideration, no more than **15%** of gift value and in any event **25,000**. Revocation restores improperly claimed deductions with the applicable interest.

Build the combined **art. 68.3** eligible-base ledger and apply its **10% of BL** limit under [LIRPF art. 69](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a69). Within the qualifying Ley 49/2002 base, apply **80% to the first 250 across all recipients together**, not 250 per charity, and **40%** above it. Use **45%** on the qualifying recipient's excess above the statutory initial tranche only when gifts to that same recipient in the current and previous year are each at least their preceding year's amount, with gifts in **both** immediately preceding periods. For 2026 test the actual **2024, 2025 and 2026** history. A change of recipient does not satisfy the same-entity condition. Preserve base allocation where both ordinary and repeated recipients exist; do not duplicate the initial 250 tranche.

Separately compute art. 68.3.b qualifying non-Ley-49 foundation/public-interest association gifts at **10%**, and political-party qualifying contributions at **20%** of at most **600**; these are not alternative labels for the Ley 49 scale. Split the resulting shared national credit **50% state/50% regional** under [arts. 67.1.b and 77.1.a](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a67), respecting each non-negative liquid-quota limit. Do not transfer unused state credit to the regional half or carry it for ten years because corporate donors can. Qualifying priority patronage and regional gift relief require their own year/entity/interaction tests.

### Legacy national habitual-home investment and rental relief

First establish the [DT 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava) gateway: qualifying home acquired or construction amounts paid **before 1 January 2013**; or qualifying rehabilitation/extension/disability-adaptation expenditure before that date with completion **before 1 January 2017**. The normal prior-claim condition requires the relevant deduction in a pre-2013 tax period, subject to the preserved art. 68.1.2 previous-home/reinvestment-base exception. [TEAC 22 April 2024, RG 00/00765/2023](https://serviciostelematicosext.hacienda.gob.es/TEAC/DYCTEA/criterio.aspx?id=00%2F00765%2F2023%2F00%2F0%2F1) also admits otherwise eligible buyers who did not file because their income created no filing duty, or who were obliged and filed but had **no gross quota in any relevant pre-2013 period** to apply the credit. A claimant who was obliged and filed but omitted the credit despite available gross quota remains excluded. If the absence of quota arose from an error, apply the ruling's case-specific analysis; do not treat an erroneous return as automatic entitlement. Ownership before 2013 does not satisfy every condition.

For construction, preserve the historical **four-year completion period from the start of investment**, plus only an actually available statutory extension and the **14 March–30 May 2020** suspension where applicable. AEAT explains that the construction-payment modality disappeared from **2022** after the maximum historical completion period, while acquisition relief on a completed qualifying home continues for eligible later payments. Do not allow a 2026 construction bill simply because a first payment preceded 2013 ([AEAT entitlement and construction transition](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c16-deducciones-generales-cuota/deduccion-inversion-vivienda-habitual-regimen-transitorio/modalidades-deduccion/adquisicion-construc-rehabilitacion-ampliacion-vivienda-habitual/quienes-tienen-derecho-deduccion.html)).

Apply the preserved **pre-repeal** [LIRPF arts. 68.1/70/78](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68) and relevant historical regulations. For ordinary acquisition/construction/rehabilitation/extension, add eligible amounts actually paid, including attributable purchase costs/taxes and financing principal, interest net of hedge receipts and eligible financing expenses. Exclude unrelated borrowing, private expenditure and amounts not borne by the claimant. The aggregate annual base is at most **9,040**, including on a joint return; excess annual expenditure is not carried. Apply **7.5% state** and the community's actual preserved regional rate, ordinarily **7.5%**, separately. The Cataluña legacy **9%** and disability-adaptation regional rules already stated in its catalogue require their own conditions.

Preserve the ordinary **three-year occupation**, occupation within **twelve months** of acquisition/completion and actual necessity exceptions in the incorporated law; obtain acquisition, completion and occupation evidence. Prior home bases already deducted and exempt reinvestment gains can postpone the new home's eligible base until the statutory accumulated-investment threshold is exceeded. Art. 70's increase-in-net-wealth test must hold, using its exclusion of interest/financing expenses and mere asset revaluation. Disability adaptation instead has its own certified necessity, eligible persons/use rights and **12,080** separate base, with **10% state** plus actual regional percentage. A new post-2012 adaptation does not acquire relief just because the house is old.

A substantiated refinance does not by itself destroy old-home eligibility: trace the replacement borrowing and payments to the original eligible debt, apportion any extra borrowing used elsewhere, and avoid counting a refinancing movement itself as fresh expenditure. The [AEAT current home-basis guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c16-deducciones-generales-cuota/deduccion-inversion-vivienda-habitual-regimen-transitorio/modalidades-deduccion/adquisicion-construc-rehabilitacion-ampliacion-vivienda-habitual/cantidades-satisfechas-derecho-deduccion-base-deducible.html) explains continuity and qualifying cancellation costs. Divorced/co-owner acquisitions and judicial allocation require their actual entitlement history; do not assume an unchanged 50% claim or unrestricted 100% claim.

For a grandfathered **DT 15 rental** claim, require the pre-2015 lease, rent paid before 2015 and prior entitlement. The preserved [2014 art. 68.7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20121031&tn=1#a68) formula is **10.05%** of eligible rent up to base **9,040** when BI≤**17,707.20**; above that and below **24,107.20**, maximum base is `9,040 −1.4125 ×(BI−17,707.20)`. At/above 24,107.20 there is no credit. The historical rate/formula remained the same in the incorporated 2014 law; do not replace it with 2026's new 10%/11,630 formula. Allocate it **50% state/50% regional** under DT 15's preserved arts. 67/77, unlike the new 2026 state-only rental credit. The current DT 15.3 expressly excludes it for the same home/year when art. 68.6 is applied. Retain the actual qualifying continuation/novation facts and choose the permitted route rather than stacking both.

### Maternity and childcare: refundable differential credits

Under [LIRPF art. 81 and RIRPF art. 60](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a81), a woman must have the descendant-minimum entitlement for the child and, **at birth**, qualifying contributory/assistance unemployment benefit or Social Security/qualifying mutual registration, or subsequently achieve registration with **at least 30 contribution days**. Where the mother dies or exclusive custody passes to the father/tutor, apply the article's remaining-entitlement conditions. Adoption/permanent/pre-adoptive fostering has the statutory **three-year period** from civil registration or the relevant resolution regardless of the child's age; a later adoption does not restart a prior fostering period's clock.

Keep a monthly ledger. Ordinarily use **100 × eligible months per child**, counting the birth/qualifying month and excluding the month a natural child turns three, using the last-day child-status test. Eligibility established at birth is not lost merely because the mother later stops working. Exclude months when either parent receives the same child's IMV childhood-aid supplement, subject to any actually applicable pre-2023 transitional entitlement. For registration after birth, the month the 30-day period is met adds a **single 150**, making that month's entitlement **250** and that year's cap **1,350**, rather than inventing 150 each following month. The current maternity credit has **no old Social Security contribution-amount ceiling**. See [AEAT monthly calculation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c18-cuota-liquida-resultante-autoliquidacion/resultado-declaracion/deduccion-maternidad-incremento-adicional-gastos-custodia/deduccion-maternidad/cuantia-limite-deduccion.html).

For the childcare increment, establish eligible custody in an authorised nursery/infant centre and paid registration/enrolment, ordinary/extended attendance or meals. The **number of months** uses qualifying **complete-month** expenses, including fully contracted months with non-teaching days. Compute `1,000 × eligible_complete_months/12`, capped at **annual effective unsubsidised eligible cost** paid by either parent, including relevant annual costs whether or not every cost itself covered a complete month. Exclude tax-exempt employer childcare/education benefits under art. 42.3.b/d and reimbursed/subsidised expenditure. Apply the prescribed equal division where several entitled fostering/tutoring claimants exist. Actual authorisation is the legally required authorisation to operate a nursery; an educational-administration authorisation is not universally required for every nursery ([RIRPF arts. 60 and 69.9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a60), [AEAT authorised-centre guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/eu_es/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c18-cuota-liquida-resultante-autoliquidacion/resultado-declaracion/deduccion-maternidad-incremento-adicional-gastos-custodia/incremento-adicional-gastos-custodia-guarderias/requisitos-condiciones-aplicar-incremento-adicional.html)).

In the year of the third birthday the childcare route can continue until the month **before the child can start the second infant-education cycle**, although the ordinary 100/month maternity amount has stopped. Do not shorten the childcare route automatically to the birthday or grant it after the relevant school-cycle start. The increment is claimed in the annual return; it is **not paid in advance**. Reconcile maternity advance amounts actually received: final differential equals the prior differential **minus full allowed maternity and childcare plus maternity advances already received**. A zero regional liability does not deny an otherwise valid refundable credit.

### Large-family and disability credits: monthly entitlement, caps and sharing

[LIRPF art. 81 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a81bis) requires actual qualifying work with Social Security/alternative-mutual registration, or the listed unemployment/pension/analogous alternative-mutual benefit route. A dependant's certificate alone is insufficient. The **24 September 2026 TEAC unification ruling 00/05409/2025** confirms the work/registration limb for its specified non-professional family caregiver registered under the dependency-care special agreement; do not reject that route merely because the care is unpaid ([official ruling](https://serviciostelematicosext.hacienda.gob.es/teac/dyctea/criterio.aspx?id=00%2F05409%2F2025%2F00%2F0%2F1)).

- For each disabled descendant/ascendant, establish the relevant art. 58/59 minimum and its income/cohabitation/return restrictions, then calculate **100 per eligible month**.
- For a qualifying large-family claimant, use **100 per eligible month**, adding another **100** for special category and **50 per month for each child exceeding the statutory category minimum**. Use the actual family certificate/category and statutory minimum, not a universal assumption that every fourth/fifth child is excess. The alternative separated/unmarried parent route requires **two children, no right to receive maintenance and entitlement to the whole descendant minimum**; it is not automatically every single-parent family.
- For a disabled spouse, require a marriage not legally separated, spouse non-exempt income no more than **8,000**, and no competing art. 81 bis descendant/ascendant disability entitlement. Use **100 per eligible month**.

Under [RIRPF art. 60 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a60bis), family category, excess-child count, civil status and disability use the **last day of each month**. Work registration or the qualifying benefit can occur on **any day** of the month for final entitlement; advance-payment eligibility has additional duration rules and must not be substituted for final annual entitlement.

For the work route, cap each base deduction by the applicable **gross accrued Social Security/alternative-mutual contributions**, ignoring bonuses, after entitlement starts and within its eligible period. Apply the cap **independently per qualifying disabled relative**. The special-family 100% addition and excess-child additions are **outside this contribution ceiling**. The listed benefit recipients are outside that ceiling. Preserve actual mixed work/benefit periods and contribution records rather than deeming an unspecified benefit eligible.

With multiple claimants for the same relative/family, divide entitlement equally unless a valid cession applies. Under a cession, the receiving entitled claimant takes the whole amount; use months when **any** entitled claimant meets the conditions and combined relevant contributions, without counting overlapping months twice, subject to the benefit-recipient exception. All advance amounts are treated as received by that claimant. Do not multiply the family credit simply because both spouses work. Family and disability credits can coexist when their independent facts hold; no general regional-gross-tax ceiling applies. Reconcile the resulting refundable differential credits and **add back advance payments already received** in the final settlement.

### Business reinvestment and historic-heritage expenditure

For [art. 68.2.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68), first satisfy the LIS art. 101 reduced-dimension conditions. Establish the part of **positive general BL attributable to net activity income** and the amount actually invested in qualifying **new tangible fixed assets or investment property used in the activity**. The same income amount cannot fund more than one asset. Investment must occur in the income year or following year and is taken when the asset is made available; a qualifying finance lease requires the purchase option ultimately exercised. Apply **5%**, or **2.5%** where the specified new-activity reduction or Ceuta/Melilla income credit applies. Claim in the investment year, subject to the income year's combined gross-tax ceiling and the applicable art. 69.2 business-credit limits. Maintain use for **five years or shorter useful life**, with the statute's permitted reinvestment replacement. It is incompatible with free depreciation and the specified Canary incentives. Imported corporate R&D and other activity credits use their own LIS percentages/limits, but art. 68.2.a **excludes LIS art. 39.2–3 options**; do not give an individual the corporate optional discounted cash-refund route.

The [art. 68.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68) heritage credit is **15%** of substantiated qualifying investment/conservation/restoration expenditure, within its separate **10%-of-BL base** ceiling under art. 69.1. For imported heritage goods, obtain the valuation/recognition within **one year** and maintain the required Spanish presence/ownership **four years**. For protected buildings/areas use the actual declared protection and eligible work, not a general age-of-building assumption. Allocate this shared national credit between the tax halves as arts. 67/77 require.

### Home energy works and electric vehicles: dated national credits

Under [LIRPF DA 50](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-4), choose the actual improvement route and certificate, not a rate inferred from the contractor's invoice. The **20%/40%** dwelling routes cover the habitual home or another owned home let/intended for housing rental, with intended letting completed **before 31 December 2027**. Works/payments end **31 December 2026**, and the post-work certificate must be issued **before 1 January 2027**. The 20% route needs at least **7% heating/cooling demand reduction**; 40% needs at least **30% non-renewable primary-energy reduction or class A/B**. Claim in the post-work certificate year, including qualifying prior payments in the statutory window, subject respectively to **5,000/7,500 annual bases**. There is no general carry-forward of the excess base in these two routes.

The **60% whole-building route** instead requires ownership of a dwelling in a predominantly residential building and the specified building-level **30% reduction or class A/B**. Works/payments can run through **2027**; the certificate must be issued **before 1 January 2028**. Garage/storage acquired with the home can qualify under this route; business-affected portions do not. Claim eligible paid amounts once the certificate exists, with **5,000 annual base**, **15,000 aggregate base**, and excess qualifying base carried within the **four following tax years**. Do not carry a lost credit caused merely by insufficient quota as though it were excess expenditure base.

For all routes, deduct public aid awarded or definitively to be awarded. Pay by the specified bank/card/transfer/nominative-cheque method, not cash. Include necessary technical/project/work/material/certificate costs but exclude fossil-fuel equipment installation/replacement. Both certificates must satisfy the registration rules; the pre-work certificate can be no more than **two years** before work starts. Allocate community-of-owners payments by the actual participation coefficient. The same work cannot use both 20% and 40%, nor a dwelling route where the work belongs under the building route. Deduct at the prescribed **state-quota-only position after art. 68 credits**, without manufacturing negative state liquid tax.

[LIRPF DA 58](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-11) gives a separate **15% new-EV** credit, base at most **20,000** including eligible acquisition costs/taxes less public aid. Choose **one** route for the single qualifying purchase: acquisition by the statutory end-2026 window with **first Spanish registration before 31 December 2026** as DA 58.2.d expressly requires, or a qualifying seller advance of at least **25%** by end-2026 followed by full payment/acquisition/first registration before the end of the **second following tax period**. Under the advance route claim in the payment year; under the ordinary route claim in the registration year. Verify category **M1/L6e/L7e/L3e/L4e/L5e**, model eligibility and applicable price ceiling under the actual national mobility-aid rules at the relevant date. Do not certify a particular car's price/model eligibility from the 15% headline alone. Business-affected vehicles are excluded, and later business allocation loses the credit.

The **15% charging-point** credit covers eligible paid installation/equipment/work in property owned by the taxpayer, excluding business use and public aid, annual base at most **4,000**. Use the prescribed non-cash payment evidence and required permits. Claim in the completion year, no later than **2026**, including qualifying earlier-window payments where the statute permits.
