Later business allocation triggers loss. Both DA 58 credits use their prescribed **state-quota-only position after art. 68 credits**. Do not duplicate an advance and registration claim, a public subsidy and unreduced expenditure base, or a regional credit whose own law prohibits the combination.

The new **2026 renewable self-consumption** credit under [DA 62](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-16) applies **10%** to eligible non-business installations, including qualifying storage, in owned property; the specified dwelling-in-predominantly-residential-building route gives **20%**. Both payment and installation/completion must be in **2026**. Base is actual eligible equipment/material/installation/work costs less granted or definitively forthcoming public aid, with an annual **5,000** ceiling. Use the statutory non-cash payment methods and actual authorisations, particularly the required electrical-installation certificate. A single installation cannot use both rates or this credit together with DA 50 for the same property/installation. Later business allocation loses entitlement. Apply at its **state-only position after art. 68 credits**. Do not treat a regional solar credit as proof the national test is met, or assume compatibility where the relevant regional rule prohibits it.

## Deadlines and filing

| Obligation | Requirement | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Annual return | Claim the relevant national and regional schedules on Modelo 100 for 2026 income in the 2027 campaign; use the approved form, deadline and payment instructions for that campaign | 2026 income | [LIRPF arts. 96.5–96.6 and 97](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a96) |
| Correction of an existing return | Apply the approved autoliquidación rectificativa procedure and its statutory exceptions | current procedure | [RIRPF art. 67 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a6-2) |
| Later loss of a regional credit | Add the wrongly deducted regional amount and applicable interest to the regional liquid tax for the breach year under the applicable recapture provision | breach year | [RIRPF art. 59.1–59.2.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a59) |

Use [Modelo 140](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/procedimientoini/GZ25.shtml) for qualifying maternity advances and [Modelo 143](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ciudadanos-familias-personas-discapacidad/deducciones-relacionadas-hijos-descendientes/deduccion-familia-numerosa/modelo-143-abono-anticipado-deduccion-numerosa.html) for qualifying family/disability advances. The official family-credit route also identifies Modelo 121 for cession by a non-filer and Modelo 122 for a non-filer's required regularisation. The nursery's Modelo 233 is its information report, not an extra credit paid to the parent. Reconcile actual receipts and changes, then claim final annual entitlement without double payment.

Income below an ordinary filing threshold does not establish that no return is needed to obtain an eligible credit/refund; test the actual filing rules and claim route. No approximate AEAT box ranges or unconfirmed 2027 campaign dates are supplied here. Preserve invoices, payment evidence, certificates, grant records and carry-forward/holding-period schedules, rather than relying on a pre-filled draft as proof of entitlement.

## Penalties and interest

| Event | Consequence | Tax year | Official source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Voluntary late tax-due filing without prior request, within 12 months | 1% plus 1% per complete month late, excluding the corresponding penalty and pre-filing interest under the article | 2026 | [LGT art. 27.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a27) |
| Such filing after 12 months | 15%, plus interest from after the initial 12-month period | 2026 | [LGT art. 27.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a27) |
| Prompt-payment conditions for surcharge relief satisfied | 25% reduction of the surcharge | 2026 | [LGT art. 27.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a27) |
| Underpayment offence | 50% minor; 50–100% serious; 100–150% very serious, subject to classification and applicable reductions | 2026 | [LGT arts. 188 and 191](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a191) |
| Default interest | Use the rate for the relevant delay segment; AEAT records 4.0625% under the continued budget for 2026 until replacement | 2026 | [LGT art. 26](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a26); [AEAT interest rule](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c18-cuota-liquida-resultante-autoliquidacion/incremento-cuotas-liquidas-estatal-autonomica/calculo-intereses-demora/reglas-calculo.html) |

A properly reported later failure of a holding condition and an original false deduction are different events. Determine the article's recapture mechanism, actual loss, prior request and conduct before classifying a penalty. A statutory reduction is conditional, not an automatic discount.

## Cross-border points

Resolve Spanish/treaty residence and the common-territory/foral connection first. The Madrid inbound-investor credit requires becoming an ordinary Madrid **IRPF taxpayer** and satisfying the residence/holding conditions above; it is not a general tax credit for every foreign investor. Foreign company securities require the actual listing, jurisdiction, ownership and role tests. A later move out of Madrid can cause recapture even while the taxpayer remains resident elsewhere in Spain ([Madrid art. 17 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOCM-m-2010-90068#a1-5)).

Ceuta/Melilla's art. 68.4 relief depends on qualifying income and the taxpayer's residence/asset conditions. A 60% relief rate is not permission to multiply every Spanish tax liability by 40%, nor is it a Social Security credit. Use [foral IRPF](es-foral-income-tax.md) for its supplied territorial methods and [resident IRPF](es-income-tax.md) for the supplied ordinary Ceuta/Melilla relief method. Retain the specific unfinished territorial and credit branches identified in those Guides and the pack README ([LIRPF art. 68.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68)).

## Two worked examples that recompute

### Canarias homeowner: overlapping property credits, tax caps and exempt aid

Hypothetical 2026 facts: an individual Canary resident aged **39**, with general plus savings BI **30,000**, pays **9,000** of qualifying first habitual-home acquisition costs and **10,000** of independently qualifying energy-rehabilitation costs on that same home. All ownership, occupation, historical incorporated conditions and energy certificates are satisfied, with no previous-home deduction/reinvestment adjustment or property grants. Separately, eligible medical invoices total **5,000**, of which an IRPF-exempt public grant covers **1,000**. The hypothetical upstream regional calculation supplies gross tax **1,200**, with no other credits at this stage. BI and regional tax are stated calculation inputs; the example does not replace the underlying state/regional tax calculation.

The under-40 middle income band gives `4% × min(9,000, 6,000) = 240` for acquisition, limited by art. 14 quater to `15% × 1,200 = 180`. Energy relief considered alone would be `12% × min(10,000, 7,000) = 840`, limited to `10% × 1,200 = 120`. **Choose the acquisition credit of 180**: art. 18 bars stacking both credits for the same home even for different payments. Medical relief is `12% × (5,000 − 1,000) = 480`, below the ordinary individual 500 cap. Total regional credits are **660**, leaving regional liquid tax **540** before later state/national stages and prepayments ([arts. 14, 14 bis, 14 quater, 16 ter and 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOC-j-2009-90008#a18)).

For a separate final-settlement stage in the same example, assume the claimant is a mother entitled at her child's 2025 birth and for all twelve months of 2026, with no excluded IMV months. Nine full qualifying nursery months give nominal childcare 750, capped by 650 of actual annual unsubsidised qualifying costs. Maternity is 1,200; actual maternity advances were 600. An upstream combined annual differential of 400, already including the regional 540 and all other tax/prepayments, becomes `400 −1,200 −650 +600 = −850`: **850 refundable**. Do not cap these differential credits at the regional 540 or deduct the 600 advance a second time.

```oa-check
name: Canarias property-credit choice, quota limits and exempt medical grant
inputs:
  eligible_acquisition: 9000
  eligible_energy_works: 10000
  medical_invoices: 5000
  exempt_medical_grant: 1000
  regional_gross_tax: 1200
  maternity_months: 12
  childcare_complete_months: 9
  childcare_net_annual_cost: 650
  maternity_advances: 600
  differential_before_family: 400
steps:
  acquisition_nominal: min(eligible_acquisition, 6000) * 0.04
  acquisition_after_tax_cap: min(acquisition_nominal, regional_gross_tax * 0.15)
  energy_nominal: min(eligible_energy_works, 7000) * 0.12
  energy_after_tax_cap: min(energy_nominal, regional_gross_tax * 0.10, regional_gross_tax * 0.15)
  chosen_property_credit: max(acquisition_after_tax_cap, energy_after_tax_cap)
  medical_base: max(0, medical_invoices - exempt_medical_grant)
  medical_credit: min(medical_base * 0.12, 500)
  total_credit: min(chosen_property_credit + medical_credit, regional_gross_tax)
  regional_liquid: regional_gross_tax - total_credit
  maternity: maternity_months * 100
  childcare: min(1000 * childcare_complete_months / 12, childcare_net_annual_cost)
  final_differential: differential_before_family - maternity - childcare + maternity_advances
expected:
  acquisition_nominal: 240.00
  acquisition_after_tax_cap: 180.00
  energy_nominal: 840.00
  energy_after_tax_cap: 120.00
  chosen_property_credit: 180.00
  medical_base: 4000.00
  medical_credit: 480.00
  total_credit: 660.00
  regional_liquid: 540.00
  maternity: 1200
  childcare: 650
  final_differential: -850
```

### Asturias protected home: carry ceiling and expiry

Hypothetical facts: in 2026 a sole eligible claimant acquires a qualifying protected habitual home and pays **9,000** of eligible acquisition costs. The verified upstream regional calculation leaves **2,000** after all other current credits. [Asturias art. 6](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2015-945#a6) generates **5,000**: use **2,000**, carrying **3,000**. Assume no new qualifying expenditure in 2027–2029 and available regional tax after current credits of **700**, **2,000** and **2,000** respectively. The subsequent-year ceiling is **1,000**, as confirmed by [AEAT's protected-home guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025-deducciones-autonomicas/comunidad-autonoma-principado-asturias/inversion-vivienda-habitual-que-tenga-protegida.html). Use **700**, **1,000** and **1,000**; **300** expires after the third following period, 2029. It is not refunded or carried into 2030. These tax amounts are hypothetical upstream inputs, not substitute IRPF scales. No co-claimant cap division or new-expenditure/carry interaction arises on these facts.

```oa-check
name: Asturias protected home, subsequent-year ceiling and three-period expiry
inputs:
  eligible_acquisition: 9000
  available_tax_2026: 2000
  available_tax_2027: 700
  available_tax_2028: 2000
  available_tax_2029: 2000
steps:
  generated: min(eligible_acquisition, 5000)
  used_2026: min(generated, available_tax_2026)
  carried_2026: generated - used_2026
  used_2027: min(carried_2026, 1000, available_tax_2027)
  carried_2027: carried_2026 - used_2027
  used_2028: min(carried_2027, 1000, available_tax_2028)
  carried_2028: carried_2027 - used_2028
  used_2029: min(carried_2028, 1000, available_tax_2029)
  expired_after_2029: carried_2028 - used_2029
  total_used: used_2026 + used_2027 + used_2028 + used_2029
expected:
  generated: 5000.00
  used_2026: 2000.00
  carried_2026: 3000.00
  used_2027: 700.00
  carried_2027: 2300.00
  used_2028: 1000.00
  carried_2028: 1300.00
  used_2029: 1000.00
  expired_after_2029: 300.00
  total_used: 4700.00
```

## Ask the client first

1. Where did you live during the year, for how many days, and do common-territory, foral, treaty or special incoming-worker rules apply?
2. What are each relevant person's general/savings BI, BL, income sources and individual/joint filing facts, including the potential family unit and other entitled parents?
3. Which births/adoptions, fostering, family recognitions, dependency/disability, education or care events occurred, on what dates, and who lived with whom?
4. Which housing, loan, donation, investment or care amounts did you actually pay, who owns/pays each share, and what certificates, grants, deposit and payment evidence exist?
5. What credits and advances have already been claimed, what tax remains available in each half, and are holding, employment, residence or recapture conditions still running?
